# 公安机关办理刑事案件程序规定

**分类**：司法解释  
**法律部门**：刑法  
**公布日期**：2012-12-13  
**生效日期**：2013-01-01

> （2012年12月13日公安部令第127号修订发布 根据2020年7月20日公安部令第159号《公安部关于修改〈公安机关办理刑事案件程序规定〉的决定》修正） 第一章　任务和基本原则 第一条　为了保障《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》的贯彻实施，保证公安机关在刑事诉讼中正确履行职权，规范办案程序，确保办案质量，提高办案效率，制定本规定。 第二条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中的任务，是保证准确、及时地查明犯罪事实，正确应用法律，惩罚犯罪分子，保障无罪的人不受刑事追究，教育公民自觉遵守法律，积极同犯罪行为作斗争，维护社会主义法制，尊重和保障人权，保护公民的人身权利、财产权利、民主权利和其他权利，保障社会主义建设事业的顺利进行。 第三条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中的基本职权，是依照法律对刑事案件立案、侦查、预审；决定、执行强制措施；对依法不追究刑事责任的不予立案，已经追究的撤销案件；对侦查终结应当起诉的案件，移送人民检察院审查决定；对不够刑事处罚的犯罪嫌疑人需要行政处理的，依法予以处理或者移送有关部门；对被判处有期徒刑的罪犯，在被交付执行刑罚前，剩余刑期在三个月以下的，代为执行刑罚；执行拘役、剥夺政治权利、驱逐出境。 第四条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，必须依靠群众，以事实为根据，以法律为准绳。对于一切公民，在适用法律上一律平等，在法律面前，不允许有任何特权。 第五条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，同人民法院、人民检察院分工负责，互相配合，互相制约，以保证准确有效地执行法律。 第六条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，依法接受人民检察院的法律监督。 第七条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，应当建立、完善和严格执行办案责任制度、执法过错责任追究制度等内部执法监督制度。 在刑事诉讼中，上级公安机关发现下级公安机关作出的决定或者办理的案件有错误的，有权予以撤销或者变更，也可以指令下级公安机关予以纠正。 下级公安机关对上级公安机关的决定必须执行，如果认为有错误，可以在执行的同时向上级公安机关报告。 第八条　公安机关办理刑事案件，应当重证据，重调查研究，不轻信口供。严禁刑讯逼供和以威胁、引诱、欺骗以及其他非法方法收集证据，不得强迫任何人证实自己有罪。 第九条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中，应当保障犯罪嫌疑人、被告人和其他诉讼参与人依法享有的辩护权和其他诉讼权利。 第十条　公安机关办理刑事案件，应当向同级人民检察院提请批准逮捕、移送审查起诉。 第十一条　公安机关办理刑事案件，对不通晓当地通用的语言文字的诉讼参与人，应当为他们翻译。

---

### 第一章　任务和基本原则

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了保障《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》的贯彻实施，保证公安机关在刑事诉讼中正确履行职权，规范办案程序，确保办案质量，提高办案效率，制定本规定。

**Article 1** These Provisions are formulated to ensure the implementation of the Criminal Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, to guarantee that public security organs correctly perform their functions and powers in criminal actions, to standardize case-handling procedures, to ensure case-handling quality, and to improve case-handling efficiency.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中的任务，是保证准确、及时地查明犯罪事实，正确应用法律，惩罚犯罪分子，保障无罪的人不受刑事追究，教育公民自觉遵守法律，积极同犯罪行为作斗争，维护社会主义法制，尊重和保障人权，保护公民的人身权利、财产权利、民主权利和其他权利，保障社会主义建设事业的顺利进行。

**Article 2** The tasks of public security organs in criminal actions are to ensure the accurate and timely ascertainment of criminal facts, the correct application of law, the punishment of criminals, the protection of innocent persons from criminal investigation, the education of citizens to voluntarily abide by law and actively fight against criminal acts, the safeguarding of the socialist legal system, the respect and protection of human rights, the protection of citizens' personal rights, property rights, democratic rights and other rights, and the smooth progress of socialist construction.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中的基本职权，是依照法律对刑事案件立案、侦查、预审；决定、执行强制措施；对依法不追究刑事责任的不予立案，已经追究的撤销案件；对侦查终结应当起诉的案件，移送人民检察院审查决定；对不够刑事处罚的犯罪嫌疑人需要行政处理的，依法予以处理或者移送有关部门；对被判处有期徒刑的罪犯，在被交付执行刑罚前，剩余刑期在三个月以下的，代为执行刑罚；执行拘役、剥夺政治权利、驱逐出境。

**Article 3** The basic functions and powers of public security organs in criminal actions are to place criminal cases on file for investigation, to investigate and to conduct preliminary hearings according to law; to decide on and execute compulsory measures; to decide not to place a case on file where criminal liability shall not be investigated according to law, or to dismiss a case that has already been placed on file; to transfer to the People's Procuratorate for examination and decision cases that, upon conclusion of investigation, should be prosecuted; to handle or transfer to relevant departments according to law criminal suspects who are not subject to criminal punishment but need administrative sanctions; to execute on behalf the punishment of criminals sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment whose remaining term is less than three months before being delivered for execution of punishment; and to execute criminal detention, deprivation of political rights, and deportation.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，必须依靠群众，以事实为根据，以法律为准绳。对于一切公民，在适用法律上一律平等，在法律面前，不允许有任何特权。

**Article 4** In conducting criminal actions, public security organs must rely on the masses, take facts as the basis, and take law as the criterion. They shall apply the law equally to all citizens; no privilege whatsoever shall be allowed before the law.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，同人民法院、人民检察院分工负责，互相配合，互相制约，以保证准确有效地执行法律。

**Article 5** In conducting criminal actions, public security organs shall, together with the People's Courts and the People's Procuratorates, divide responsibilities, cooperate with each other, and check each other, so as to ensure the accurate and effective implementation of law.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，依法接受人民检察院的法律监督。

**Article 6** In conducting criminal actions, public security organs shall, according to law, accept legal supervision by the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　公安机关进行刑事诉讼，应当建立、完善和严格执行办案责任制度、执法过错责任追究制度等内部执法监督制度。  
在刑事诉讼中，上级公安机关发现下级公安机关作出的决定或者办理的案件有错误的，有权予以撤销或者变更，也可以指令下级公安机关予以纠正。  
下级公安机关对上级公安机关的决定必须执行，如果认为有错误，可以在执行的同时向上级公安机关报告。

**Article 7** In conducting criminal actions, public security organs shall establish, improve and strictly implement internal law enforcement supervision systems such as the case-handling responsibility system and the law enforcement fault accountability system.  
In criminal actions, if a public security organ at a higher level discovers that a decision made or a case handled by a public security organ at a lower level is erroneous, it shall have the power to revoke or modify it, or may instruct the lower-level public security organ to rectify it.  
A public security organ at a lower level must execute the decision of the public security organ at a higher level; if it considers the decision to be erroneous, it may, while executing it, report to the higher-level public security organ.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　公安机关办理刑事案件，应当重证据，重调查研究，不轻信口供。严禁刑讯逼供和以威胁、引诱、欺骗以及其他非法方法收集证据，不得强迫任何人证实自己有罪。

**Article 8** In handling criminal cases, public security organs shall attach importance to evidence and to investigation and research, and shall not readily believe confessions. It shall be strictly prohibited to collect evidence by extortion of confessions through torture or by threats, enticement, deception or other illegal methods, and no person shall be compelled to prove his own guilt.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　公安机关在刑事诉讼中，应当保障犯罪嫌疑人、被告人和其他诉讼参与人依法享有的辩护权和其他诉讼权利。

**Article 9** In criminal actions, public security organs shall safeguard the right to defense and other litigation rights that criminal suspects, defendants and other participants in proceedings enjoy according to law.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　公安机关办理刑事案件，应当向同级人民检察院提请批准逮捕、移送审查起诉。

**Article 10** In handling criminal cases, public security organs shall submit to the People's Procuratorate at the same level requests for approval of arrest and transfer cases for examination and prosecution.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　公安机关办理刑事案件，对不通晓当地通用的语言文字的诉讼参与人，应当为他们翻译。  
在少数民族聚居或者多民族杂居的地区，应当使用当地通用的语言进行讯问。对外公布的诉讼文书，应当使用当地通用的文字。

**Article 11** In handling criminal cases, if participants in proceedings are not familiar with the spoken or written language commonly used in the locality, public security organs shall provide interpretation for them.  
In areas where minority nationalities live in concentrated communities or where several nationalities live together, interrogations shall be conducted in the spoken language commonly used in the locality. Litigation documents that are made public shall be in the written language commonly used in the locality.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　公安机关办理刑事案件，各地区、各部门之间应当加强协作和配合，依法履行协查、协办职责。  
上级公安机关应当加强监督、协调和指导。

**Article 12** In handling criminal cases, all regions and all departments of public security organs shall strengthen collaboration and cooperation, and shall perform duties of assisting in investigation and handling according to law.  
The public security organ at a higher level shall strengthen supervision, coordination and guidance.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　根据《中华人民共和国引渡法》《中华人民共和国国际刑事司法协助法》，中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约和公安部签订的双边、多边合作协议，或者按照互惠原则，我国公安机关可以和外国警察机关开展刑事司法协助和警务合作。

**Article 13** In accordance with the Extradition Law of the People's Republic of China, the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law of the People's Republic of China, international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, bilateral or multilateral cooperation agreements signed by the Ministry of Public Security, or on the principle of reciprocity, the public security organs of China may carry out criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation with foreign police authorities.

### 第二章　管 辖

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　根据刑事诉讼法的规定，除下列情形外，刑事案件由公安机关管辖：  
（一）监察机关管辖的职务犯罪案件；  
（二）人民检察院管辖的在对诉讼活动实行法律监督中发现的司法工作人员利用职权实施的非法拘禁、刑讯逼供、非法搜查等侵犯公民权利、损害司法公正的犯罪，以及经省级以上人民检察院决定立案侦查的公安机关管辖的国家机关工作人员利用职权实施的重大犯罪案件；  
（三）人民法院管辖的自诉案件。对于人民法院直接受理的被害人有证据证明的轻微刑事案件，因证据不足驳回起诉，人民法院移送公安机关或者被害人向公安机关控告的，公安机关应当受理；被害人直接向公安机关控告的，公安机关应当受理；  
（四）军队保卫部门管辖的军人违反职责的犯罪和军队内部发生的刑事案件；  
（五）监狱管辖的罪犯在监狱内犯罪的刑事案件；  
（六）海警部门管辖的海（岛屿）岸线以外我国管辖海域内发生的刑事案件。对于发生在沿海港岙口、码头、滩涂、台轮停泊点等区域的，由公安机关管辖；  
（七）其他依照法律和规定应当由其他机关管辖的刑事案件。

**Article 14** According to the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, except for the following circumstances, criminal cases shall be under the jurisdiction of public security organs:  
(1) Duty-related criminal cases under the jurisdiction of supervisory authorities;  
(2) Crimes such as illegal detention, extortion of confessions through torture, and illegal search committed by judicial officers by taking advantage of their functions and powers, which are discovered in the course of legal supervision over litigation activities and are under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate, as well as major criminal cases committed by state functionaries of organs under the jurisdiction of public security organs by taking advantage of their functions and powers, which are decided to be placed on file for investigation by the People's Procuratorate at or above the provincial level;  
(3) Cases of private prosecution under the jurisdiction of the People's Court. For minor criminal cases for which the victim has evidence and which are directly accepted by the People's Court, if the People's Court dismisses the prosecution due to insufficient evidence and transfers the case to the public security organ, or if the victim files a complaint with the public security organ, the public security organ shall accept the case; if the victim directly files a complaint with the public security organ, the public security organ shall accept it;  
(4) Crimes of dereliction of duty by servicemen and criminal cases occurring within the military under the jurisdiction of the military security departments;  
(5) Criminal cases of crimes committed by prisoners within a prison under the jurisdiction of the prison;  
(6) Criminal cases occurring in the sea areas under China's jurisdiction beyond the sea (island) coastline under the jurisdiction of the coast guard departments. For cases occurring in areas such as coastal harbors and inlets, docks, tidal flats, and berthing points for Taiwan fishing boats, they shall be under the jurisdiction of public security organs;  
(7) Other criminal cases that shall be under the jurisdiction of other organs according to law and regulations.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　刑事案件由犯罪地的公安机关管辖。如果由犯罪嫌疑人居住地的公安机关管辖更为适宜的，可以由犯罪嫌疑人居住地的公安机关管辖。  
法律、司法解释或者其他规范性文件对有关犯罪案件的管辖作出特别规定的，从其规定。

**Article 15** Criminal cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the public security organ of the place where the crime was committed. If it is more appropriate for the case to be under the jurisdiction of the public security organ of the place where the criminal suspect resides, it may be under the jurisdiction of the public security organ of the place where the criminal suspect resides.  
Where laws, judicial interpretations or other normative documents provide otherwise for the jurisdiction of relevant criminal cases, those provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　犯罪地包括犯罪行为发生地和犯罪结果发生地。犯罪行为发生地，包括犯罪行为的实施地以及预备地、开始地、途经地、结束地等与犯罪行为有关的地点；犯罪行为有连续、持续或者继续状态的，犯罪行为连续、持续或者继续实施的地方都属于犯罪行为发生地。犯罪结果发生地，包括犯罪对象被侵害地、犯罪所得的实际取得地、藏匿地、转移地、使用地、销售地。  
居住地包括户籍所在地、经常居住地。经常居住地是指公民离开户籍所在地最后连续居住一年以上的地方，但住院就医的除外。单位登记的住所地为其居住地。主要营业地或者主要办事机构所在地与登记的住所地不一致的，主要营业地或者主要办事机构所在地为其居住地。

**Article 16** The place where a crime was committed includes the place where the criminal act occurred and the place where the criminal result occurred. The place where the criminal act occurred includes the place where the criminal act was carried out as well as the place of preparation, starting place, transit place, ending place and other places related to the criminal act; if the criminal act is in a continuous, sustained or ongoing state, the places where the criminal act is continuously, sustainedly or ongoingly carried out shall all be regarded as the place where the criminal act occurred. The place where the criminal result occurred includes the place where the criminal object was infringed upon, the place where the criminal proceeds were actually obtained, the place of concealment, the place of transfer, the place of use, and the place of sale.  
The place of residence includes the place of domicile registration and the place of habitual residence. The place of habitual residence refers to the place where a citizen has left the place of domicile registration and has continuously resided for one year or more, except for the case of being hospitalized for medical treatment. The registered domicile of a unit shall be its place of residence. If the principal place of business or the location of its main administrative body is inconsistent with the registered domicile, the principal place of business or the location of its main administrative body shall be its place of residence.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　针对或者主要利用计算机网络实施的犯罪，用于实施犯罪行为的网络服务使用的服务器所在地，网络服务提供者所在地，被侵害的网络信息系统及其管理者所在地，以及犯罪过程中犯罪嫌疑人、被害人使用的网络信息系统所在地，被害人被侵害时所在地和被害人财产遭受损失地公安机关可以管辖。

**Article 17** For crimes committed against or mainly using computer networks, the public security organ of the place where the server used for the network service for committing the criminal act is located, the place where the network service provider is located, the place where the infringed network information system and its administrator are located, and the place where the network information system used by the criminal suspect or the victim during the criminal process is located, the place where the victim was located when infringed, and the place where the victim's property suffered losses may have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　行驶中的交通工具上发生的刑事案件，由交通工具最初停靠地公安机关管辖；必要时，交通工具始发地、途经地、目的地公安机关也可以管辖。

**Article 18** For a criminal case occurring on a moving means of transportation, the public security organ of the place where the means of transportation first stops shall have jurisdiction; when necessary, the public security organ of the place of departure, the place of transit, or the place of destination of the means of transportation may also have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　在中华人民共和国领域外的中国航空器内发生的刑事案件，由该航空器在中国最初降落地的公安机关管辖。

**Article 19** For a criminal case occurring in a Chinese aircraft outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the public security organ of the place in China where the aircraft first lands shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　中国公民在中国驻外使、领馆内的犯罪，由其主管单位所在地或者原户籍地的公安机关管辖。  
中国公民在中华人民共和国领域外的犯罪，由其入境地、离境前居住地或者现居住地的公安机关管辖；被害人是中国公民的，也可由被害人离境前居住地或者现居住地的公安机关管辖。

**Article 20** For a crime committed by a Chinese citizen inside a Chinese embassy or consulate abroad, the public security organ of the place where the competent unit of that citizen is located or of the place of his original domicile registration shall have jurisdiction.  
For a crime committed by a Chinese citizen outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the public security organ of the place of his entry, of the place of his residence before departure, or of his current place of residence shall have jurisdiction; if the victim is a Chinese citizen, the public security organ of the place of the victim's residence before departure or of the victim's current place of residence may also have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　几个公安机关都有权管辖的刑事案件，由最初受理的公安机关管辖。必要时，可以由主要犯罪地的公安机关管辖。  
具有下列情形之一的，公安机关可以在职责范围内并案侦查：  
（一）一人犯数罪的；  
（二）共同犯罪的；  
（三）共同犯罪的犯罪嫌疑人还实施其他犯罪的；  
（四）多个犯罪嫌疑人实施的犯罪存在关联，并案处理有利于查明犯罪事实的。

**Article 21** Where several public security organs have jurisdiction over a criminal case, the public security organ that first accepted the case shall have jurisdiction. When necessary, the public security organ in the place of the principal crime may have jurisdiction.  
Under any of the following circumstances, the public security organ may, within its scope of duties, conduct combined investigation:  
(1) One person commits several crimes;  
(2) Joint crimes;  
(3) A criminal suspect of a joint crime also commits other crimes; or  
(4) The crimes committed by multiple criminal suspects are related, and combined handling is conducive to ascertaining the facts of the crimes.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　对管辖不明确或者有争议的刑事案件，可以由有关公安机关协商。协商不成的，由共同的上级公安机关指定管辖。  
对情况特殊的刑事案件，可以由共同的上级公安机关指定管辖。  
提请上级公安机关指定管辖时，应当在有关材料中列明犯罪嫌疑人基本情况、涉嫌罪名、案件基本事实、管辖争议情况、协商情况和指定管辖理由，经公安机关负责人批准后，层报有权指定管辖的上级公安机关。

**Article 22** For a criminal case with unclear or disputed jurisdiction, the relevant public security organs may consult. If consultation fails, the common superior public security organ shall designate jurisdiction.  
For a criminal case with special circumstances, the common superior public security organ may designate jurisdiction.  
When requesting the superior public security organ to designate jurisdiction, the basic information of the criminal suspect, the suspected crime, the basic facts of the case, the status of the jurisdictional dispute, the consultation status and the reasons for designating jurisdiction shall be stated in the relevant materials, and upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ, shall be reported level by level to the superior public security organ with the authority to designate jurisdiction.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　上级公安机关指定管辖的，应当将指定管辖决定书分别送达被指定管辖的公安机关和其他有关的公安机关，并根据办案需要抄送同级人民法院、人民检察院。  
原受理案件的公安机关，在收到上级公安机关指定其他公安机关管辖的决定书后，不再行使管辖权，同时应当将犯罪嫌疑人、涉案财物以及案卷材料等移送被指定管辖的公安机关。  
对指定管辖的案件，需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，由被指定管辖的公安机关提请同级人民检察院审查批准；需要提起公诉的，由该公安机关移送同级人民检察院审查决定。

**Article 23** Where a superior public security organ designates jurisdiction, it shall serve the decision on designation of jurisdiction on the designated public security organ and other relevant public security organs, and send a copy to the People's Court and the People's Procuratorate at the same level as needed for handling the case.  
After receiving the decision of the superior public security organ designating another public security organ to have jurisdiction, the public security organ that originally accepted the case shall no longer exercise jurisdiction, and shall at the same time transfer the criminal suspect, the property involved, and the case files, etc. to the designated public security organ.  
For a case with designated jurisdiction, if the arrest of a criminal suspect is necessary, the designated public security organ shall request the People's Procuratorate at the same level to examine and approve the arrest; if a public prosecution is required, the public security organ shall transfer the case to the People's Procuratorate at the same level for examination and decision.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　县级公安机关负责侦查发生在本辖区内的刑事案件。  
设区的市一级以上公安机关负责下列犯罪中重大案件的侦查：  
（一）危害国家安全犯罪；  
（二）恐怖活动犯罪；  
（三）涉外犯罪；  
（四）经济犯罪；  
（五）集团犯罪；  
（六）跨区域犯罪。  
上级公安机关认为有必要的，可以侦查下级公安机关管辖的刑事案件；下级公安机关认为案情重大需要上级公安机关侦查的刑事案件，可以请求上一级公安机关管辖。

**Article 24** The public security organ at the county level shall be responsible for investigating criminal cases occurring within its jurisdiction.  
The public security organ at or above the city level with districts shall be responsible for investigating major cases of the following crimes:  
(1) Crimes endangering national security;  
(2) Terrorist activity crimes;  
(3) Crimes involving foreign affairs;  
(4) Economic crimes;  
(5) Group crimes;  
(6) Cross-regional crimes.  
Where a superior public security organ deems it necessary, it may investigate a criminal case under the jurisdiction of a subordinate public security organ; where a subordinate public security organ considers that a case is major and requires investigation by a superior public security organ, it may request the public security organ at the next higher level to have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　公安机关内部对刑事案件的管辖，按照刑事侦查机构的设置及其职责分工确定。

**Article 25** The jurisdiction over criminal cases within the public security organs shall be determined in accordance with the establishment and division of functions and duties of criminal investigation agencies.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　铁路公安机关管辖铁路系统的机关、厂、段、院、校、所、队、工区等单位发生的刑事案件，车站工作区域内、列车内发生的刑事案件，铁路沿线发生的盗窃或者破坏铁路、通信、电力线路和其他重要设施的刑事案件，以及内部职工在铁路线上工作时发生的刑事案件。  
铁路系统的计算机信息系统延伸到地方涉及铁路业务的网点，其计算机信息系统发生的刑事案件由铁路公安机关管辖。  
对倒卖、伪造、变造火车票的刑事案件，由最初受理案件的铁路公安机关或者地方公安机关管辖。必要时，可以移送主要犯罪地的铁路公安机关或者地方公安机关管辖。  
在列车上发生的刑事案件，犯罪嫌疑人在列车运行途中被抓获的，由前方停靠站所在地的铁路公安机关管辖；必要时，也可以由列车始发站、终点站所在地的铁路公安机关管辖。犯罪嫌疑人不是在列车运行途中被抓获的，由负责该列车乘务的铁路公安机关管辖；但在列车运行途经的车站被抓获的，也可以由该车站所在地的铁路公安机关管辖。  
在国际列车上发生的刑事案件，根据我国与相关国家签订的协定确定管辖；没有协定的，由该列车始发或者前方停靠的中国车站所在地的铁路公安机关管辖。  
铁路建设施工工地发生的刑事案件由地方公安机关管辖。

**Article 26** The railway public security organs shall have jurisdiction over criminal cases occurring in units such as organs, factories, depots, hospitals, schools, offices, teams, work areas, etc. within the railway system, criminal cases occurring within station work areas and on trains, criminal cases of theft or damage to railways, communication, power lines and other important facilities along the railway line, and criminal cases occurring when internal employees are working on the railway line.  
For the computer information systems of the railway system that extend to local outlets involving railway business, the criminal cases occurring in such computer information systems shall be under the jurisdiction of the railway public security organs.  
With respect to criminal cases of scalping, forging or altering train tickets, the railway public security organ or the local public security organ that first accepted the case shall have jurisdiction. When necessary, it may be transferred to the railway public security organ or the local public security organ in the place of the principal crime for jurisdiction.  
For a criminal case occurring on a train, if the criminal suspect is arrested during the operation of the train, the railway public security organ in the place of the next stop ahead shall have jurisdiction; when necessary, the railway public security organ in the place of the departure station or the terminal station may also have jurisdiction. If the criminal suspect is not arrested during the operation of the train, the railway public security organ responsible for the crew service of that train shall have jurisdiction; however, if the suspect is arrested at a station along the train route, the railway public security organ in the place of that station may also have jurisdiction.  
For a criminal case occurring on an international train, jurisdiction shall be determined according to the agreements signed between China and the relevant countries; if there is no agreement, the railway public security organ in the place of the Chinese station where the train departs or makes the next stop ahead shall have jurisdiction.  
Criminal cases occurring on railway construction sites shall be under the jurisdiction of the local public security organs.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　民航公安机关管辖民航系统的机关、厂、段、院、校、所、队、工区等单位、机场工作区域内、民航飞机内发生的刑事案件。  
重大飞行事故刑事案件由犯罪结果发生地机场公安机关管辖。犯罪结果发生地未设机场公安机关或者不在机场公安机关管辖范围内的，由地方公安机关管辖，有关机场公安机关予以协助。

**Article 27** The civil aviation public security organs shall have jurisdiction over criminal cases occurring in units such as organs, factories, depots, hospitals, schools, offices, teams, work areas, etc. within the civil aviation system, within airport work areas, and on civil aviation aircraft.  
Criminal cases of major flight accidents shall be under the jurisdiction of the airport public security organ in the place where the criminal result occurred. If the place where the criminal result occurred does not have an airport public security organ or is not within the jurisdiction of the airport public security organ, the local public security organ shall have jurisdiction, and the relevant airport public security organ shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　海关走私犯罪侦查机构管辖中华人民共和国海关关境内发生的涉税走私犯罪和发生在海关监管区内的非涉税走私犯罪等刑事案件。

**Article 28** The anti-smuggling criminal investigation agency of the Customs shall have jurisdiction over criminal cases of duty-related smuggling crimes occurring within the customs territory of the People's Republic of China and non-duty-related smuggling crimes occurring within the customs supervision zones.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　公安机关侦查的刑事案件的犯罪嫌疑人涉及监察机关管辖的案件时，应当及时与同级监察机关协商，一般应当由监察机关为主调查，公安机关予以协助。

**Article 29** Where a criminal suspect in a criminal case investigated by a public security organ is also involved in a case under the jurisdiction of a supervisory organ, the public security organ shall promptly consult with the supervisory organ at the same level, and generally the supervisory organ shall take the lead in the investigation, with the public security organ providing assistance.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　公安机关侦查的刑事案件涉及人民检察院管辖的案件时，应当将属于人民检察院管辖的刑事案件移送人民检察院。涉嫌主罪属于公安机关管辖的，由公安机关为主侦查；涉嫌主罪属于人民检察院管辖的，公安机关予以配合。  
公安机关侦查的刑事案件涉及其他侦查机关管辖的案件时，参照前款规定办理。

**Article 30** Where a criminal case investigated by a public security organ involves a case under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate, the public security organ shall transfer the criminal case that belongs to the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate to the People's Procuratorate. If the suspected principal crime is under the jurisdiction of the public security organ, the public security organ shall take the lead in the investigation; if the suspected principal crime is under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate, the public security organ shall provide cooperation.  
Where a criminal case investigated by a public security organ involves a case under the jurisdiction of another investigation organ, the preceding paragraph shall be applied mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　公安机关和军队互涉刑事案件的管辖分工按照有关规定办理。  
公安机关和武装警察部队互涉刑事案件的管辖分工依照公安机关和军队互涉刑事案件的管辖分工的原则办理。

**Article 31** The division of jurisdiction over criminal cases involving both public security organs and the military shall be handled in accordance with relevant provisions.  
The division of jurisdiction over criminal cases involving both public security organs and the armed police forces shall be handled in accordance with the principle of the division of jurisdiction over criminal cases involving both public security organs and the military.

### 第三章　回 避

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　公安机关负责人、侦查人员有下列情形之一的，应当自行提出回避申请，没有自行提出回避申请的，应当责令其回避，当事人及其法定代理人也有权要求他们回避：  
（一）是本案的当事人或者是当事人的近亲属的；  
（二）本人或者他的近亲属和本案有利害关系的；  
（三）担任过本案的证人、鉴定人、辩护人、诉讼代理人的；  
（四）与本案当事人有其他关系，可能影响公正处理案件的。

**Article 32** The responsible person of a public security organ or an investigator shall, under any of the following circumstances, voluntarily file an application for withdrawal; if no such application is filed voluntarily, he or she shall be ordered to withdraw, and the party concerned and his or her statutory agent shall also have the right to request their withdrawal:  
(1) Being a party to the case or a close relative of a party;  
(2) Having an interest in the case personally or through a close relative;  
(3) Having served as a witness, expert evaluator, defender, or litigation agent in the case; or  
(4) Having any other relationship with a party to the case that may affect the impartial handling of the case.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　公安机关负责人、侦查人员不得有下列行为：  
（一）违反规定会见本案当事人及其委托人；  
（二）索取、接受本案当事人及其委托人的财物或者其他利益；  
（三）接受本案当事人及其委托人的宴请，或者参加由其支付费用的活动；  
（四）其他可能影响案件公正办理的不正当行为。  
违反前款规定的，应当责令其回避并依法追究法律责任。当事人及其法定代理人有权要求其回避。

**Article 33** The responsible person of a public security organ or an investigator shall not engage in any of the following acts:  
(1) Meeting with a party to the case or his or her authorized representative in violation of regulations;  
(2) Demanding or accepting property or other benefits from a party to the case or his or her authorized representative;  
(3) Accepting entertainment or participating in activities paid for by a party to the case or his or her authorized representative; or  
(4) Other improper acts that may affect the impartial handling of the case.  
Whoever violates the preceding paragraph shall be ordered to withdraw and shall be subject to legal liability according to law. The party concerned and his or her statutory agent shall have the right to request his or her withdrawal.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　公安机关负责人、侦查人员自行提出回避申请的，应当说明回避的理由；口头提出申请的，公安机关应当记录在案。  
当事人及其法定代理人要求公安机关负责人、侦查人员回避，应当提出申请，并说明理由；口头提出申请的，公安机关应当记录在案。

**Article 34** Where the responsible person of a public security organ or an investigator voluntarily files an application for withdrawal, he or she shall state the reasons for withdrawal; if the application is made orally, the public security organ shall record it in the case file.  
Where a party concerned or his or her statutory agent requests the withdrawal of the responsible person of a public security organ or an investigator, he or she shall file an application and state the reasons; if the request is made orally, the public security organ shall record it in the case file.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　侦查人员的回避，由县级以上公安机关负责人决定；县级以上公安机关负责人的回避，由同级人民检察院检察委员会决定。

**Article 35** The withdrawal of an investigator shall be decided by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level; the withdrawal of the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level shall be decided by the procuratorial committee of the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　当事人及其法定代理人对侦查人员提出回避申请的，公安机关应当在收到回避申请后二日以内作出决定并通知申请人；情况复杂的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以在收到回避申请后五日以内作出决定。  
当事人及其法定代理人对县级以上公安机关负责人提出回避申请的，公安机关应当及时将申请移送同级人民检察院。

**Article 36** Where a party concerned or his or her statutory agent applies for the withdrawal of an investigator, the public security organ shall make a decision within two days after receiving the application for withdrawal and notify the applicant; under complex circumstances, with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a decision may be made within five days after receiving the application for withdrawal.  
Where a party concerned or his or her statutory agent applies for the withdrawal of the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, the public security organ shall promptly transfer the application to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　当事人及其法定代理人对驳回申请回避的决定不服的，可以在收到驳回申请回避决定书后五日以内向作出决定的公安机关申请复议。  
公安机关应当在收到复议申请后五日以内作出复议决定并书面通知申请人。

**Article 37** Where a party concerned or his or her statutory agent is dissatisfied with the decision to reject the application for withdrawal, he or she may, within five days after receiving the decision to reject the application for withdrawal, apply for reconsideration to the public security organ that made the decision.  
The public security organ shall, within five days after receiving the application for reconsideration, make a reconsideration decision and notify the applicant in writing.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　在作出回避决定前，申请或者被申请回避的公安机关负责人、侦查人员不得停止对案件的侦查。  
作出回避决定后，申请或者被申请回避的公安机关负责人、侦查人员不得再参与本案的侦查工作。

**Article 38** Before a decision on withdrawal is made, the responsible person of the public security organ or investigator who applied for or is the subject of an application for withdrawal shall not suspend the investigation of the case.  
After a decision on withdrawal is made, the responsible person of the public security organ or investigator who applied for or was the subject of an application for withdrawal shall not participate in the investigation of the case again.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　被决定回避的公安机关负责人、侦查人员在回避决定作出以前所进行的诉讼活动是否有效，由作出决定的机关根据案件情况决定。

**Article 39** Whether the procedural activities conducted before the decision on withdrawal by the responsible person of a public security organ or investigator who has been ordered to withdraw are valid shall be determined by the organ that made the decision based on the circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　本章关于回避的规定适用于记录人、翻译人员和鉴定人。  
记录人、翻译人员和鉴定人需要回避的，由县级以上公安机关负责人决定。

**Article 40** The provisions in this Chapter on withdrawal shall apply to recorders, interpreters and expert evaluators.  
Where a recorder, interpreter or expert evaluator needs to withdraw, the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level shall make the decision.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　辩护人、诉讼代理人可以依照本章的规定要求回避、申请复议。

**Article 41** Defenders and litigation agents may, in accordance with the provisions of this Chapter, request withdrawal and apply for reconsideration.

### 第四章　律师参与刑事诉讼

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　公安机关应当保障辩护律师在侦查阶段依法从事下列执业活动：  
（一）向公安机关了解犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌的罪名和案件有关情况，提出意见；  
（二）与犯罪嫌疑人会见和通信，向犯罪嫌疑人了解案件有关情况；  
（三）为犯罪嫌疑人提供法律帮助、代理申诉、控告；  
（四）为犯罪嫌疑人申请变更强制措施。

**Article 42** The public security organ shall ensure that defense lawyers engage in the following professional activities according to law during the investigation stage:  
(1) Learning from the public security organ about the crimes suspected of by the criminal suspect and the relevant circumstances of the case, and presenting opinions;  
(2) Meeting and communicating with the criminal suspect, and learning from the criminal suspect about the relevant circumstances of the case;  
(3) Providing legal assistance to the criminal suspect, and acting on his/her behalf in petitions and accusations;  
(4) Applying for modification of compulsory measures for the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　公安机关在第一次讯问犯罪嫌疑人或者对犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施的时候，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权委托律师作为辩护人，并告知其如果因经济困难或者其他原因没有委托辩护律师的，可以向法律援助机构申请法律援助。告知的情形应当记录在案。  
对于同案的犯罪嫌疑人委托同一名辩护律师的，或者两名以上未同案处理但实施的犯罪存在关联的犯罪嫌疑人委托同一名辩护律师的，公安机关应当要求其更换辩护律师。

**Article 43** When interrogating a criminal suspect for the first time or taking compulsory measures against a criminal suspect, the public security organ shall inform the criminal suspect of the right to retain a lawyer as a defender, and shall inform him/her that if he/she has not retained a defense lawyer due to financial difficulties or other reasons, he/she may apply to a legal aid agency for legal aid. The fact of such notification shall be recorded in the case file.  
Where a criminal suspect in the same case retains the same defense lawyer, or where two or more criminal suspects who are not dealt with in the same case but whose crimes are related retain the same defense lawyer, the public security organ shall require them to change the defense lawyer.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　犯罪嫌疑人可以自己委托辩护律师。犯罪嫌疑人在押的，也可以由其监护人、近亲属代为委托辩护律师。  
犯罪嫌疑人委托辩护律师的请求可以书面提出，也可以口头提出。口头提出的，公安机关应当制作笔录，由犯罪嫌疑人签名、捺指印。

**Article 44** A criminal suspect may retain a defense lawyer on his/her own. If the criminal suspect is in custody, his/her guardian or close relative may also retain a defense lawyer on his/her behalf.  
The request of a criminal suspect to retain a defense lawyer may be made in writing or orally. If made orally, the public security organ shall make a record, which shall be signed and fingerprinted by the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　在押的犯罪嫌疑人向看守所提出委托辩护律师要求的，看守所应当及时将其请求转达给办案部门，办案部门应当及时向犯罪嫌疑人委托的辩护律师或者律师事务所转达该项请求。  
在押的犯罪嫌疑人仅提出委托辩护律师的要求，但提不出具体对象的，办案部门应当及时通知犯罪嫌疑人的监护人、近亲属代为委托辩护律师。犯罪嫌疑人无监护人或者近亲属的，办案部门应当及时通知当地律师协会或者司法行政机关为其推荐辩护律师。

**Article 45** Where a criminal suspect in custody makes a request to the detention house to retain a defense lawyer, the detention house shall promptly transmit the request to the case-handling department, and the case-handling department shall promptly transmit the request to the defense lawyer or law firm retained by the criminal suspect.  
Where a criminal suspect in custody only makes a request to retain a defense lawyer but does not specify a particular person, the case-handling department shall promptly notify the guardian or close relative of the criminal suspect to retain a defense lawyer on his/her behalf. If the criminal suspect has no guardian or close relative, the case-handling department shall promptly notify the local lawyers association or judicial administrative authority to recommend a defense lawyer for him/her.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　符合下列情形之一，犯罪嫌疑人没有委托辩护人的，公安机关应当自发现该情形之日起三日以内通知法律援助机构为犯罪嫌疑人指派辩护律师：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人是盲、聋、哑人，或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人可能被判处无期徒刑、死刑。

**Article 46** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances and has not retained a defender, the public security organ shall, within three days from the date of discovering such circumstance, notify a legal aid agency to assign a defense lawyer to the criminal suspect:  
(1) The criminal suspect is blind, deaf, or mute, or is a mentally ill person who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his/her own conduct;  
(2) The criminal suspect may be sentenced to life imprisonment or the death penalty.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　公安机关收到在押的犯罪嫌疑人提出的法律援助申请后，应当在二十四小时以内将其申请转交所在地的法律援助机构，并在三日以内通知申请人的法定代理人、近亲属或者其委托的其他人员协助提供有关证件、证明等相关材料。犯罪嫌疑人的法定代理人、近亲属或者其委托的其他人员地址不详无法通知的，应当在转交申请时一并告知法律援助机构。  
犯罪嫌疑人拒绝法律援助机构指派的律师作为辩护人或者自行委托辩护人的，公安机关应当在三日以内通知法律援助机构。

**Article 47** After receiving a legal aid application filed by a criminal suspect in custody, the public security organ shall, within twenty-four hours, transfer the application to the legal aid agency in its locality, and within three days, notify the applicant's legal representative, close relative, or other person authorized by him/her to assist in providing relevant certificates, documents, and other relevant materials. If the address of the criminal suspect's legal representative, close relative, or other person authorized by him/her is unknown and notification is impossible, the public security organ shall inform the legal aid agency of this fact when transferring the application.  
Where a criminal suspect refuses the lawyer assigned by the legal aid agency to act as his/her defender, or retains a defender on his/her own, the public security organ shall notify the legal aid agency within three days.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　辩护律师接受犯罪嫌疑人委托或者法律援助机构的指派后，应当及时告知公安机关并出示律师执业证书、律师事务所证明和委托书或者法律援助公函。

**Article 48** After accepting the retention by a criminal suspect or the assignment by a legal aid agency, a defense lawyer shall promptly notify the public security organ and present the lawyer's practice certificate, the law firm's certificate, and the letter of authorization or the legal aid official letter.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　犯罪嫌疑人、被告人入所羁押时没有委托辩护人，法律援助机构也没有指派律师提供辩护的，看守所应当告知其有权约见值班律师，获得法律咨询、程序选择建议、申请变更强制措施、对案件处理提出意见等法律帮助，并为犯罪嫌疑人、被告人约见值班律师提供便利。  
没有委托辩护人、法律援助机构没有指派律师提供辩护的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人，向看守所申请由值班律师提供法律帮助的，看守所应当在二十四小时内通知值班律师。

**Article 49** Where a criminal suspect or defendant is taken into custody and has not retained a defender, and no legal aid agency has assigned a lawyer to provide defense, the detention house shall inform him/her of the right to meet with a duty lawyer to obtain legal assistance such as legal consultation, suggestions on procedural options, applications for modification of compulsory measures, and opinions on case handling, and shall facilitate the meeting between the criminal suspect or defendant and the duty lawyer.  
Where a criminal suspect or defendant who has not retained a defender and for whom no legal aid agency has assigned a lawyer to provide defense applies to the detention house for legal assistance from a duty lawyer, the detention house shall notify the duty lawyer within twenty-four hours.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　辩护律师向公安机关了解案件有关情况的，公安机关应当依法将犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌的罪名以及当时已查明的该罪的主要事实，犯罪嫌疑人被采取、变更、解除强制措施，延长侦查羁押期限等案件有关情况，告知接受委托或者指派的辩护律师，并记录在案。

**Article 50** Where a defense lawyer inquires with the public security organ about the relevant circumstances of a case, the public security organ shall, according to law, inform the defense lawyer who has been retained or assigned of the crimes suspected of by the criminal suspect and the main facts of such crimes that have been ascertained at that time, the compulsory measures taken, modified, or lifted against the criminal suspect, the extension of the period of custodial investigation, and other relevant circumstances of the case, and shall make a record of such information.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　辩护律师可以同在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人会见、通信。

**Article 51** A defense lawyer may meet and communicate with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　对危害国家安全犯罪案件、恐怖活动犯罪案件，办案部门应当在将犯罪嫌疑人送看守所羁押时书面通知看守所；犯罪嫌疑人被监视居住的，应当在送交执行时书面通知执行机关。  
辩护律师在侦查期间要求会见前款规定案件的在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人，应当向办案部门提出申请。  
对辩护律师提出的会见申请，办案部门应当在收到申请后三日以内，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，作出许可或者不许可的决定，书面通知辩护律师，并及时通知看守所或者执行监视居住的部门。除有碍侦查或者可能泄露国家秘密的情形外，应当作出许可的决定。  
公安机关不许可会见的，应当说明理由。有碍侦查或者可能泄露国家秘密的情形消失后，公安机关应当许可会见。  
有下列情形之一的，属于本条规定的“有碍侦查”：  
（一）可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（二）可能引起犯罪嫌疑人自残、自杀或者逃跑的；  
（三）可能引起同案犯逃避、妨碍侦查的；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人的家属与犯罪有牵连的。

**Article 52** For cases involving crimes endangering national security or terrorist activities, the case-handling department shall, when sending the criminal suspect to the detention house for custody, notify the detention house in writing; if the criminal suspect is under residential surveillance, it shall notify the enforcement authority in writing when handing him/her over for enforcement.  
Where a defense lawyer requests during the investigation stage to meet with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance in a case specified in the preceding paragraph, he/she shall apply to the case-handling department.  
With respect to the meeting application filed by the defense lawyer, the case-handling department shall, within three days after receiving the application, submit it to the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, make a decision to permit or not to permit, notify the defense lawyer in writing, and promptly notify the detention house or the department executing residential surveillance. Except where it would hinder the investigation or might divulge state secrets, the decision to permit the meeting shall be made.  
If the public security organ does not permit the meeting, it shall state the reasons. After the circumstances that hindered the investigation or might have divulged state secrets disappear, the public security organ shall permit the meeting.  
The circumstances that "hinder the investigation" as mentioned in this Article include:  
(1) Where it might destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with a witness's testimony, or lead to collusion;  
(2) Where it might cause the criminal suspect to cause self-harm, commit suicide, or escape;  
(3) Where it might cause other offenders in the same case to evade or obstruct the investigation;  
(4) Where the family members of the criminal suspect are implicated in the crime.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　辩护律师要求会见在押的犯罪嫌疑人，看守所应当在查验其律师执业证书、律师事务所证明和委托书或者法律援助公函后，在四十八小时以内安排律师会见到犯罪嫌疑人，同时通知办案部门。  
侦查期间，辩护律师会见危害国家安全犯罪案件、恐怖活动犯罪案件在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人时，看守所或者监视居住执行机关还应当查验侦查机关的许可决定文书。

**Article 53** Where a defense lawyer requests to meet with a criminal suspect in custody, the detention house shall, after verifying the lawyer's practice certificate, the law firm's certificate, and the letter of authorization or the legal aid official letter, arrange for the lawyer to meet with the criminal suspect within forty-eight hours, and simultaneously notify the case-handling department.  
During the investigation stage, when a defense lawyer meets with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance in a case involving a crime endangering national security or a terrorist activity, the detention house or the authority executing residential surveillance shall also verify the document of permission from the investigation authority.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　辩护律师会见在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人需要聘请翻译人员的，应当向办案部门提出申请。办案部门应当在收到申请后三日以内，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，作出许可或者不许可的决定，书面通知辩护律师。对于具有[本规定第三十二条](#art-32)所列情形之一的，作出不予许可的决定，并通知其更换；不具有相关情形的，应当许可。  
翻译人员参与会见的，看守所或者监视居住执行机关应当查验公安机关的许可决定文书。

**Article 54** Where a defense lawyer needs to engage an interpreter for a meeting with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, he/she shall apply to the case-handling department. The case-handling department shall, within three days after receiving the application, submit it to the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and make a decision to permit or not to permit, and notify the defense lawyer in writing. If any of the circumstances listed in Article 32 of these Provisions exist, a decision not to permit shall be made, and the lawyer shall be notified to replace the interpreter; if there are no such circumstances, permission shall be granted.  
Where an interpreter participates in the meeting, the detention house or the authority executing residential surveillance shall verify the decision document of permission from the public security organ.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　辩护律师会见在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人时，看守所或者监视居住执行机关应当采取必要的管理措施，保障会见顺利进行，并告知其遵守会见的有关规定。辩护律师会见犯罪嫌疑人时，公安机关不得监听，不得派员在场。  
辩护律师会见在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人时，违反法律规定或者会见的规定的，看守所或者监视居住执行机关应当制止。对于严重违反规定或者不听劝阻的，可以决定停止本次会见，并及时通报其所在的律师事务所、所属的律师协会以及司法行政机关。

**Article 55** When a defense lawyer meets with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, the detention house or the authority executing residential surveillance shall take necessary management measures to ensure the smooth progress of the meeting, and shall inform the lawyer to comply with the relevant regulations on meetings. When a defense lawyer meets with a criminal suspect, the public security organ shall not eavesdrop or send personnel to be present.  
If a defense lawyer, when meeting with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, violates legal provisions or the regulations on meetings, the detention house or the authority executing residential surveillance shall stop the violation. For serious violations or refusal to heed warnings, the meeting may be terminated, and the law firm to which the lawyer belongs, the lawyers association, and the judicial administrative authority shall be promptly notified.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　辩护人或者其他任何人在刑事诉讼中，违反法律规定，实施干扰诉讼活动行为的，应当依法追究法律责任。  
辩护人实施干扰诉讼活动行为，涉嫌犯罪，属于公安机关管辖的，应当由办理辩护人所承办案件的公安机关报请上一级公安机关指定其他公安机关立案侦查，或者由上一级公安机关立案侦查。不得指定原承办案件公安机关的下级公安机关立案侦查。辩护人是律师的，立案侦查的公安机关应当及时通知其所在的律师事务所、所属的律师协会以及司法行政机关。

**Article 56** Where a defender or any other person, in criminal proceedings, violates legal provisions and commits acts that interfere with litigation activities, legal liability shall be investigated according to law.  
If a defender commits acts that interfere with litigation activities that are suspected of a crime and fall within the jurisdiction of the public security organ, the public security organ handling the case in which the defender is engaged shall request the public security organ at the next higher level to designate another public security organ to file a case for investigation, or the public security organ at the next higher level shall file a case for investigation. The public security organ at a lower level than the organ originally handling the case shall not be designated to file a case for investigation. If the defender is a lawyer, the public security organ that files the case for investigation shall promptly notify the law firm to which the lawyer belongs, the lawyers association, and the judicial administrative authority.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　辩护律师对在执业活动中知悉的委托人的有关情况和信息，有权予以保密。但是，辩护律师在执业活动中知悉委托人或者其他人，准备或者正在实施危害国家安全、公共安全以及严重危害他人人身安全的犯罪的，应当及时告知司法机关。

**Article 57** A defense lawyer shall have the right to keep confidential the relevant circumstances and information of the client that come to his/her knowledge during professional activities. However, if a defense lawyer, during professional activities, learns that the client or any other person is preparing to commit or is committing a crime that endangers national security, public security, or seriously endangers the personal safety of others, he/she shall promptly inform the judicial authority.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　案件侦查终结前，辩护律师提出要求的，公安机关应当听取辩护律师的意见，根据情况进行核实，并记录在案。辩护律师提出书面意见的，应当附卷。  
对辩护律师收集的犯罪嫌疑人不在犯罪现场、未达到刑事责任年龄、属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的证据，公安机关应当进行核实并将有关情况记录在案，有关证据应当附卷。

**Article 58** Before the conclusion of investigation of a case, if a defense lawyer so requests, the public security organ shall listen to the opinions of the defense lawyer, verify the facts as appropriate, and record them in the case file. If the defense lawyer submits written opinions, they shall be attached to the case file.  
With respect to the evidence collected by the defense lawyer proving that a criminal suspect was not at the crime scene, had not reached the age of criminal liability, or was a mentally ill person who is not criminally liable according to law, the public security organ shall verify such evidence, record the relevant information in the case file, and the relevant evidence shall be attached to the case file.

### 第五章　证 据

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　可以用于证明案件事实的材料，都是证据。  
证据包括：  
（一）物证；  
（二）书证；  
（三）证人证言；  
（四）被害人陈述；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人供述和辩解；  
（六）鉴定意见；  
（七）勘验、检查、侦查实验、搜查、查封、扣押、提取、辨认等笔录；  
（八）视听资料、电子数据。  
证据必须经过查证属实，才能作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 59** All materials that can be used to prove the facts of a case are evidence.  
Evidence includes:  
(1) Physical evidence;  
(2) Documentary evidence;  
(3) Testimony of witnesses;  
(4) Statements of victims;  
(5) Confessions and exculpations of criminal suspects;  
(6) Expert evaluation opinions;  
(7) Records of inspection, examination, investigative experimentation, search, sealing, seizure, extraction, identification, etc.;  
(8) Audio-visual materials and electronic data.  
Evidence must be verified before it can be used as the basis for determining the facts of a case.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　公安机关必须依照法定程序，收集、调取能够证实犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪、犯罪情节轻重的各种证据。必须保证一切与案件有关或者了解案情的公民，有客观地充分地提供证据的条件，除特殊情况外，可以吸收他们协助调查。

**Article 60** The public security organ must, in accordance with statutory procedures, collect and obtain all kinds of evidence that can prove whether a criminal suspect is guilty or innocent and the seriousness of the crime. It must ensure that all citizens who are related to the case or have knowledge of the case have the conditions to objectively and fully provide evidence, and except under special circumstances, they may be enlisted to assist in the investigation.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　公安机关向有关单位和个人收集、调取证据时，应当告知其必须如实提供证据。  
对涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的证据，应当保密。  
对于伪造证据、隐匿证据或者毁灭证据的，应当追究其法律责任。

**Article 61** When a public security organ collects or obtains evidence from relevant entities or individuals, it shall inform them that they must provide evidence truthfully.  
Evidence involving State secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy shall be kept confidential.  
Any person who forges, conceals, or destroys evidence shall be investigated for legal liability.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　公安机关向有关单位和个人调取证据，应当经办案部门负责人批准，开具调取证据通知书，明确调取的证据和提供时限。被调取单位及其经办人、持有证据的个人应当在通知书上盖章或者签名，拒绝盖章或者签名的，公安机关应当注明。必要时，应当采用录音录像方式固定证据内容及取证过程。

**Article 62** When a public security organ obtains evidence from relevant entities or individuals, it shall obtain approval from the head of the case-handling department and issue a notice of evidence collection, specifying the evidence to be collected and the time limit for provision. The entity from which evidence is obtained, its handling person, or the individual holding the evidence shall affix a seal or sign on the notice; if they refuse to affix a seal or sign, the public security organ shall note that. Where necessary, audio-visual recording shall be used to document the content of the evidence and the collection process.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　公安机关接受或者依法调取的行政机关在行政执法和查办案件过程中收集的物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据、鉴定意见、勘验笔录、检查笔录等证据材料，经公安机关审查符合法定要求的，可以作为证据使用。

**Article 63** Physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data, expert opinions, inspection records, examination records, and other evidentiary materials collected by administrative organs in the course of administrative law enforcement and case investigation that are received by or legally obtained by a public security organ may be used as evidence after the public security organ reviews and confirms that they meet the statutory requirements.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　收集、调取的物证应当是原物。只有在原物不便搬运、不易保存或者依法应当由有关部门保管、处理或者依法应当返还时，才可以拍摄或者制作足以反映原物外形或者内容的照片、录像或者复制品。  
物证的照片、录像或者复制品经与原物核实无误或者经鉴定证明为真实的，或者以其他方式确能证明其真实的，可以作为证据使用。原物的照片、录像或者复制品，不能反映原物的外形和特征的，不能作为证据使用。

**Article 64** The physical evidence collected or obtained shall be the original object. Only when the original object is inconvenient to transport, not easy to preserve, or should be kept or handled by the relevant department according to law, or should be returned according to law, may photographs, video recordings, or replicas that sufficiently reflect the original object's appearance or content be taken or produced.  
Photographs, video recordings, or replicas of physical evidence may be used as evidence if they have been verified to be consistent with the original object, or have been appraised to be authentic, or can otherwise be proven to be authentic. If the photographs, video recordings, or replicas of the original object cannot reflect the original object's appearance and characteristics, they shall not be used as evidence.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　收集、调取的书证应当是原件。只有在取得原件确有困难时，才可以使用副本或者复制件。  
书证的副本、复制件，经与原件核实无误或者经鉴定证明为真实的，或者以其他方式确能证明其真实的，可以作为证据使用。书证有更改或者更改迹象不能作出合理解释的，或者书证的副本、复制件不能反映书证原件及其内容的，不能作为证据使用。

**Article 65** The documentary evidence collected or obtained shall be the original. Only when it is genuinely difficult to obtain the original may copies or duplicates be used.  
Copies or duplicates of documentary evidence may be used as evidence if they have been verified to be consistent with the original, or have been appraised to be authentic, or can otherwise be proven to be authentic. If the documentary evidence has alterations or signs of alteration that cannot be reasonably explained, or if the copies or duplicates cannot reflect the original documentary evidence and its content, they shall not be used as evidence.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　收集、调取电子数据，能够扣押电子数据原始存储介质的，应当扣押原始存储介质，并制作笔录、予以封存。  
确因客观原因无法扣押原始存储介质的，可以现场提取或者网络在线提取电子数据。无法扣押原始存储介质，也无法现场提取或者网络在线提取的，可以采取打印、拍照或者录音录像等方式固定相关证据，并在笔录中注明原因。  
收集、调取的电子数据，足以保证完整性，无删除、修改、增加等情形的，可以作为证据使用。经审查无法确定真伪，或者制作、取得的时间、地点、方式等有疑问，不能提供必要证明或者作出合理解释的，不能作为证据使用。

**Article 66** When collecting or obtaining electronic data, if the original storage medium of the electronic data can be seized, the original storage medium shall be seized, and a record shall be made and sealed.  
If the original storage medium cannot be seized for objective reasons, electronic data may be extracted on-site or online. If neither the original storage medium nor on-site or online extraction is possible, the relevant evidence may be fixed by means such as printing, photographing, or audio-visual recording, and the reasons shall be noted in the record.  
Electronic data collected or obtained that sufficiently guarantees integrity and has no deletion, modification, or addition may be used as evidence. If the authenticity cannot be determined upon review, or if there are doubts about the time, place, or method of creation or acquisition, and the necessary proof or reasonable explanation cannot be provided, such evidence shall not be used as evidence.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　物证的照片、录像或者复制品，书证的副本、复制件，视听资料、电子数据的复制件，应当附有关制作过程及原件、原物存放处的文字说明，并由制作人和物品持有人或者物品持有单位有关人员签名。

**Article 67** Photographs, video recordings, or replicas of physical evidence, copies or duplicates of documentary evidence, and copies of audio-visual materials or electronic data shall be accompanied by written explanations regarding the production process and the location of the original or original object, and shall be signed by the producer and the holder of the item or a relevant person from the entity holding the item.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　公安机关提请批准逮捕书、起诉意见书必须忠实于事实真象。故意隐瞒事实真象的，应当依法追究责任。

**Article 68** The written request for approval of arrest and the indictment opinion submitted by a public security organ must be faithful to the facts of the case. Anyone who intentionally conceals the facts of the case shall be held liable according to law.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　需要查明的案件事实包括：  
（一）犯罪行为是否存在；  
（二）实施犯罪行为的时间、地点、手段、后果以及其他情节；  
（三）犯罪行为是否为犯罪嫌疑人实施；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人的身份；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人实施犯罪行为的动机、目的；  
（六）犯罪嫌疑人的责任以及与其他同案人的关系；  
（七）犯罪嫌疑人有无法定从重、从轻、减轻处罚以及免除处罚的情节；  
（八）其他与案件有关的事实。

**Article 69** The case facts that need to be ascertained include:  
(1) Whether a criminal act exists;  
(2) The time, place, method, consequences, and other circumstances of the criminal act;  
(3) Whether the criminal act was committed by a criminal suspect;  
(4) The identity of the criminal suspect;  
(5) The motive and purpose of the criminal suspect in committing the criminal act;  
(6) The criminal suspect's liability and relationship with other accomplices;  
(7) Whether the criminal suspect has any statutory circumstances for heavier, lighter, or mitigated punishment, or exemption from punishment;  
(8) Other facts relevant to the case.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　公安机关移送审查起诉的案件，应当做到犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分。  
证据确实、充分，应当符合以下条件：  
（一）认定的案件事实都有证据证明；  
（二）认定案件事实的证据均经法定程序查证属实；  
（三）综合全案证据，对所认定事实已排除合理怀疑。  
对证据的审查，应当结合案件的具体情况，从各证据与待证事实的关联程度、各证据之间的联系等方面进行审查判断。  
只有犯罪嫌疑人供述，没有其他证据的，不能认定案件事实；没有犯罪嫌疑人供述，证据确实、充分的，可以认定案件事实。

**Article 70** For a case transferred by a public security organ for review and prosecution, the criminal facts shall be clear, and the evidence shall be reliable and sufficient.  
Evidence being reliable and sufficient shall satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) Each fact determined in the case is supported by evidence;  
(2) The evidence used to determine the case facts has been verified to be true through statutory procedures;  
(3) Comprehensive evaluation of all evidence in the case excludes reasonable doubt regarding the determined facts.  
The review of evidence shall be conducted in light of the specific circumstances of the case, examining the degree of relevance of each piece of evidence to the fact to be proved and the connections between pieces of evidence.  
If there is only the confession of the criminal suspect and no other evidence, the case facts cannot be determined; if there is no confession of the criminal suspect, but the evidence is reliable and sufficient, the case facts may be determined.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　采用刑讯逼供等非法方法收集的犯罪嫌疑人供述和采用暴力、威胁等非法方法收集的证人证言、被害人陈述，应当予以排除。  
收集物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据违反法定程序，可能严重影响司法公正的，应当予以补正或者作出合理解释；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，对该证据应当予以排除。  
在侦查阶段发现有应当排除的证据的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，应当依法予以排除，不得作为提请批准逮捕、移送审查起诉的依据。  
人民检察院认为可能存在以非法方法收集证据情形，要求公安机关进行说明的，公安机关应当及时进行调查，并向人民检察院作出书面说明。

**Article 71** Confessions of criminal suspects obtained through illegal methods such as extortion of confessions by torture, and testimony of witnesses or statements of victims obtained through illegal methods such as violence or threats, shall be excluded.  
If the collection of physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, or electronic data violates statutory procedures and may seriously affect judicial fairness, such evidence shall be corrected or a reasonable explanation shall be provided; if it cannot be corrected or a reasonable explanation cannot be provided, the evidence shall be excluded.  
If evidence that should be excluded is discovered during the investigation stage, after approval by the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, such evidence shall be excluded according to law and shall not be used as a basis for requesting approval of arrest or transferring for review and prosecution.  
If the People's Procuratorate considers that there may be circumstances where evidence was collected by illegal methods and requires the public security organ to provide an explanation, the public security organ shall promptly investigate and provide a written explanation to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　人民法院认为现有证据材料不能证明证据收集的合法性，通知有关侦查人员或者公安机关其他人员出庭说明情况的，有关侦查人员或者其他人员应当出庭。必要时，有关侦查人员或者其他人员也可以要求出庭说明情况。侦查人员或者其他人员出庭，应当向法庭说明证据收集过程，并就相关情况接受发问。  
经人民法院通知，人民警察应当就其执行职务时目击的犯罪情况出庭作证。

**Article 72** If the People's Court considers that the existing evidentiary materials cannot prove the legality of evidence collection and notifies relevant investigative personnel or other personnel of the public security organ to appear in court to provide an explanation, such investigative or other personnel shall appear in court. Where necessary, relevant investigative or other personnel may also request to appear in court to provide an explanation. Investigative personnel or other personnel who appear in court shall explain to the court the process of evidence collection and accept questions regarding relevant circumstances.  
Upon notification by the People's Court, a people's policeman shall appear in court to testify regarding criminal acts witnessed in the course of performing duties.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　凡是知道案件情况的人，都有作证的义务。  
生理上、精神上有缺陷或者年幼，不能辨别是非，不能正确表达的人，不能作证人。  
对于证人能否辨别是非，能否正确表达，必要时可以进行审查或者鉴别。

**Article 73** Any person who has knowledge of the circumstances of a case has the obligation to testify.  
A person who, due to physical or mental defects or being too young to discern right from wrong or to express correctly, cannot serve as a witness.  
Where necessary, a person's ability to discern right from wrong and to express correctly may be examined or identified.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　公安机关应当保障证人及其近亲属的安全。  
对证人及其近亲属进行威胁、侮辱、殴打或者打击报复，构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任；尚不够刑事处罚的，依法给予治安管理处罚。

**Article 74** Public security organs shall ensure the safety of witnesses and their close relatives.  
Anyone who threatens, insults, assaults, or retaliates against a witness or their close relatives, if constituting a crime, shall be investigated for criminal liability according to law; if the conduct is not serious enough for criminal punishment, public security administration penalties shall be imposed according to law.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　对危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪、黑社会性质的组织犯罪、毒品犯罪等案件，证人、鉴定人、被害人因在侦查过程中作证，本人或者其近亲属的人身安全面临危险的，公安机关应当采取以下一项或者多项保护措施：  
（一）不公开真实姓名、住址、通讯方式和工作单位等个人信息；  
（二）禁止特定的人员接触被保护人；  
（三）对被保护人的人身和住宅采取专门性保护措施；  
（四）将被保护人带到安全场所保护；  
（五）变更被保护人的住所和姓名；  
（六）其他必要的保护措施。  
证人、鉴定人、被害人认为因在侦查过程中作证，本人或者其近亲属的人身安全面临危险，向公安机关请求予以保护，公安机关经审查认为符合前款规定的条件，确有必要采取保护措施的，应当采取上述一项或者多项保护措施。  
公安机关依法采取保护措施，可以要求有关单位和个人配合。  
案件移送审查起诉时，应当将采取保护措施的相关情况一并移交人民检察院。

**Article 75** For cases involving crimes endangering national security, terrorist activities, organized crimes of a mafia nature, drug crimes, etc., if a witness, expert evaluator, or victim, due to testifying during the investigation process, faces danger to the personal safety of themselves or their close relatives, the public security organ shall take one or more of the following protective measures:  
(1) Not disclosing personal information such as true names, addresses, communication methods, and work units;  
(2) Prohibiting specific persons from contacting the protected person;  
(3) Taking specialized protective measures for the protected person's person and residence;  
(4) Moving the protected person to a safe place for protection;  
(5) Changing the protected person's residence and name;  
(6) Other necessary protective measures.  
If a witness, expert evaluator, or victim considers that, due to testifying during the investigation process, they or their close relatives face danger to personal safety, and requests protection from the public security organ, and the public security organ, upon review, considers that the conditions in the preceding paragraph are met and that protective measures are indeed necessary, it shall take one or more of the above protective measures.  
A public security organ may require relevant entities or individuals to cooperate when taking protective measures according to law.  
When a case is transferred for review and prosecution, the relevant information regarding the protective measures taken shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate together.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　公安机关依法决定不公开证人、鉴定人、被害人的真实姓名、住址、通讯方式和工作单位等个人信息的，可以在起诉意见书、询问笔录等法律文书、证据材料中使用化名等代替证人、鉴定人、被害人的个人信息。但是，应当另行书面说明使用化名的情况并标明密级，单独成卷。

**Article 76** If a public security organ decides according to law not to disclose personal information such as the true names, addresses, communication methods, and work units of a witness, expert evaluator, or victim, it may use pseudonyms or other substitutes for such personal information in legal documents and evidentiary materials such as the indictment opinion and interrogation records. However, a separate written explanation of the use of pseudonyms shall be provided, indicating the classification level, and filed separately.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　证人保护工作所必需的人员、经费、装备等，应当予以保障。  
证人因履行作证义务而支出的交通、住宿、就餐等费用，应当给予补助。证人作证的补助列入公安机关业务经费。

**Article 77** The necessary personnel, funds, equipment, etc., required for witness protection work shall be ensured.  
Witnesses shall be compensated for expenses such as transportation, accommodation, and meals incurred in fulfilling the obligation to testify. The compensation for witness testimony shall be included in the public security organ's operational funds.

### 第六章　强制措施

#### 第一节　拘 传

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　公安机关根据案件情况对需要拘传的犯罪嫌疑人，或者经过传唤没有正当理由不到案的犯罪嫌疑人，可以拘传到其所在市、县公安机关执法办案场所进行讯问。  
需要拘传的，应当填写呈请拘传报告书，并附有关材料，报县级以上公安机关负责人批准。

**Article 78** A public security organ may, based on the circumstances of the case, summon for interrogation a criminal suspect who needs to be summoned by a summons or a criminal suspect who fails to appear before the organ without justifiable grounds after being summoned, to the law enforcement and case handling premises of the public security organ in the city or county where the suspect resides.  
Where a summons is required, a written report requesting the summons shall be completed, together with relevant materials, and submitted to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　公安机关拘传犯罪嫌疑人应当出示拘传证，并责令其在拘传证上签名、捺指印。  
犯罪嫌疑人到案后，应当责令其在拘传证上填写到案时间；拘传结束后，应当由其在拘传证上填写拘传结束时间。犯罪嫌疑人拒绝填写的，侦查人员应当在拘传证上注明。

**Article 79** When summoning a criminal suspect by a summons, a public security organ shall present the summons warrant and order the suspect to sign and affix his or her fingerprint on the warrant.  
After the criminal suspect arrives, the suspect shall be ordered to fill in the time of arrival on the summons warrant; after the summons ends, the suspect shall fill in the time of conclusion of the summons on the warrant. If the criminal suspect refuses to fill it in, the investigating personnel shall make a note thereof on the summons warrant.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　拘传持续的时间不得超过十二小时；案情特别重大、复杂，需要采取拘留、逮捕措施的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，拘传持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。不得以连续拘传的形式变相拘禁犯罪嫌疑人。  
拘传期限届满，未作出采取其他强制措施决定的，应当立即结束拘传。

**Article 80** The duration of a summons shall not exceed twelve hours; if the case is particularly serious or complex and measures of detention or arrest are required, with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, the duration of the summons shall not exceed twenty-four hours. The criminal suspect shall not be detained in a disguised form by means of continuous summons.  
Upon expiration of the summons period, if no decision to take other compulsory measures has been made, the summons shall be terminated immediately.

#### 第二节　取保候审

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　公安机关对具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，可以取保候审：  
（一）可能判处管制、拘役或者独立适用附加刑的；  
（二）可能判处有期徒刑以上刑罚，采取取保候审不致发生社会危险性的；  
（三）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理，怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女，采取取保候审不致发生社会危险性的；  
（四）羁押期限届满，案件尚未办结，需要继续侦查的。  
对拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，证据不符合逮捕条件，以及提请逮捕后，人民检察院不批准逮捕，需要继续侦查，并且符合取保候审条件的，可以依法取保候审。

**Article 81** A public security organ may grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The suspect may be sentenced to public surveillance, criminal detention, or independently subject to a supplementary punishment;  
(2) The suspect may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment or a heavier punishment, and the grant of release on bail pending investigation or trial will not create any danger to society;  
(3) The suspect is a woman who suffers from a serious disease, is unable to take care of herself, is pregnant, or is nursing her own infant, and the grant of release on bail pending investigation or trial will not create any danger to society;  
(4) The period of custody has expired, the case has not yet been concluded, and further investigation is needed.  
For a criminal suspect who has been detained, where the evidence does not meet the conditions for arrest, or after arrest is requested, the People's Procuratorate does not approve the arrest, and further investigation is needed, if the conditions for release on bail pending investigation or trial are met, release on bail pending investigation or trial may be granted according to law.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　对累犯，犯罪集团的主犯，以自伤、自残办法逃避侦查的犯罪嫌疑人，严重暴力犯罪以及其他严重犯罪的犯罪嫌疑人不得取保候审，但犯罪嫌疑人具有[本规定第八十一条](#art-81)第一款第三项、第四项规定情形的除外。

**Article 82** Release on bail pending investigation or trial shall not be granted to a recidivist, a principal offender of a criminal gang, a criminal suspect who evades investigation by self-inflicted injury or self-mutilation, or a criminal suspect involved in serious violent crimes or other serious crimes, except where the criminal suspect falls under the circumstances specified in subparagraphs (3) and (4) of the first paragraph of Article 81 of these Provisions.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　需要对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审的，应当制作呈请取保候审报告书，说明取保候审的理由、采取的保证方式以及应当遵守的规定，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作取保候审决定书。取保候审决定书应当向犯罪嫌疑人宣读，由犯罪嫌疑人签名、捺指印。

**Article 83** Where it is necessary to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect, a written report requesting release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be prepared, stating the reasons for the release on bail, the method of guarantee to be adopted, and the provisions to be observed. After approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be prepared. The decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be read to the criminal suspect, who shall sign and affix his or her fingerprint on it.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　公安机关决定对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审的，应当责令犯罪嫌疑人提出保证人或者交纳保证金。  
对同一犯罪嫌疑人，不得同时责令其提出保证人和交纳保证金。对未成年人取保候审，应当优先适用保证人保证。

**Article 84** Where a public security organ decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect, it shall order the criminal suspect to provide a guarantor or pay a deposit.  
For the same criminal suspect, the suspect shall not be ordered both to provide a guarantor and to pay a deposit at the same time. For a minor granted release on bail pending investigation or trial, the guarantee by a guarantor shall be applied preferentially.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　采取保证人保证的，保证人必须符合以下条件，并经公安机关审查同意：  
（一）与本案无牵连；  
（二）有能力履行保证义务；  
（三）享有政治权利，人身自由未受到限制；  
（四）有固定的住处和收入。

**Article 85** Where a guarantor provides a guarantee, the guarantor must meet the following conditions and be examined and approved by the public security organ:  
(1) Having no involvement in the case;  
(2) Having the ability to perform the guarantee obligations;  
(3) Enjoying political rights and not being subject to restrictions on personal freedom;  
(4) Having a fixed domicile and income.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　保证人应当履行以下义务：  
（一）监督被保证人遵守[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条的规定；  
（二）发现被保证人可能发生或者已经发生违反[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条规定的行为的，应当及时向执行机关报告。  
保证人应当填写保证书，并在保证书上签名、捺指印。

**Article 86** A guarantor shall perform the following obligations:  
(1) Supervise the person guaranteed to comply with the provisions of Articles 89 and 90 of these Provisions;  
(2) If discovering that the person guaranteed may commit or has already committed any act in violation of the provisions of Articles 89 and 90 of these Provisions, promptly report to the executing authority.  
The guarantor shall fill in a guarantee bond and sign and affix his or her fingerprint on it.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　犯罪嫌疑人的保证金起点数额为人民币一千元。犯罪嫌疑人为未成年人的，保证金起点数额为人民币五百元。具体数额应当综合考虑保证诉讼活动正常进行的需要、犯罪嫌疑人的社会危险性、案件的性质、情节、可能判处刑罚的轻重以及犯罪嫌疑人的经济状况等情况确定。

**Article 87** The minimum amount of the deposit for a criminal suspect is RMB 1,000. If the criminal suspect is a minor, the minimum amount of the deposit is RMB 500. The specific amount shall be determined by taking into comprehensive consideration the need to ensure the normal progress of litigation activities, the social danger of the criminal suspect, the nature and circumstances of the case, the severity of the possible sentence, and the financial situation of the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　县级以上公安机关应当在其指定的银行设立取保候审保证金专门账户，委托银行代为收取和保管保证金。  
提供保证金的人，应当一次性将保证金存入取保候审保证金专门账户。保证金应当以人民币交纳。  
保证金应当由办案部门以外的部门管理。严禁截留、坐支、挪用或者以其他任何形式侵吞保证金。

**Article 88** A public security organ at or above the county level shall establish a special account for the deposit for release on bail pending investigation or trial in a bank designated by it, and entrust the bank to collect and keep the deposit on its behalf.  
The person providing the deposit shall deposit the full amount at one time into the special account for the deposit for release on bail pending investigation or trial. The deposit shall be paid in RMB.  
The deposit shall be managed by a department other than the case-handling department. It is strictly forbidden to intercept, use without authorization, misappropriate, or embezzle the deposit in any other form.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　公安机关在宣布取保候审决定时，应当告知被取保候审人遵守以下规定：  
（一）未经执行机关批准不得离开所居住的市、县；  
（二）住址、工作单位和联系方式发生变动的，在二十四小时以内向执行机关报告；  
（三）在传讯的时候及时到案；  
（四）不得以任何形式干扰证人作证；  
（五）不得毁灭、伪造证据或者串供。

**Article 89** When announcing the decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial, a public security organ shall inform the person released on bail to observe the following provisions:  
(1) Not to leave the city or county where he or she resides without the approval of the executing authority;  
(2) To report to the executing authority within twenty-four hours if his or her address, work unit, or contact information changes;  
(3) To appear before the authority in a timely manner when summoned;  
(4) Not to interfere with witnesses giving testimony in any form;  
(5) Not to destroy or falsify evidence or collude with others in making confessions.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　公安机关在决定取保候审时，还可以根据案件情况，责令被取保候审人遵守以下一项或者多项规定：  
（一）不得进入与其犯罪活动等相关联的特定场所；  
（二）不得与证人、被害人及其近亲属、同案犯以及与案件有关联的其他特定人员会见或者以任何方式通信；  
（三）不得从事与其犯罪行为等相关联的特定活动；  
（四）将护照等出入境证件、驾驶证件交执行机关保存。  
公安机关应当综合考虑案件的性质、情节、社会影响、犯罪嫌疑人的社会关系等因素，确定特定场所、特定人员和特定活动的范围。

**Article 90** When deciding to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, a public security organ may also, based on the circumstances of the case, order the person released on bail to observe one or more of the following provisions:  
(1) Not to enter specific places related to his or her criminal activities;  
(2) Not to meet or communicate by any means with witnesses, victims and their close relatives, accomplices, or other specific persons related to the case;  
(3) Not to engage in specific activities related to his or her criminal acts;  
(4) To surrender his or her passport and other exit-entry documents, and driver's license to the executing authority for safekeeping.  
A public security organ shall determine the scope of specific places, specific persons, and specific activities by comprehensively considering factors such as the nature and circumstances of the case, social impact, and the criminal suspect's social connections.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　公安机关决定取保候审的，应当及时通知被取保候审人居住地的派出所执行。必要时，办案部门可以协助执行。  
采取保证人担保形式的，应当同时送交有关法律文书、被取保候审人基本情况、保证人基本情况等材料。采取保证金担保形式的，应当同时送交有关法律文书、被取保候审人基本情况和保证金交纳情况等材料。

**Article 91** Where a public security organ decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, it shall promptly notify the police station at the place where the person released on bail resides to execute the decision. If necessary, the case-handling department may assist in execution.  
If the form of guarantor guarantee is adopted, relevant legal documents, basic information of the person released on bail, and basic information of the guarantor shall be delivered at the same time. If the form of deposit guarantee is adopted, relevant legal documents, basic information of the person released on bail, and information on the payment of the deposit shall be delivered at the same time.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，负责执行的县级公安机关应当在收到法律文书和有关材料后二十四小时以内，指定被取保候审人居住地派出所核实情况后执行。

**Article 92** Where the People's Court or the People's Procuratorate decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, the public security organ at the county level responsible for execution shall, within twenty-four hours after receiving the legal documents and relevant materials, designate the police station at the place where the person released on bail resides to verify the circumstances and then execute the decision.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　执行取保候审的派出所应当履行下列职责：  
（一）告知被取保候审人必须遵守的规定，及其违反规定或者在取保候审期间重新犯罪应当承担的法律后果；  
（二）监督、考察被取保候审人遵守有关规定，及时掌握其活动、住址、工作单位、联系方式及变动情况；  
（三）监督保证人履行保证义务；  
（四）被取保候审人违反应当遵守的规定以及保证人未履行保证义务的，应当及时制止、采取紧急措施，同时告知决定机关。

**Article 93** The police station executing the release on bail pending investigation or trial shall perform the following duties:  
(1) Inform the person released on bail of the provisions that must be observed, and the legal consequences of violating such provisions or committing new crimes during the period of release on bail;  
(2) Supervise and inspect the person released on bail to observe relevant provisions, and promptly keep track of the person's activities, address, work unit, contact information, and any changes;  
(3) Supervise the guarantor to perform the guarantee obligations;  
(4) If the person released on bail violates the provisions that shall be observed or the guarantor fails to perform the guarantee obligations, promptly stop the violation, take emergency measures, and at the same time inform the decision-making authority.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　执行取保候审的派出所应当定期了解被取保候审人遵守取保候审规定的有关情况，并制作笔录。

**Article 94** The police station executing the release on bail pending investigation or trial shall regularly ascertain the relevant circumstances of the person released on bail's compliance with the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or trial, and make a record thereof.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　被取保候审人无正当理由不得离开所居住的市、县。有正当理由需要离开所居住的市、县的，应当经负责执行的派出所负责人批准。  
人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，负责执行的派出所在批准被取保候审人离开所居住的市、县前，应当征得决定取保候审的机关同意。

**Article 95** A person released on bail pending investigation or trial shall not leave the city or county where he or she resides without justifiable grounds. If justifiable grounds exist for needing to leave the city or county where he or she resides, approval of the responsible person of the police station responsible for execution shall be obtained.  
Where the People's Court or the People's Procuratorate decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, before approving the person released on bail to leave the city or county where he or she resides, the police station responsible for execution shall seek consent from the authority that decided the release on bail.
