<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　被取保候审人在取保候审期间违反[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条规定，已交纳保证金的，公安机关应当根据其违反规定的情节，决定没收部分或者全部保证金，并且区别情形，责令其具结悔过、重新交纳保证金、提出保证人，变更强制措施或者给予治安管理处罚；需要予以逮捕的，可以对其先行拘留。  
人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，被取保候审人违反应当遵守的规定，负责执行的派出所应当及时通知决定取保候审的机关。

**Article 96** If a person released on bail pending investigation or trial violates the provisions of Article 89 or Article 90 of these Provisions during the period of release on bail, and has paid a deposit, the public security organ shall, based on the circumstances of the violation, decide to confiscate part or all of the deposit, and depending on the situation, order the person to submit a letter of repentance, pay a new deposit, provide a new guarantor, change the compulsory measures, or impose a public security administrative penalty; if arrest is necessary, the person may be detained in advance.  
Where the People's Court or the People's Procuratorate decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, if the person released on bail violates the provisions that shall be observed, the police station responsible for execution shall promptly notify the authority that decided the release on bail.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　需要没收保证金的，应当经过严格审核后，报县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作没收保证金决定书。  
决定没收五万元以上保证金的，应当经设区的市一级以上公安机关负责人批准。

**Article 97** Where a deposit needs to be confiscated, after strict review, the matter shall be submitted to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and a decision on confiscation of the deposit shall be prepared.  
If a decision is made to confiscate a deposit of RMB 50,000 or more, it shall be approved by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the level of a city divided into districts.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　没收保证金的决定，公安机关应当在三日以内向被取保候审人宣读，并责令其在没收保证金决定书上签名、捺指印；被取保候审人在逃或者具有其他情形不能到场的，应当向其成年家属、法定代理人、辩护人或者单位、居住地的居民委员会、村民委员会宣布，由其成年家属、法定代理人、辩护人或者单位、居住地的居民委员会或者村民委员会的负责人在没收保证金决定书上签名。  
被取保候审人或者其成年家属、法定代理人、辩护人或者单位、居民委员会、村民委员会负责人拒绝签名的，公安机关应当在没收保证金决定书上注明。

**Article 98** The public security organ shall, within three days, read out the decision to confiscate the bond to the person released on bail pending investigation or trial, and order him to sign and affix his fingerprint on the written decision to confiscate the bond; if the person released on bail pending investigation or trial is at large or otherwise unable to be present, the decision shall be announced to his adult family members, legal representatives, defenders, or his work unit, or the residents' committee or villagers' committee of his place of residence, and the adult family members, legal representatives, defenders, or the responsible persons of the work unit, residents' committee or villagers' committee shall sign on the written decision to confiscate the bond.  
Where the person released on bail pending investigation or trial, or his adult family members, legal representatives, defenders, work unit, or responsible persons of the residents' committee or villagers' committee refuse to sign, the public security organ shall indicate such on the written decision to confiscate the bond.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　公安机关在宣读没收保证金决定书时，应当告知如果对没收保证金的决定不服，被取保候审人或者其法定代理人可以在五日以内向作出决定的公安机关申请复议。公安机关应当在收到复议申请后七日以内作出决定。  
被取保候审人或者其法定代理人对复议决定不服的，可以在收到复议决定书后五日以内向上一级公安机关申请复核一次。上一级公安机关应当在收到复核申请后七日以内作出决定。对上级公安机关撤销或者变更没收保证金决定的，下级公安机关应当执行。

**Article 99** When reading out the written decision to confiscate the bond, the public security organ shall inform that if the person released on bail pending investigation or trial or his legal representative does not accept the decision to confiscate the bond, he may, within five days, apply to the public security organ that made the decision for reconsideration. The public security organ shall make a decision within seven days after receiving the application for reconsideration.  
Where the person released on bail pending investigation or trial or his legal representative does not accept the reconsideration decision, he may, within five days after receiving the written reconsideration decision, apply to the public security organ at the next higher level for a re-examination once. The public security organ at the next higher level shall make a decision within seven days after receiving the application for re-examination. Where the higher-level public security organ revokes or modifies the decision to confiscate the bond, the lower-level public security organ shall execute such decision.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　没收保证金的决定已过复议期限，或者复议、复核后维持原决定或者变更没收保证金数额的，公安机关应当及时通知指定的银行将没收的保证金按照国家的有关规定上缴国库。人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，还应当在三日以内通知决定取保候审的机关。

**Article 100** Where the time limit for reconsideration of the decision to confiscate the bond has expired, or the original decision is upheld or the amount of the bond confiscated is modified after reconsideration or re-examination, the public security organ shall promptly notify the designated bank to turn over the confiscated bond to the State treasury in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State. Where a People's Court or People's Procuratorate decides to release on bail pending investigation or trial, the public security organ shall, within three days, also notify the organ that made the decision to release on bail pending investigation or trial.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　被取保候审人在取保候审期间，没有违反[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条有关规定，也没有重新故意犯罪的，或者具有[本规定第一百八十六条](#art-186)规定的情形之一的，在解除取保候审、变更强制措施的同时，公安机关应当制作退还保证金决定书，通知银行如数退还保证金。  
被取保候审人可以凭退还保证金决定书到银行领取退还的保证金。被取保候审人委托他人领取的，应当出具委托书。

**Article 101** Where, during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, the person released on bail pending investigation or trial has not violated the relevant provisions of Articles 89 and 90 of these Provisions, and has not committed another intentional crime, or falls under any of the circumstances provided in Article 186 of these Provisions, at the same time as the release on bail is lifted or compulsory measures are modified, the public security organ shall make a written decision to return the bond and notify the bank to return the bond in full.  
The person released on bail pending investigation or trial may, by presenting the written decision to return the bond, collect the returned bond from the bank. Where the person released on bail pending investigation or trial entrusts another person to collect the bond, he shall issue a letter of authorization.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　被取保候审人没有违反[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条规定，但在取保候审期间涉嫌重新故意犯罪被立案侦查的，负责执行的公安机关应当暂扣其交纳的保证金，待人民法院判决生效后，根据有关判决作出处理。

**Article 102** Where the person released on bail pending investigation or trial has not violated the provisions of Articles 89 and 90 of these Provisions, but is placed on file for investigation on suspicion of committing another intentional crime during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, the public security organ responsible for enforcement shall temporarily withhold the bond he has paid, and after the judgment of the People's Court becomes effective, handle it in accordance with the relevant judgment.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　被保证人违反应当遵守的规定，保证人未履行保证义务的，查证属实后，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，对保证人处一千元以上二万元以下罚款；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 103** Where the person guaranteed violates the provisions he shall comply with, and the guarantor fails to perform the guarantee obligations, after verification and upon approval by the person in charge of a public security organ at or above the county level, the guarantor shall be fined not less than 1,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan; if a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　决定对保证人罚款的，应当报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作对保证人罚款决定书，在三日以内送达保证人，告知其如果对罚款决定不服，可以在收到决定书之日起五日以内向作出决定的公安机关申请复议。公安机关应当在收到复议申请后七日以内作出决定。  
保证人对复议决定不服的，可以在收到复议决定书后五日以内向上一级公安机关申请复核一次。上一级公安机关应当在收到复核申请后七日以内作出决定。对上级公安机关撤销或者变更罚款决定的，下级公安机关应当执行。

**Article 104** Where a decision is made to fine the guarantor, approval shall be obtained from the person in charge of a public security organ at or above the county level, a written decision to fine the guarantor shall be made, and it shall be served on the guarantor within three days, informing him that if he does not accept the fine decision, he may, within five days from the date of receipt of the written decision, apply to the public security organ that made the decision for reconsideration. The public security organ shall make a decision within seven days after receiving the application for reconsideration.  
Where the guarantor does not accept the reconsideration decision, he may, within five days after receiving the written reconsideration decision, apply to the public security organ at the next higher level for a re-examination once. The public security organ at the next higher level shall make a decision within seven days after receiving the application for re-examination. Where the higher-level public security organ revokes or modifies the fine decision, the lower-level public security organ shall execute such decision.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　对于保证人罚款的决定已过复议期限，或者复议、复核后维持原决定或者变更罚款数额的，公安机关应当及时通知指定的银行将保证人罚款按照国家的有关规定上缴国库。人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，还应当在三日以内通知决定取保候审的机关。

**Article 105** Where the time limit for reconsideration of the decision to fine the guarantor has expired, or the original decision is upheld or the amount of fine is modified after reconsideration or re-examination, the public security organ shall promptly notify the designated bank to turn over the fine on the guarantor to the State treasury in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State. Where a People's Court or People's Procuratorate decides to release on bail pending investigation or trial, the public security organ shall, within three days, also notify the organ that made the decision to release on bail pending investigation or trial.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　对于犯罪嫌疑人采取保证人保证的，如果保证人在取保候审期间情况发生变化，不愿继续担保或者丧失担保条件，公安机关应当责令被取保候审人重新提出保证人或者交纳保证金，或者作出变更强制措施的决定。  
人民法院、人民检察院决定取保候审的，负责执行的派出所应当自发现保证人不愿继续担保或者丧失担保条件之日起三日以内通知决定取保候审的机关。

**Article 106** Where a guarantor guarantee is adopted for a criminal suspect, if the situation of the guarantor changes during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, and he is unwilling to continue to guarantee or has lost the conditions for guarantee, the public security organ shall order the person released on bail pending investigation or trial to propose a new guarantor or pay a bond, or make a decision to modify the compulsory measures.  
Where a People's Court or People's Procuratorate decides to release on bail pending investigation or trial, the responsible police station shall, within three days from the date of discovering that the guarantor is unwilling to continue to guarantee or has lost the conditions for guarantee, notify the organ that made the decision to release on bail pending investigation or trial.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　公安机关在取保候审期间不得中断对案件的侦查，对取保候审的犯罪嫌疑人，根据案情变化，应当及时变更强制措施或者解除取保候审。  
取保候审最长不得超过十二个月。

**Article 107** The public security organ shall not suspend the investigation of a case during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial. For a criminal suspect released on bail pending investigation or trial, the public security organ shall, based on changes in the circumstances of the case, timely modify the compulsory measures or lift the release on bail pending investigation or trial.  
The period of release on bail pending investigation or trial shall not exceed twelve months at most.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　需要解除取保候审的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作解除取保候审决定书、通知书，并及时通知负责执行的派出所、被取保候审人、保证人和有关单位。  
人民法院、人民检察院作出解除取保候审决定的，负责执行的公安机关应当根据决定书及时解除取保候审，并通知被取保候审人、保证人和有关单位。

**Article 108** Where it is necessary to lift the release on bail pending investigation or trial, approval shall be obtained from the person in charge of a public security organ at or above the county level, a written decision and a notice to lift the release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be made, and the responsible police station, the person released on bail pending investigation or trial, the guarantor, and relevant entities shall be promptly notified.  
Where a People's Court or People's Procuratorate makes a decision to lift the release on bail pending investigation or trial, the responsible public security organ shall, based on the written decision, promptly lift the release on bail pending investigation or trial, and notify the person released on bail pending investigation or trial, the guarantor, and relevant entities.

#### 第三节　监视居住

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　公安机关对符合逮捕条件，有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，可以监视居住：  
（一）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理的；  
（二）怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女；  
（三）系生活不能自理的人的唯一扶养人；  
（四）因案件的特殊情况或者办理案件的需要，采取监视居住措施更为适宜的；  
（五）羁押期限届满，案件尚未办结，需要采取监视居住措施的。  
对人民检察院决定不批准逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，需要继续侦查，并且符合监视居住条件的，可以监视居住。  
对于符合取保候审条件，但犯罪嫌疑人不能提出保证人，也不交纳保证金的，可以监视居住。  
对于被取保候审人违反[本规定第八十九条](#art-89)、第九十条规定的，可以监视居住。

**Article 109** The public security organ may place under residential surveillance a criminal suspect who meets the conditions for arrest and falls under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) suffering from a serious illness and unable to take care of himself;  
(2) being a woman who is pregnant or is nursing her own infant;  
(3) being the sole supporter of a person who cannot take care of himself;  
(4) due to the special circumstances of the case or the needs of handling the case, it is more appropriate to adopt residential surveillance;  
(5) the period of custody has expired, the case has not yet been concluded, and it is necessary to adopt residential surveillance.  
Where a People's Procuratorate decides not to approve arrest, and the criminal suspect needs further investigation and meets the conditions for residential surveillance, he may be placed under residential surveillance.  
Where the conditions for release on bail pending investigation or trial are met, but the criminal suspect cannot propose a guarantor or pay a bond, he may be placed under residential surveillance.  
Where the person released on bail pending investigation or trial violates the provisions of Articles 89 and 90 of these Provisions, he may be placed under residential surveillance.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　对犯罪嫌疑人监视居住，应当制作呈请监视居住报告书，说明监视居住的理由、采取监视居住的方式以及应当遵守的规定，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作监视居住决定书。监视居住决定书应当向犯罪嫌疑人宣读，由犯罪嫌疑人签名、捺指印。

**Article 110** To place a criminal suspect under residential surveillance, a written report requesting residential surveillance shall be prepared, stating the reasons for residential surveillance, the method of residential surveillance, and the provisions to be complied with, and, after approval by the person in charge of a public security organ at or above the county level, a written decision on residential surveillance shall be made. The written decision on residential surveillance shall be read out to the criminal suspect, who shall sign and affix his fingerprint on it.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　监视居住应当在犯罪嫌疑人、被告人住处执行；无固定住处的，可以在指定的居所执行。对于涉嫌危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪，在住处执行可能有碍侦查的，经上一级公安机关批准，也可以在指定的居所执行。  
有下列情形之一的，属于本条规定的“有碍侦查”：  
（一）可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（二）可能引起犯罪嫌疑人自残、自杀或者逃跑的；  
（三）可能引起同案犯逃避、妨碍侦查的；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人在住处执行监视居住有人身危险的；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的家属或者所在单位人员与犯罪有牵连的。  
指定居所监视居住的，不得要求被监视居住人支付费用。

**Article 111** Residential surveillance shall be executed at the domicile of the criminal suspect or defendant; if there is no fixed domicile, it may be executed at a designated residence. For crimes suspected of endangering national security or terrorist activities, if execution at the domicile may hinder investigation, with the approval of the public security organ at the next higher level, it may also be executed at a designated residence.  
Any of the following circumstances falls under the term 'may hinder investigation' as provided in this Article:  
(1) it may result in destruction or falsification of evidence, interference with witnesses giving testimony, or collusion in making confessions;  
(2) it may cause the criminal suspect to injure himself, commit suicide, or escape;  
(3) it may cause other accomplices to evade or obstruct investigation;  
(4) the criminal suspect or defendant may be in personal danger if residential surveillance is executed at the domicile;  
(5) the family members or personnel of the work unit of the criminal suspect or defendant are involved in the crime.  
Where residential surveillance is executed at a designated residence, the person under residential surveillance shall not be required to pay fees.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　固定住处，是指被监视居住人在办案机关所在的市、县内生活的合法住处；指定的居所，是指公安机关根据案件情况，在办案机关所在的市、县内为被监视居住人指定的生活居所。  
指定的居所应当符合下列条件：  
（一）具备正常的生活、休息条件；  
（二）便于监视、管理；  
（三）保证安全。  
公安机关不得在羁押场所、专门的办案场所或者办公场所执行监视居住。

**Article 112** A fixed domicile means the lawful residence where the person under residential surveillance lives within the city or county where the case-handling authority is located; a designated residence means the living residence designated by the public security organ for the person under residential surveillance within the city or county where the case-handling authority is located based on the circumstances of the case.  
The designated residence shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) having normal living and rest conditions;  
(2) being convenient for surveillance and management;  
(3) ensuring safety.  
The public security organ shall not execute residential surveillance in a custodial facility, a special case-handling site, or an office premises.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　指定居所监视居住的，除无法通知的以外，应当制作监视居住通知书，在执行监视居住后二十四小时以内，由决定机关通知被监视居住人的家属。  
有下列情形之一的，属于本条规定的“无法通知”：  
（一）不讲真实姓名、住址、身份不明的；  
（二）没有家属的；  
（三）提供的家属联系方式无法取得联系的；  
（四）因自然灾害等不可抗力导致无法通知的。  
无法通知的情形消失以后，应当立即通知被监视居住人的家属。  
无法通知家属的，应当在监视居住通知书中注明原因。

**Article 113** Where residential surveillance is executed at a designated residence, except where it is impossible to notify, a written notice of residential surveillance shall be prepared and, within 24 hours after the execution of residential surveillance, the authority that made the decision shall notify the family members of the person under residential surveillance.  
Any of the following circumstances falls under the term 'impossible to notify' as provided in this Article:  
(1) the person does not provide his true name, address, and identity is unknown;  
(2) there are no family members;  
(3) the contact information provided for family members cannot be reached;  
(4) notification is impossible due to force majeure such as natural disasters.  
After the circumstances making notification impossible disappear, the family members of the person under residential surveillance shall be notified immediately.  
If it is impossible to notify the family members, the reason shall be noted in the written notice of residential surveillance.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　被监视居住人委托辩护律师，适用[本规定第四十三条](#art-43)、第四十四条、第四十五条规定。

**Article 114** Where the person under residential surveillance entrusts a defense lawyer, the provisions of Articles 43, 44, and 45 of these Provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　公安机关在宣布监视居住决定时，应当告知被监视居住人必须遵守以下规定：  
（一）未经执行机关批准不得离开执行监视居住的处所；  
（二）未经执行机关批准不得会见他人或者以任何方式通信；  
（三）在传讯的时候及时到案；  
（四）不得以任何形式干扰证人作证；  
（五）不得毁灭、伪造证据或者串供；  
（六）将护照等出入境证件、身份证件、驾驶证件交执行机关保存。

**Article 115** When announcing the decision on residential surveillance, the public security organ shall inform the person under residential surveillance that he must comply with the following provisions:  
(1) he shall not leave the premises where residential surveillance is executed without the approval of the executing authority;  
(2) he shall not meet with others or communicate by any means without the approval of the executing authority;  
(3) he shall appear before the authority in a timely manner when summoned;  
(4) he shall not interfere with witnesses giving testimony in any form;  
(5) he shall not destroy or falsify evidence or collude in confessions;  
(6) he shall deliver his passport and other exit-entry documents, identity card, and driving license to the executing authority for keeping.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　公安机关对被监视居住人，可以采取电子监控、不定期检查等监视方法对其遵守监视居住规定的情况进行监督；在侦查期间，可以对被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人的电话、传真、信函、邮件、网络等通信进行监控。

**Article 116** The public security organ may adopt surveillance methods such as electronic monitoring and irregular inspections to supervise the compliance of the person under residential surveillance with the provisions on residential surveillance; during the period of investigation, it may monitor the communications of a criminal suspect under residential surveillance, including telephone, fax, correspondence, email, and internet communications.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　公安机关决定监视居住的，由被监视居住人住处或者指定居所所在地的派出所执行，办案部门可以协助执行。必要时，也可以由办案部门负责执行，派出所或者其他部门协助执行。

**Article 117** Where a public security organ decides on residential surveillance, it shall be executed by the police station of the location of the domicile or designated residence of the person under residential surveillance, and the case-handling department may assist in execution. Where necessary, it may also be executed by the case-handling department, with the assistance of the police station or other departments.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　人民法院、人民检察院决定监视居住的，负责执行的县级公安机关应当在收到法律文书和有关材料后二十四小时以内，通知被监视居住人住处或者指定居所所在地的派出所，核实被监视居住人身份、住处或者居所等情况后执行。必要时，可以由人民法院、人民检察院协助执行。  
负责执行的派出所应当及时将执行情况通知决定监视居住的机关。

**Article 118** Where a People's Court or a People's Procuratorate decides to place a person under residential surveillance, the county-level public security organ responsible for enforcement shall, within 24 hours after receiving the legal documents and relevant materials, notify the local police station at the place of residence or designated domicile of the person under residential surveillance, verify the identity, residence or domicile of the person, and then execute the decision. If necessary, the People's Court or the People's Procuratorate may assist in the execution.  
The responsible police station shall promptly notify the organ that decided the residential surveillance of the execution status.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　负责执行监视居住的派出所或者办案部门应当严格对被监视居住人进行监督考察，确保安全。

**Article 119** The police station or case-handling department responsible for executing residential surveillance shall strictly supervise and inspect the person under residential surveillance to ensure safety.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　被监视居住人有正当理由要求离开住处或者指定的居所以及要求会见他人或者通信的，应当经负责执行的派出所或者办案部门负责人批准。  
人民法院、人民检察院决定监视居住的，负责执行的派出所在批准被监视居住人离开住处或者指定的居所以及与他人会见或者通信前，应当征得决定监视居住的机关同意。

**Article 120** Where a person under residential surveillance requests to leave the residence or designated domicile, or requests to meet others or correspond, for justified reasons, approval shall be obtained from the head of the responsible police station or case-handling department.  
Where a People's Court or a People's Procuratorate decides on residential surveillance, the responsible police station shall, before approving the person's leaving the residence or designated domicile or meeting others or corresponding, obtain the consent of the organ that decided the residential surveillance.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　被监视居住人违反应当遵守的规定，公安机关应当区分情形责令被监视居住人具结悔过或者给予治安管理处罚。情节严重的，可以予以逮捕；需要予以逮捕的，可以对其先行拘留。  
人民法院、人民检察院决定监视居住的，被监视居住人违反应当遵守的规定，负责执行的派出所应当及时通知决定监视居住的机关。

**Article 121** Where a person under residential surveillance violates the provisions that shall be observed, the public security organ shall order the person to submit a statement of repentance or impose a public security penalty depending on the circumstances. If the circumstances are serious, the person may be arrested; if arrest is necessary, the person may be held in custody in advance.  
Where a People's Court or a People's Procuratorate decides on residential surveillance and the person under residential surveillance violates the provisions that shall be observed, the responsible police station shall promptly notify the organ that decided the residential surveillance.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　在监视居住期间，公安机关不得中断案件的侦查，对被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人，应当根据案情变化，及时解除监视居住或者变更强制措施。  
监视居住最长不得超过六个月。

**Article 122** During the period of residential surveillance, the public security organ shall not discontinue the investigation of the case. For a criminal suspect under residential surveillance, the organ shall promptly cancel the residential surveillance or modify the compulsory measures based on changes in the circumstances of the case.  
The maximum term of residential surveillance shall not exceed six months.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　需要解除监视居住的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作解除监视居住决定书，并及时通知负责执行的派出所、被监视居住人和有关单位。  
人民法院、人民检察院作出解除、变更监视居住决定的，负责执行的公安机关应当及时解除并通知被监视居住人和有关单位。

**Article 123** Where it is necessary to cancel residential surveillance, approval shall be obtained from the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, a decision to cancel residential surveillance shall be prepared, and the responsible police station, the person under residential surveillance, and the relevant entities shall be notified promptly.  
Where a People's Court or a People's Procuratorate makes a decision to cancel or modify residential surveillance, the public security organ responsible for enforcement shall promptly cancel the residential surveillance and notify the person under residential surveillance and the relevant entities.

#### 第四节　拘 留

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　公安机关对于现行犯或者重大嫌疑分子，有下列情形之一的，可以先行拘留：  
（一）正在预备犯罪、实行犯罪或者在犯罪后即时被发觉的；  
（二）被害人或者在场亲眼看见的人指认他犯罪的；  
（三）在身边或者住处发现有犯罪证据的；  
（四）犯罪后企图自杀、逃跑或者在逃的；  
（五）有毁灭、伪造证据或者串供可能的；  
（六）不讲真实姓名、住址，身份不明的；  
（七）有流窜作案、多次作案、结伙作案重大嫌疑的。

**Article 124** A public security organ may detain in advance a flagrant offender or a major suspect under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The person is preparing to commit a crime, is committing a crime, or is discovered immediately after committing a crime;  
(2) The victim or an eyewitness identifies the person as the offender;  
(3) Evidence of a crime is found on the person or at the person's residence;  
(4) The person attempts to commit suicide, escape, or is at large after committing a crime;  
(5) The person is likely to destroy or falsify evidence or collude with others;  
(6) The person does not provide true name or address, and identity is unknown;  
(7) The person is under strong suspicion of committing crimes across different regions, committing multiple crimes, or committing crimes in a group.

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　拘留犯罪嫌疑人，应当填写呈请拘留报告书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作拘留证。执行拘留时，必须出示拘留证，并责令被拘留人在拘留证上签名、捺指印，拒绝签名、捺指印的，侦查人员应当注明。  
紧急情况下，对于符合[本规定第一百二十四条](#art-124)所列情形之一的，经出示人民警察证，可以将犯罪嫌疑人口头传唤至公安机关后立即审查，办理法律手续。

**Article 125** To detain a criminal suspect, a request for detention report shall be completed, approval shall be obtained from the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, and a detention warrant shall be prepared. When executing the detention, the detention warrant must be produced, and the detained person shall be ordered to sign and affix a fingerprint on the detention warrant. If the person refuses to sign or affix a fingerprint, the investigating personnel shall make a note thereof.  
In emergency circumstances, where the circumstances fall under any of the items listed in Article 124 of these Provisions, after producing the People's Police identification card, the criminal suspect may be orally summoned to the public security organ for immediate interrogation and handling of legal procedures.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　拘留后，应当立即将被拘留人送看守所羁押，至迟不得超过二十四小时。  
异地执行拘留，无法及时将犯罪嫌疑人押解回管辖地的，应当在宣布拘留后立即将其送抓获地看守所羁押，至迟不得超过二十四小时。到达管辖地后，应当立即将犯罪嫌疑人送看守所羁押。

**Article 126** After detention, the detained person shall be immediately sent to a detention center for custody, and shall in no case exceed 24 hours.  
Where the detention is executed in a different locality and the criminal suspect cannot be promptly escorted back to the place under jurisdiction, the suspect shall be immediately sent to a detention center at the place of capture for custody after the detention is announced, and shall in no case exceed 24 hours. Upon arrival at the place under jurisdiction, the criminal suspect shall be immediately sent to a detention center for custody.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　除无法通知或者涉嫌危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪通知可能有碍侦查的情形以外，应当在拘留后二十四小时以内制作拘留通知书，通知被拘留人的家属。拘留通知书应当写明拘留原因和羁押处所。  
本条规定的“无法通知”的情形适用[本规定第一百一十三条](#art-113)第二款的规定。  
有下列情形之一的，属于本条规定的“有碍侦查”：  
（一）可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（二）可能引起同案犯逃避、妨碍侦查的；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人的家属与犯罪有牵连的。  
无法通知、有碍侦查的情形消失以后，应当立即通知被拘留人的家属。  
对于没有在二十四小时以内通知家属的，应当在拘留通知书中注明原因。

**Article 127** Except where notification is impossible or where the suspect is suspected of committing crimes of endangering national security or terrorist activities and notification may hinder the investigation, a detention notification shall be prepared within 24 hours after detention to notify the family members of the detained person. The detention notification shall state the reasons for detention and the place of custody.  
The circumstances under which 'notification is impossible' as provided in this Article shall be governed by the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 113 of these Provisions.  
Any of the following circumstances falls under the circumstances where 'notification may hinder the investigation' as provided in this Article:  
(1) The person is likely to destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with witness testimony, or collude with others;  
(2) The person is likely to cause a co-offender to evade or hinder investigation;  
(3) The family members of the criminal suspect are implicated in the crime.  
After the circumstances that make notification impossible or hinder the investigation disappear, the family members of the detained person shall be notified immediately.  
Where the family members have not been notified within 24 hours, the reasons shall be noted in the detention notification.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　对被拘留的人，应当在拘留后二十四小时以内进行讯问。发现不应当拘留的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作释放通知书，看守所凭释放通知书发给被拘留人释放证明书，将其立即释放。

**Article 128** The detained person shall be interrogated within 24 hours after detention. If it is discovered that the person should not have been detained, approval shall be obtained from the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, a release notice shall be prepared, and the detention center shall, based on the release notice, issue a release certificate to the detained person and immediately release the person.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，经过审查认为需要逮捕的，应当在拘留后的三日以内，提请人民检察院审查批准。在特殊情况下，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，提请审查批准逮捕的时间可以延长一日至四日。  
对流窜作案、多次作案、结伙作案的重大嫌疑分子，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，提请审查批准逮捕的时间可以延长至三十日。  
本条规定的“流窜作案”，是指跨市、县管辖范围连续作案，或者在居住地作案后逃跑到外市、县继续作案；“多次作案”，是指三次以上作案；“结伙作案”，是指二人以上共同作案。

**Article 129** For a detained criminal suspect who is considered to require arrest after review, the public security organ shall, within three days after detention, submit the matter to the People's Procuratorate for examination and approval. Under special circumstances, with the approval of the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, the time limit for submitting for examination and approval of arrest may be extended by one to four days.  
For a major suspect who commits crimes across different regions, commits multiple crimes, or commits crimes in a group, with the approval of the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, the time limit for submitting for examination and approval of arrest may be extended to up to 30 days.  
'Crimes across different regions' as provided in this Article means continuously committing crimes across the jurisdiction of different cities or counties, or fleeing to another city or county to continue committing crimes after committing crimes at the place of residence; 'multiple crimes' means committing crimes three or more times; 'crimes in a group' means two or more persons committing crimes jointly.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　犯罪嫌疑人不讲真实姓名、住址，身份不明的，应当对其身份进行调查。对符合逮捕条件的犯罪嫌疑人，也可以按其自报的姓名提请批准逮捕。

**Article 130** Where a criminal suspect does not provide true name and address, and identity is unknown, an investigation into the identity of the suspect shall be conducted. For a criminal suspect who meets the conditions for arrest, the matter may also be submitted for approval of arrest under the name declared by the suspect.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人审查后，根据案件情况报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，分别作出如下处理：  
（一）需要逮捕的，在拘留期限内，依法办理提请批准逮捕手续；  
（二）应当追究刑事责任，但不需要逮捕的，依法直接向人民检察院移送审查起诉，或者依法办理取保候审或者监视居住手续后，向人民检察院移送审查起诉；  
（三）拘留期限届满，案件尚未办结，需要继续侦查的，依法办理取保候审或者监视居住手续；  
（四）具有[本规定第一百八十六条](#art-186)规定情形之一的，释放被拘留人，发给释放证明书；需要行政处理的，依法予以处理或者移送有关部门。

**Article 131** After reviewing a detained criminal suspect, based on the circumstances of the case and upon approval by the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, the following actions shall be taken respectively:  
(1) If arrest is necessary, the formalities for submitting an application for approval of arrest shall be handled according to law within the detention period;  
(2) If criminal liability shall be investigated but arrest is not necessary, the case shall be directly transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution according to law, or after handling the formalities for release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance according to law, the case shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution;  
(3) If the detention period expires but the case is not yet completed and further investigation is necessary, the formalities for release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance shall be handled according to law;  
(4) If any of the circumstances provided in Article 186 of these Provisions exists, the detained person shall be released and a release certificate shall be issued; if administrative handling is necessary, the person shall be handled according to law or transferred to the relevant department.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　人民检察院决定拘留犯罪嫌疑人的，由县级以上公安机关凭人民检察院送达的决定拘留的法律文书制作拘留证并立即执行。必要时，可以请人民检察院协助。拘留后，应当及时通知人民检察院。  
公安机关未能抓获犯罪嫌疑人的，应当将执行情况和未能抓获犯罪嫌疑人的原因通知作出拘留决定的人民检察院。对于犯罪嫌疑人在逃的，在人民检察院撤销拘留决定之前，公安机关应当组织力量继续执行。

**Article 132** Where a People's Procuratorate decides to detain a criminal suspect, the public security organ at or above the county level shall prepare a detention warrant based on the legal document for the decision of detention served by the People's Procuratorate and execute it immediately. If necessary, the People's Procuratorate may be requested to assist. After detention, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified promptly.  
If the public security organ fails to capture the criminal suspect, the status of execution and the reasons for failure to capture the suspect shall be notified to the People's Procuratorate that made the detention decision. If the criminal suspect is at large, before the People's Procuratorate revokes the detention decision, the public security organ shall organize forces to continue execution.

#### 第五节　逮 捕

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　对有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪嫌疑人，采取取保候审尚不足以防止发生下列社会危险性的，应当提请批准逮捕：  
（一）可能实施新的犯罪的；  
（二）有危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的现实危险的；  
（三）可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（四）可能对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的；  
（五）企图自杀或者逃跑的。  
对于有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚的，或者有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处徒刑以上刑罚，曾经故意犯罪或者身份不明的，应当提请批准逮捕。  
公安机关在根据第一款的规定提请人民检察院审查批准逮捕时，应当对犯罪嫌疑人具有社会危险性说明理由。

**Article 133** For a criminal suspect for whom there is evidence to support the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to a punishment of imprisonment or above, if the imposition of release on bail pending investigation is not sufficient to prevent the following social dangers, approval of arrest shall be sought:  
(1) The suspect may commit a new crime;  
(2) There is a real danger of endangering national security, public security, or social order;  
(3) The suspect may destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with witness testimony, or collude with others;  
(4) The suspect may retaliate against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(5) The suspect attempts to commit suicide or escape.  
For a criminal suspect for whom there is evidence to support the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of 10 years or more, or for whom there is evidence to support the facts of a crime, who may be sentenced to a punishment of imprisonment or above, and who has previously intentionally committed a crime or whose identity is unknown, approval of arrest shall be sought.  
When the public security organ submits an application to the People's Procuratorate for examination and approval of arrest in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 1, it shall state the reasons why the criminal suspect presents social danger.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　有证据证明有犯罪事实，是指同时具备下列情形：  
（一）有证据证明发生了犯罪事实；  
（二）有证据证明该犯罪事实是犯罪嫌疑人实施的；  
（三）证明犯罪嫌疑人实施犯罪行为的证据已有查证属实的。  
前款规定的“犯罪事实”既可以是单一犯罪行为的事实，也可以是数个犯罪行为中任何一个犯罪行为的事实。

**Article 134** 'There is evidence to support the facts of a crime' means that the following circumstances are simultaneously present:  
(1) There is evidence to prove that the facts of a crime have occurred;  
(2) There is evidence to prove that the facts of the crime were committed by the criminal suspect;  
(3) The evidence proving that the criminal suspect committed the criminal act has been verified as true.  
The 'facts of a crime' provided in the preceding paragraph may be the facts of a single criminal act or the facts of any one of several criminal acts.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　被取保候审人违反取保候审规定，具有下列情形之一的，可以提请批准逮捕：  
（一）涉嫌故意实施新的犯罪行为的；  
（二）有危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的现实危险的；  
（三）实施毁灭、伪造证据或者干扰证人作证、串供行为，足以影响侦查工作正常进行的；  
（四）对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的；  
（五）企图自杀、逃跑，逃避侦查的；  
（六）未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县，情节严重的，或者两次以上未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县的；  
（七）经传讯无正当理由不到案，情节严重的，或者经两次以上传讯不到案的；  
（八）违反规定进入特定场所、从事特定活动或者与特定人员会见、通信两次以上的。

**Article 135** Where a person released on bail pending investigation violates the provisions on release on bail pending investigation and falls under any of the following circumstances, approval of arrest may be sought:  
(1) The person is suspected of intentionally committing a new criminal act;  
(2) There is a real danger of endangering national security, public security, or social order;  
(3) The person has committed acts of destroying or falsifying evidence or interfering with witness testimony or colluding with others, which are sufficient to affect the normal progress of the investigation;  
(4) The person has retaliated against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(5) The person attempts to commit suicide or escape to evade investigation;  
(6) The person, without approval, leaves the city or county where the person resides, with serious circumstances, or leaves without approval on two or more occasions;  
(7) The person, upon summons, fails to appear without justifiable reasons, with serious circumstances, or fails to appear after two or more summons;  
(8) The person violates provisions by entering a specific place, engaging in a specific activity, or meeting or corresponding with a specific person on two or more occasions.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　被监视居住人违反监视居住规定，具有下列情形之一的，可以提请批准逮捕：  
（一）涉嫌故意实施新的犯罪行为的；  
（二）实施毁灭、伪造证据或者干扰证人作证、串供行为，足以影响侦查工作正常进行的；  
（三）对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的；  
（四）企图自杀、逃跑，逃避侦查的；  
（五）未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所，情节严重的，或者两次以上未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所的；  
（六）未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信，情节严重的，或者两次以上未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信的；  
（七）经传讯无正当理由不到案，情节严重的，或者经两次以上传讯不到案的。

**Article 136** Where a person under residential surveillance violates the provisions on residential surveillance and falls under any of the following circumstances, approval of arrest may be sought:  
(1) The person is suspected of intentionally committing a new criminal act;  
(2) The person has committed acts of destroying or falsifying evidence or interfering with witness testimony or colluding with others, which are sufficient to affect the normal progress of the investigation;  
(3) The person has retaliated against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(4) The person attempts to commit suicide or escape to evade investigation;  
(5) The person, without approval, leaves the place where residential surveillance is being executed, with serious circumstances, or leaves without approval on two or more occasions;  
(6) The person, without approval, meets others or corresponds, with serious circumstances, or meets others or corresponds without approval on two or more occasions;  
(7) The person, upon summons, fails to appear without justifiable reasons, with serious circumstances, or fails to appear after two or more summons.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　需要提请批准逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作提请批准逮捕书，连同案卷材料、证据，一并移送同级人民检察院审查批准。  
犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪认罚的，应当记录在案，并在提请批准逮捕书中写明有关情况。

**Article 137** Where approval of arrest of a criminal suspect is required, approval shall be obtained from the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, a request for approval of arrest shall be prepared, and filed together with the case files and evidence with the People's Procuratorate at the same level for examination and approval.  
If the criminal suspect voluntarily admits guilt and accepts punishment, the matter shall be recorded in the case file, and the relevant information shall be stated in the request for approval of arrest.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　对于人民检察院不批准逮捕并通知补充侦查的，公安机关应当按照人民检察院的补充侦查提纲补充侦查。  
公安机关补充侦查完毕，认为符合逮捕条件的，应当重新提请批准逮捕。

**Article 138** Where the People's Procuratorate disapproves an arrest and notifies the public security organ to conduct supplementary investigation, the public security organ shall conduct supplementary investigation in accordance with the supplementary investigation outline of the People's Procuratorate.  
After the public security organ completes the supplementary investigation and deems that the conditions for arrest are met, it shall re-submit a request for approval of arrest.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　对于人民检察院不批准逮捕而未说明理由的，公安机关可以要求人民检察院说明理由。

**Article 139** Where the People's Procuratorate disapproves an arrest without stating the reasons, the public security organ may request the People's Procuratorate to state the reasons.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　对于人民检察院决定不批准逮捕的，公安机关在收到不批准逮捕决定书后，如果犯罪嫌疑人已被拘留的，应当立即释放，发给释放证明书，并在执行完毕后三日以内将执行回执送达作出不批准逮捕决定的人民检察院。

**Article 140** Where the People's Procuratorate decides not to approve an arrest, after receiving the written decision of disapproval of arrest, if the criminal suspect has been detained, the public security organ shall immediately release him, issue a release certificate, and, within three days after the execution is completed, serve the execution receipt on the People's Procuratorate that made the decision of disapproval of arrest.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　对人民检察院不批准逮捕的决定，认为有错误需要复议的，应当在收到不批准逮捕决定书后五日以内制作要求复议意见书，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，送交同级人民检察院复议。  
如果意见不被接受，认为需要复核的，应当在收到人民检察院的复议决定书后五日以内制作提请复核意见书，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，连同人民检察院的复议决定书，一并提请上一级人民检察院复核。

**Article 141** If the public security organ considers that a decision of the People's Procuratorate to disapprove an arrest is erroneous and a reconsideration is necessary, it shall, within five days after receiving the written decision of disapproval of arrest, prepare a written opinion requesting reconsideration, submit it to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and then deliver it to the same-level People's Procuratorate for reconsideration.  
If the opinion is not accepted and a review is deemed necessary, the public security organ shall, within five days after receiving the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate, prepare a written opinion requesting review, submit it to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and then submit it together with the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level for review.

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　接到人民检察院批准逮捕决定书后，应当由县级以上公安机关负责人签发逮捕证，立即执行，并在执行完毕后三日以内将执行回执送达作出批准逮捕决定的人民检察院。如果未能执行，也应当将回执送达人民检察院，并写明未能执行的原因。

**Article 142** After receiving the written decision of approval of arrest from the People's Procuratorate, the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level shall issue an arrest warrant and execute it immediately, and within three days after the execution is completed, serve the execution receipt on the People's Procuratorate that made the decision of approval of arrest. If the arrest cannot be executed, the receipt shall also be served on the People's Procuratorate, with the reasons for the failure to execute clearly stated.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　执行逮捕时，必须出示逮捕证，并责令被逮捕人在逮捕证上签名、捺指印，拒绝签名、捺指印的，侦查人员应当注明。逮捕后，应当立即将被逮捕人送看守所羁押。  
执行逮捕的侦查人员不得少于二人。

**Article 143** When executing an arrest, the arrest warrant must be presented, and the arrested person shall be ordered to sign and affix his fingerprint on the arrest warrant; if he refuses to sign or affix his fingerprint, the investigators shall note this fact. After the arrest, the arrested person shall be immediately delivered to a detention house for custody.  
The number of investigators executing the arrest shall be no less than two.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　对被逮捕的人，必须在逮捕后的二十四小时以内进行讯问。发现不应当逮捕的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作释放通知书，送看守所和原批准逮捕的人民检察院。看守所凭释放通知书立即释放被逮捕人，并发给释放证明书。

**Article 144** A person arrested must be interrogated within twenty-four hours after the arrest. If it is discovered that the arrest should not have been made, with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a release notice shall be prepared and served on the detention house and the People's Procuratorate that originally approved the arrest. The detention house shall immediately release the arrested person upon the release notice and issue a release certificate.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　对犯罪嫌疑人执行逮捕后，除无法通知的情形以外，应当在逮捕后二十四小时以内，制作逮捕通知书，通知被逮捕人的家属。逮捕通知书应当写明逮捕原因和羁押处所。  
本条规定的“无法通知”的情形适用[本规定第一百一十三条](#art-113)第二款的规定。  
无法通知的情形消除后，应当立即通知被逮捕人的家属。  
对于没有在二十四小时以内通知家属的，应当在逮捕通知书中注明原因。

**Article 145** After executing an arrest on a criminal suspect, except in circumstances where notification is impossible, an arrest notice shall be prepared within twenty-four hours after the arrest to notify the family members of the arrested person. The arrest notice shall specify the reasons for the arrest and the place of custody.  
The circumstances of "notification is impossible" as provided for in this Article shall be governed by the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 113 of these Provisions.  
After the circumstances making notification impossible are eliminated, the family members of the arrested person shall be notified immediately.  
If the family members are not notified within twenty-four hours, the reasons shall be stated in the arrest notice.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　人民法院、人民检察院决定逮捕犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的，由县级以上公安机关凭人民法院、人民检察院决定逮捕的法律文书制作逮捕证并立即执行。必要时，可以请人民法院、人民检察院协助执行。执行逮捕后，应当及时通知决定机关。  
公安机关未能抓获犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的，应当将执行情况和未能抓获的原因通知决定逮捕的人民检察院、人民法院。对于犯罪嫌疑人、被告人在逃的，在人民检察院、人民法院撤销逮捕决定之前，公安机关应当组织力量继续执行。

**Article 146** Where a People's Court or People's Procuratorate decides to arrest a criminal suspect or defendant, the public security organ at or above the county level shall, on the basis of the legal document of the People's Court or People's Procuratorate deciding the arrest, issue an arrest warrant and execute it immediately. When necessary, the public security organ may request the People's Court or People's Procuratorate to assist in the execution. After executing the arrest, the public security organ shall promptly notify the deciding organ.  
If the public security organ fails to apprehend the criminal suspect or defendant, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate or People's Court that decided the arrest of the execution status and the reasons for the failure to apprehend. Where the criminal suspect or defendant is at large, before the People's Procuratorate or People's Court rescinds the arrest decision, the public security organ shall organize forces to continue the execution.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　人民检察院在审查批准逮捕工作中发现公安机关的侦查活动存在违法情况，通知公安机关予以纠正的，公安机关应当调查核实，对于发现的违法情况应当及时纠正，并将纠正情况书面通知人民检察院。

**Article 147** Where, in the course of examining and approving arrests, the People's Procuratorate discovers that there are illegal activities in the investigative activities of the public security organ and notifies the public security organ to make corrections, the public security organ shall investigate and verify the situation, promptly correct any illegal activities discovered, and notify the People's Procuratorate in writing of the corrective actions taken.

#### 第六节　羁 押

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　对犯罪嫌疑人逮捕后的侦查羁押期限不得超过二个月。案情复杂、期限届满不能侦查终结的案件，应当制作提请批准延长侦查羁押期限意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，在期限届满七日前送请同级人民检察院转报上一级人民检察院批准延长一个月。

**Article 148** The period of investigative custody after the arrest of a criminal suspect shall not exceed two months. For cases that are complex and cannot be concluded upon expiration of the period, a written opinion requesting approval for extension of the investigative custody period shall be prepared, submitted to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and then, seven days before the expiration of the period, sent to the same-level People's Procuratorate for transmission to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level for approval of an extension of one month.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　下列案件在[本规定第一百四十八条](#art-148)规定的期限届满不能侦查终结的，应当制作提请批准延长侦查羁押期限意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，在期限届满七日前送请同级人民检察院层报省、自治区、直辖市人民检察院批准，延长二个月：  
（一）交通十分不便的边远地区的重大复杂案件；  
（二）重大的犯罪集团案件；  
（三）流窜作案的重大复杂案件；  
（四）犯罪涉及面广，取证困难的重大复杂案件。

**Article 149** For the following types of cases that cannot be concluded upon expiration of the period specified in Article 148 of these Provisions, a written opinion requesting approval for extension of the investigative custody period shall be prepared, submitted to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and then, seven days before the expiration of the period, sent to the same-level People's Procuratorate for transmission level by level to the People's Procuratorate of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government for approval of an extension of two months:  
(1) Major and complex cases in remote areas with extremely inconvenient transportation;  
(2) Major criminal group cases;  
(3) Major and complex cases involving itinerant criminal activities;  
(4) Major and complex cases involving a wide range of crimes and difficulty in obtaining evidence.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　对犯罪嫌疑人可能判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚，依照[本规定第一百四十九条](#art-149)规定的延长期限届满，仍不能侦查终结的，应当制作提请批准延长侦查羁押期限意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，在期限届满七日前送请同级人民检察院层报省、自治区、直辖市人民检察院批准，再延长二个月。

**Article 150** Where a criminal suspect may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of ten years or more, and upon expiration of the extension period provided for in Article 149 of these Provisions, the investigation still cannot be concluded, a written opinion requesting approval for extension of the investigative custody period shall be prepared, submitted to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, and then, seven days before the expiration of the period, sent to the same-level People's Procuratorate for transmission level by level to the People's Procuratorate of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government for approval of an additional extension of two months.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　在侦查期间，发现犯罪嫌疑人另有重要罪行的，应当自发现之日起五日以内报县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，重新计算侦查羁押期限，制作变更羁押期限通知书，送达看守所，并报批准逮捕的人民检察院备案。  
前款规定的“另有重要罪行”，是指与逮捕时的罪行不同种的重大犯罪以及同种犯罪并将影响罪名认定、量刑档次的重大犯罪。

**Article 151** Where, during the investigation, it is discovered that a criminal suspect has committed another important crime, within five days from the date of discovery, the matter shall be reported to the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level for approval, after which the period of investigative custody shall be recalculated, a notice of change of custody period shall be prepared and served on the detention house, and the matter shall be reported to the People's Procuratorate that approved the arrest for record.  
The "another important crime" as provided for in the preceding paragraph refers to a major crime of a different type from the crime for which the arrest was made, or a major crime of the same type that will affect the determination of the charge or the sentencing level.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　犯罪嫌疑人不讲真实姓名、住址，身份不明的，应当对其身份进行调查。经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，侦查羁押期限自查清其身份之日起计算，但不得停止对其犯罪行为的侦查取证。  
对于犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分，确实无法查明其身份的，按其自报的姓名移送人民检察院审查起诉。

**Article 152** Where a criminal suspect refuses to disclose his true name and address and his identity is unknown, an investigation into his identity shall be conducted. With the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, the period of investigative custody shall be calculated from the date on which his identity is ascertained, but the investigation and evidence collection for his criminal acts shall not be suspended.  
Where the criminal facts are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and his identity cannot be ascertained despite all efforts, the case shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution under the name he himself provides.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　看守所应当凭公安机关签发的拘留证、逮捕证收押被拘留、逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人。犯罪嫌疑人、被告人被送至看守所羁押时，看守所应当在拘留证、逮捕证上注明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人到达看守所的时间。  
查获被通缉、脱逃的犯罪嫌疑人以及执行追捕、押解任务需要临时寄押的，应当持通缉令或者其他有关法律文书并经寄押地县级以上公安机关负责人批准，送看守所寄押。  
临时寄押的犯罪嫌疑人出所时，看守所应当出具羁押该犯罪嫌疑人的证明，载明该犯罪嫌疑人基本情况、羁押原因、入所和出所时间。

**Article 153** A detention house shall, on the basis of the detention warrant or arrest warrant issued by the public security organ, take into custody criminal suspects or defendants who are detained or arrested. When a criminal suspect or defendant is delivered to the detention house for custody, the detention house shall note on the detention warrant or arrest warrant the time of the criminal suspect's or defendant's arrival at the detention house.  
Where a wanted or escaped criminal suspect is apprehended or temporary custody is required during a pursuit or escort mission, a wanted circular or other relevant legal document shall be presented, and with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level in the place of temporary custody, the suspect shall be delivered to a detention house for temporary custody.  
When a temporarily held criminal suspect leaves the detention house, the detention house shall issue a certificate of custody for that criminal suspect, specifying the basic information of the criminal suspect, the reason for custody, and the time of entry and exit.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　看守所收押犯罪嫌疑人、被告人和罪犯，应当进行健康和体表检查，并予以记录。

**Article 154** When taking into custody criminal suspects, defendants, and convicts, a detention house shall conduct health and body surface examinations and record the results.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　看守所收押犯罪嫌疑人、被告人和罪犯，应当对其人身和携带的物品进行安全检查。发现违禁物品、犯罪证据和可疑物品，应当制作笔录，由被羁押人签名、捺指印后，送办案机关处理。  
对女性的人身检查，应当由女工作人员进行。

**Article 155** When taking into custody criminal suspects, defendants, and convicts, a detention house shall conduct a security check on their persons and the items they carry. If prohibited articles, criminal evidence, or suspicious items are found, a record shall be made, signed and fingerprinted by the person in custody, and then submitted to the case-handling authority for disposal.  
Body searches on females shall be conducted by female staff members.

#### 第七节　其他规定

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　继续盘问期间发现需要对犯罪嫌疑人拘留、逮捕、取保候审或者监视居住的，应当立即办理法律手续。

**Article 156** If, during continued questioning, it is discovered that a criminal suspect needs to be detained, arrested, released on bail pending investigation or trial, or placed under residential surveillance, the legal formalities shall be handled immediately.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　对犯罪嫌疑人执行拘传、拘留、逮捕、押解过程中，应当依法使用约束性警械。遇有暴力性对抗或者暴力犯罪行为，可以依法使用制服性警械或者武器。

**Article 157** In the course of executing a summons by force, detention, arrest, or escort of a criminal suspect, restraining police instruments shall be used according to law. In the event of violent resistance or violent criminal acts, subduing police instruments or weapons may be used according to law.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　公安机关发现对犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施不当的，应当及时撤销或者变更。犯罪嫌疑人在押的，应当及时释放。公安机关释放被逮捕的人或者变更逮捕措施的，应当通知批准逮捕的人民检察院。

**Article 158** If a public security organ discovers that the compulsory measures taken against a criminal suspect are inappropriate, it shall promptly revoke or change them. If the criminal suspect is in custody, he/she shall be promptly released. Where a public security organ releases an arrested person or changes the arrest measure, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate that approved the arrest.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　犯罪嫌疑人被逮捕后，人民检察院经审查认为不需要继续羁押，建议予以释放或者变更强制措施的，公安机关应当予以调查核实。认为不需要继续羁押的，应当予以释放或者变更强制措施；认为需要继续羁押的，应当说明理由。  
公安机关应当在十日以内将处理情况通知人民检察院。

**Article 159** After a criminal suspect is arrested, if the People's Procuratorate, upon review, considers that continued custody is unnecessary and recommends release or a change of compulsory measures, the public security organ shall investigate and verify the matter. If it considers that continued custody is unnecessary, it shall release the suspect or change the compulsory measure; if it considers that continued custody is necessary, it shall state the reasons.  
The public security organ shall notify the People's Procuratorate of the handling results within ten days.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人有权申请变更强制措施。公安机关应当在收到申请后三日以内作出决定；不同意变更强制措施的，应当告知申请人，并说明理由。

**Article 160** The criminal suspect, his/her legal representative, close relative, or defender shall have the right to apply for a change of compulsory measures. The public security organ shall make a decision within three days after receiving the application; if it disagrees to change the compulsory measure, it shall inform the applicant and state the reasons.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　公安机关对被采取强制措施法定期限届满的犯罪嫌疑人，应当予以释放，解除取保候审、监视居住或者依法变更强制措施。  
犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人对于公安机关采取强制措施法定期限届满的，有权要求公安机关解除强制措施。公安机关应当进行审查，对于情况属实的，应当立即解除或者变更强制措施。  
对于犯罪嫌疑人、被告人羁押期限即将届满的，看守所应当立即通知办案机关。

**Article 161** Where the statutory time limit for compulsory measures taken against a criminal suspect expires, the public security organ shall release him/her, lift the release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance, or change the compulsory measure according to law.  
The criminal suspect, his/her legal representative, close relative, or defender, where the statutory time limit for compulsory measures taken by the public security organ expires, shall have the right to demand that the public security organ lift the compulsory measure. The public security organ shall conduct a review; if the situation is verified, it shall immediately lift or change the compulsory measure.  
Where the custody period of a criminal suspect or defendant is about to expire, the detention house shall immediately notify the case-handling organ.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　取保候审变更为监视居住的，取保候审、监视居住变更为拘留、逮捕的，对原强制措施不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 162** Where release on bail pending investigation is changed to residential surveillance, or where release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance is changed to detention or arrest, the legal formalities for cancellation of the original compulsory measure shall no longer be handled.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　案件在取保候审、监视居住期间移送审查起诉后，人民检察院决定重新取保候审、监视居住或者变更强制措施的，对原强制措施不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 163** Where, after a case is transferred for review and prosecution during the period of release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance, the People's Procuratorate decides to re-impose release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, or to change the compulsory measure, the legal formalities for cancellation of the original compulsory measure shall no longer be handled.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　公安机关依法对县级以上各级人民代表大会代表拘传、取保候审、监视居住、拘留或者提请批准逮捕的，应当书面报请该代表所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会许可。

**Article 164** Where a public security organ, according to law, imposes a summons for interrogation, release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, or detention on a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level, or submits a request for approval of arrest, it shall, in writing, report to and seek permission from the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　公安机关对现行犯拘留的时候，发现其是县级以上人民代表大会代表的，应当立即向其所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会报告。  
公安机关在依法执行拘传、取保候审、监视居住、拘留或者逮捕中，发现被执行人是县级以上人民代表大会代表的，应当暂缓执行，并报告决定或者批准机关。如果在执行后发现被执行人是县级以上人民代表大会代表的，应当立即解除，并报告决定或者批准机关。

**Article 165** Where, at the time of detaining an active offender, a public security organ discovers that the person is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level, it shall immediately report to the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs.  
Where, in the course of executing a summons for interrogation, release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, detention, or arrest according to law, a public security organ discovers that the person subject to execution is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level, it shall suspend the execution and report to the decision-making or approving organ. If it discovers after execution that the person subject to execution is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level, it shall immediately lift the measure and report to the decision-making or approving organ.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　公安机关依法对乡、民族乡、镇的人民代表大会代表拘传、取保候审、监视居住、拘留或者执行逮捕的，应当在执行后立即报告其所属的人民代表大会。

**Article 166** Where a public security organ, according to law, imposes a summons for interrogation, release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, detention, or executes arrest on a deputy to the people's congress of a township, ethnic township, or town, it shall, immediately after execution, report to the people's congress to which the deputy belongs.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　公安机关依法对政治协商委员会委员拘传、取保候审、监视居住的，应当将有关情况通报给该委员所属的政协组织。

**Article 167** Where a public security organ, according to law, imposes a summons for interrogation, release on bail pending investigation, or residential surveillance on a member of a political consultative conference, it shall notify the relevant information to the CPPCC organization to which the member belongs.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　公安机关依法对政治协商委员会委员执行拘留、逮捕前，应当向该委员所属的政协组织通报情况；情况紧急的，可在执行的同时或者执行以后及时通报。

**Article 168** Before a public security organ executes detention or arrest on a member of a political consultative conference according to law, it shall notify the situation to the CPPCC organization to which the member belongs; in an emergency, it may notify at the time of execution or promptly after execution.

### 第七章　立案、撤案

#### 第一节　受 案

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　公安机关对于公民扭送、报案、控告、举报或者犯罪嫌疑人自动投案的，都应当立即接受，问明情况，并制作笔录，经核对无误后，由扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人、投案人签名、捺指印。必要时，应当对接受过程录音录像。

**Article 169** A public security organ shall, in every case where a citizen makes a citizen's arrest, reports a case, lodges a complaint or information, or a criminal suspect voluntarily surrenders, immediately accept the matter, inquire about the circumstances, and make a record. After verification to ensure accuracy, the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, informant, or the person surrendering shall sign and affix a fingerprint. Where necessary, audio or video recording of the acceptance process shall be made.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　公安机关对扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人、投案人提供的有关证据材料等应当登记，制作接受证据材料清单，由扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人、投案人签名，并妥善保管。必要时，应当拍照或者录音录像。

**Article 170** A public security organ shall register the relevant evidentiary materials provided by the person making a citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, informant, or the person surrendering, prepare an inventory of the received evidentiary materials, have them signed by the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, informant, or the person surrendering, and keep them properly. Where necessary, photographs or audio/video recordings shall be made.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　公安机关接受案件时，应当制作受案登记表和受案回执，并将受案回执交扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人。扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人无法取得联系或者拒绝接受回执的，应当在回执中注明。

**Article 171** When accepting a case, a public security organ shall prepare a case acceptance registration form and a case acceptance receipt, and shall hand over the case acceptance receipt to the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant. If the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant cannot be contacted or refuses to accept the receipt, a notation shall be made in the receipt.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　公安机关接受控告、举报的工作人员，应当向控告人、举报人说明诬告应负的法律责任。但是，只要不是捏造事实、伪造证据，即使控告、举报的事实有出入，甚至是错告的，也要和诬告严格加以区别。

**Article 172** The staff members of a public security organ accepting a complaint or information shall explain to the complainant or informant the legal liability for a false accusation. However, as long as there is no fabrication of facts or forgery of evidence, even if the facts of the complaint or information are inaccurate, or even if it is a mistaken accusation, it shall be strictly distinguished from a false accusation.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　公安机关应当保障扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人及其近亲属的安全。  
扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人如果不愿意公开自己的身份，应当为其保守秘密，并在材料中注明。

**Article 173** A public security organ shall ensure the safety of the person making a citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, informant, and their close relatives.  
If the person making a citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant does not wish to disclose his/her identity, the public security organ shall keep it confidential and indicate this in the materials.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　对接受的案件，或者发现的犯罪线索，公安机关应当迅速进行审查。发现案件事实或者线索不明的，必要时，经办案部门负责人批准，可以进行调查核实。  
调查核实过程中，公安机关可以依照有关法律和规定采取询问、查询、勘验、鉴定和调取证据材料等不限制被调查对象人身、财产权利的措施。但是，不得对被调查对象采取强制措施，不得查封、扣押、冻结被调查对象的财产，不得采取技术侦查措施。

**Article 174** A public security organ shall promptly review an accepted case or a discovered criminal clue. If the case facts or clues are unclear, where necessary, upon approval by the head of the case-handling department, investigation and verification may be conducted.  
During the process of investigation and verification, a public security organ may, in accordance with relevant laws and regulations, take measures such as inquiries, searches, inspections, expert evaluations, and collection of evidentiary materials that do not restrict the personal or property rights of the investigated person. However, no compulsory measures shall be taken against the investigated person, no sealing, seizure, or freezing of the investigated person's property shall be conducted, and no technical investigation measures shall be taken.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　经过审查，认为有犯罪事实，但不属于自己管辖的案件，应当立即报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作移送案件通知书，在二十四小时以内移送有管辖权的机关处理，并告知扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人。对于不属于自己管辖而又必须采取紧急措施的，应当先采取紧急措施，然后办理手续，移送主管机关。  
对不属于公安机关职责范围的事项，在接报案时能够当场判断的，应当立即口头告知扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人向其他主管机关报案。  
对于重复报案、案件正在办理或者已经办结的，应当向扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人作出解释，不再登记，但有新的事实或者证据的除外。

**Article 175** After review, if it is determined that a crime has occurred but the case is not within its own jurisdiction, the public security organ shall immediately report to and obtain approval from the head of the public security organ at or above the county level, prepare a case transfer notice, and transfer the case to the organ with jurisdiction within twenty-four hours for handling, and inform the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant. If the case is not within its own jurisdiction but emergency measures must be taken, it shall first take the emergency measures, then go through the formalities and transfer the case to the competent authority.  
For matters that are not within the functions of a public security organ, if it can be determined on the spot at the time of accepting the report, it shall immediately orally inform the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant to report to another competent authority.  
For repeated reports, or where the case is being handled or has already been concluded, an explanation shall be given to the person making the citizen's arrest, the reporter, complainant, or informant, and no registration shall be made, except where there are new facts or evidence.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　经过审查，对告诉才处理的案件，公安机关应当告知当事人向人民法院起诉。  
对被害人有证据证明的轻微刑事案件，公安机关应当告知被害人可以向人民法院起诉；被害人要求公安机关处理的，公安机关应当依法受理。  
人民法院审理自诉案件，依法调取公安机关已经收集的案件材料和有关证据的，公安机关应当及时移交。

**Article 176** After review, for cases to be handled only upon complaint, the public security organ shall inform the party to bring a lawsuit to the People's Court.  
For minor criminal cases for which the victim has evidence to prove, the public security organ shall inform the victim that he/she may bring a lawsuit to the People's Court; if the victim requests the public security organ to handle the case, the public security organ shall accept it according to law.  
Where the People's Court, when hearing a private prosecution case, lawfully requests the case materials and relevant evidence already collected by the public security organ, the public security organ shall promptly transfer them.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　经过审查，对于不够刑事处罚需要给予行政处理的，依法予以处理或者移送有关部门。

**Article 177** After review, for cases that do not warrant criminal punishment but require administrative handling, the public security organ shall handle them according to law or transfer them to the relevant department.

#### 第二节　立 案

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　公安机关接受案件后，经审查，认为有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任，且属于自己管辖的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，予以立案；认为没有犯罪事实，或者犯罪事实显著轻微不需要追究刑事责任，或者具有其他依法不追究刑事责任情形的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，不予立案。  
对有控告人的案件，决定不予立案的，公安机关应当制作不予立案通知书，并在三日以内送达控告人。  
决定不予立案后又发现新的事实或者证据，或者发现原认定事实错误，需要追究刑事责任的，应当及时立案处理。

**Article 178** After accepting a case, a public security organ shall, upon review and if it finds that a crime has occurred and criminal liability shall be investigated and that the case falls within its own jurisdiction, file the case upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level; if it finds that no crime has occurred, or that the crime is obviously minor and does not warrant investigation of criminal liability, or if there are other circumstances under which criminal liability shall not be investigated according to law, it shall not file the case upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level.  
For a case involving a complainant, if a decision is made not to file the case, the public security organ shall prepare a notice of decision not to file the case and serve it to the complainant within three days.  
If, after a decision is made not to file the case, new facts or evidence are discovered, or it is found that the original determination of facts was erroneous and criminal liability shall be investigated, the public security organ shall promptly file the case and handle it.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　控告人对不予立案决定不服的，可以在收到不予立案通知书后七日以内向作出决定的公安机关申请复议；公安机关应当在收到复议申请后三十日以内作出决定，并将决定书送达控告人。  
控告人对不予立案的复议决定不服的，可以在收到复议决定书后七日以内向上一级公安机关申请复核；上一级公安机关应当在收到复核申请后三十日以内作出决定。对上级公安机关撤销不予立案决定的，下级公安机关应当执行。  
案情重大、复杂的，公安机关可以延长复议、复核时限，但是延长时限不得超过三十日，并书面告知申请人。

**Article 179** If a complainant disagrees with a decision not to file the case, they may apply for reconsideration to the public security organ that made the decision within seven days after receiving the notice of decision not to file the case; the public security organ shall make a decision within thirty days after receiving the application for reconsideration and serve the decision document to the complainant.  
If a complainant disagrees with the reconsideration decision not to file the case, they may apply for review to the public security organ at the next higher level within seven days after receiving the reconsideration decision document; the public security organ at the next higher level shall make a decision within thirty days after receiving the application for review. If the higher-level public security organ revokes the decision not to file the case, the lower-level public security organ shall implement it.  
If the case is major and complex, the public security organ may extend the time limit for reconsideration or review, but the extended time limit shall not exceed thirty days, and shall notify the applicant in writing.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　对行政执法机关移送的案件，公安机关应当自接受案件之日起三日以内进行审查，认为有犯罪事实，需要追究刑事责任，依法决定立案的，应当书面通知移送案件的行政执法机关；认为没有犯罪事实，或者犯罪事实显著轻微，不需要追究刑事责任，依法不予立案的，应当说明理由，并将不予立案通知书送达移送案件的行政执法机关，相应退回案件材料。  
公安机关认为行政执法机关移送的案件材料不全的，应当在接受案件后二十四小时以内通知移送案件的行政执法机关在三日以内补正，但不得以材料不全为由不接受移送案件。  
公安机关认为行政执法机关移送的案件不属于公安机关职责范围的，应当书面通知移送案件的行政执法机关向其他主管机关移送案件，并说明理由。

**Article 180** For a case transferred by an administrative enforcement agency, the public security organ shall conduct a review within three days from the date of accepting the case. If it finds that a crime has occurred and criminal liability shall be investigated and decides to file the case according to law, it shall notify the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case in writing; if it finds that no crime has occurred, or the crime is obviously minor and does not warrant investigation of criminal liability, and decides not to file the case according to law, it shall state the reasons and serve the notice of decision not to file the case to the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case, and return the case materials accordingly.  
If the public security organ considers the case materials transferred by the administrative enforcement agency to be incomplete, it shall, within twenty-four hours after accepting the case, notify the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case to supplement or correct the materials within three days; however, it shall not refuse to accept the transferred case on the ground of incomplete materials.  
If the public security organ considers that the case transferred by the administrative enforcement agency does not fall within the scope of its duties, it shall notify the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case in writing to transfer the case to another competent authority and state the reasons.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　移送案件的行政执法机关对不予立案决定不服的，可以在收到不予立案通知书后三日以内向作出决定的公安机关申请复议；公安机关应当在收到行政执法机关的复议申请后三日以内作出决定，并书面通知移送案件的行政执法机关。

**Article 181** If an administrative enforcement agency that transferred a case disagrees with a decision not to file the case, it may apply for reconsideration to the public security organ that made the decision within three days after receiving the notice of decision not to file the case; the public security organ shall make a decision within three days after receiving the reconsideration application from the administrative enforcement agency and notify the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case in writing.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　对人民检察院要求说明不立案理由的案件，公安机关应当在收到通知书后七日以内，对不立案的情况、依据和理由作出书面说明，回复人民检察院。公安机关作出立案决定的，应当将立案决定书复印件送达人民检察院。  
人民检察院通知公安机关立案的，公安机关应当在收到通知书后十五日以内立案，并将立案决定书复印件送达人民检察院。

**Article 182** For a case in which the People's Procuratorate requests an explanation of the reasons for not filing the case, the public security organ shall, within seven days after receiving the notice, provide a written explanation of the circumstances, basis, and reasons for not filing the case and reply to the People's Procuratorate. If the public security organ makes a decision to file the case, it shall serve a copy of the decision to file the case to the People's Procuratorate.  
If the People's Procuratorate notifies the public security organ to file the case, the public security organ shall file the case within fifteen days after receiving the notice and serve a copy of the decision to file the case to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　人民检察院认为公安机关不应当立案而立案，提出纠正意见的，公安机关应当进行调查核实，并将有关情况回复人民检察院。

**Article 183** If the People's Procuratorate considers that a public security organ should not have filed a case but did so, and raises a corrective opinion, the public security organ shall conduct an investigation and verification and reply to the People's Procuratorate regarding the relevant circumstances.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　经立案侦查，认为有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任，但不属于自己管辖或者需要由其他公安机关并案侦查的案件，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作移送案件通知书，移送有管辖权的机关或者并案侦查的公安机关，并在移送案件后三日以内书面通知扭送人、报案人、控告人、举报人或者移送案件的行政执法机关；犯罪嫌疑人已经到案的，应当依照本规定的有关规定通知其家属。

**Article 184** For a case that, after case filing and investigation, is found to involve a crime requiring investigation of criminal liability but does not fall within its own jurisdiction or needs to be investigated jointly by another public security organ, the public security organ shall, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, prepare a notice of case transfer, transfer the case to the organ with jurisdiction or the public security organ conducting joint investigation, and within three days after the transfer, notify in writing the person who seized and delivered the suspect, the person who reported the case, the complainant, the informant, or the administrative enforcement agency that transferred the case; if the criminal suspect has already been brought to the scene, the public security organ shall notify their family members in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Provisions.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　案件变更管辖或者移送其他公安机关并案侦查时，与案件有关的法律文书、证据、财物及其孳息等应当随案移交。  
移交时，由接收人、移交人当面查点清楚，并在交接单据上共同签名。

**Article 185** When the jurisdiction of a case is changed or the case is transferred to another public security organ for joint investigation, legal documents, evidence, property and its fruits, etc., related to the case shall be transferred along with the case.  
During the transfer, the receiver and the transferor shall check and count the items face to face and jointly sign the transfer receipt.

#### 第三节　撤 案

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　经过侦查，发现具有下列情形之一的，应当撤销案件：  
（一）没有犯罪事实的；  
（二）情节显著轻微、危害不大，不认为是犯罪的；  
（三）犯罪已过追诉时效期限的；  
（四）经特赦令免除刑罚的；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人死亡的；  
（六）其他依法不追究刑事责任的。  
对于经过侦查，发现有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任，但不是被立案侦查的犯罪嫌疑人实施的，或者共同犯罪案件中部分犯罪嫌疑人不够刑事处罚的，应当对有关犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查，并对该案件继续侦查。

**Article 186** After investigation, if any of the following circumstances is found, the case shall be dismissed:  
(1) no crime has occurred;  
(2) the circumstances are obviously minor and the harm is insignificant, so it is not deemed a crime;  
(3) the statute of limitations for prosecution has expired;  
(4) the punishment has been exempted by a special amnesty decree;  
(5) the criminal suspect has died;  
(6) other circumstances under which criminal liability shall not be investigated according to law.  
If, after investigation, it is found that a crime has occurred and criminal liability shall be investigated, but the crime was not committed by the criminal suspect under investigation, or in a joint crime, some criminal suspects are not subject to criminal punishment, the investigation of the relevant criminal suspect(s) shall be terminated, and the investigation of the case shall continue.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　需要撤销案件或者对犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查的，办案部门应当制作撤销案件或者终止侦查报告书，报县级以上公安机关负责人批准。  
公安机关决定撤销案件或者对犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查时，原犯罪嫌疑人在押的，应当立即释放，发给释放证明书。原犯罪嫌疑人被逮捕的，应当通知原批准逮捕的人民检察院。对原犯罪嫌疑人采取其他强制措施的，应当立即解除强制措施；需要行政处理的，依法予以处理或者移交有关部门。  
对查封、扣押的财物及其孳息、文件，或者冻结的财产，除按照法律和有关规定另行处理的以外，应当解除查封、扣押、冻结，并及时返还或者通知当事人。
