**Article 187** If a case needs to be dismissed or the investigation of a criminal suspect needs to be terminated, the case-handling department shall prepare a report on dismissal of the case or termination of investigation and submit it for approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level.  
When a public security organ decides to dismiss a case or terminate the investigation of a criminal suspect, if the former criminal suspect is in custody, it shall immediately release the person and issue a release certificate. If the former criminal suspect was arrested, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate that approved the arrest. If other compulsory measures were taken against the former criminal suspect, those compulsory measures shall be immediately lifted; if administrative handling is necessary, the public security organ shall handle it according to law or transfer the matter to the relevant department.  
With respect to sealed-up, seized property and its fruits, documents, or frozen assets, unless otherwise handled in accordance with laws and relevant provisions, the sealing-up, seizure, and freezing shall be lifted, and the property shall be promptly returned or the parties notified.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　犯罪嫌疑人自愿如实供述涉嫌犯罪的事实，有重大立功或者案件涉及国家重大利益，需要撤销案件的，应当层报公安部，由公安部商请最高人民检察院核准后撤销案件。报请撤销案件的公安机关应当同时将相关情况通报同级人民检察院。  
公安机关根据前款规定撤销案件的，应当对查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息作出处理。

**Article 188** If a criminal suspect voluntarily and truthfully confesses the suspected criminal facts, has made major meritorious service, or the case involves major national interests, and the case needs to be dismissed, the public security organ shall report the matter level by level to the Ministry of Public Security, which shall consult the Supreme People's Procuratorate for approval before dismissing the case. The public security organ that reports the case for dismissal shall simultaneously notify the People's Procuratorate at the corresponding level of the relevant circumstances.  
If a public security organ dismisses a case in accordance with the preceding paragraph, it shall dispose of the sealed-up, seized, and frozen property and its fruits.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　公安机关作出撤销案件决定后，应当在三日以内告知原犯罪嫌疑人、被害人或者其近亲属、法定代理人以及案件移送机关。  
公安机关作出终止侦查决定后，应当在三日以内告知原犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 189** After a public security organ makes a decision to dismiss a case, it shall, within three days, notify the former criminal suspect, the victim or their close relatives, legal representatives, and the case transfer authority.  
After a public security organ makes a decision to terminate investigation, it shall, within three days, notify the former criminal suspect.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　公安机关撤销案件以后又发现新的事实或者证据，或者发现原认定事实错误，认为有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任的，应当重新立案侦查。  
对犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查后又发现新的事实或者证据，或者发现原认定事实错误，需要对其追究刑事责任的，应当继续侦查。

**Article 190** If, after a public security organ dismisses a case, new facts or evidence are discovered, or it is found that the original determination of facts was erroneous and it is determined that a crime has occurred and criminal liability shall be investigated, the public security organ shall file the case anew and conduct investigation.  
If, after the investigation of a criminal suspect is terminated, new facts or evidence are discovered, or it is found that the original determination of facts was erroneous and the criminal suspect needs to be investigated for criminal liability, the investigation shall be continued.

### 第八章　侦 查

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　公安机关对已经立案的刑事案件，应当及时进行侦查，全面、客观地收集、调取犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪、罪轻或者罪重的证据材料。

**Article 191** A public security organ shall promptly conduct investigation into a criminal case that has been filed, and shall comprehensively and objectively collect and obtain evidentiary materials regarding the criminal suspect's guilt or innocence, and regarding whether the crime is minor or serious.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　公安机关经过侦查，对有证据证明有犯罪事实的案件，应当进行预审，对收集、调取的证据材料的真实性、合法性、关联性及证明力予以审查、核实。

**Article 192** After investigation, for a case where evidence proves that a crime has occurred, the public security organ shall conduct a preliminary hearing to examine and verify the authenticity, legality, relevance, and probative force of the collected and obtained evidentiary materials.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　公安机关侦查犯罪，应当严格依照法律规定的条件和程序采取强制措施和侦查措施，严禁在没有证据的情况下，仅凭怀疑就对犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施和侦查措施。

**Article 193** When investigating a crime, the public security organ shall strictly follow the conditions and procedures provided by law in taking compulsory measures and investigation measures, and it is strictly forbidden to take compulsory measures or investigation measures against a criminal suspect based solely on suspicion without evidence.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　公安机关开展勘验、检查、搜查、辨认、查封、扣押等侦查活动，应当邀请有关公民作为见证人。  
下列人员不得担任侦查活动的见证人：  
（一）生理上、精神上有缺陷或者年幼，不具有相应辨别能力或者不能正确表达的人；  
（二）与案件有利害关系，可能影响案件公正处理的人；  
（三）公安机关的工作人员或者其聘用的人员。  
确因客观原因无法由符合条件的人员担任见证人的，应当对有关侦查活动进行全程录音录像，并在笔录中注明有关情况。

**Article 194** When carrying out investigation activities such as inspection, examination, search, identification, sealing-up, and seizure, the public security organ shall invite relevant citizens to serve as witnesses.  
The following persons shall not serve as witnesses for investigation activities:  
(1) persons who are physically or mentally disabled or are minors, and lack the ability to discern or cannot correctly express themselves;  
(2) persons who have an interest in the case and might affect the impartial handling of the case;  
(3) staff members of the public security organ or persons employed by it.  
If, due to objective reasons, it is truly impossible to have a qualified person serve as a witness, the entire investigation activity shall be recorded by audio and video, and the relevant circumstances shall be noted in the record.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　公安机关侦查犯罪，涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的，应当保密。

**Article 195** When investigating a crime involving state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, the public security organ shall maintain confidentiality.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　当事人和辩护人、诉讼代理人、利害关系人对于公安机关及其侦查人员有下列行为之一的，有权向该机关申诉或者控告：  
（一）采取强制措施法定期限届满，不予以释放、解除或者变更的；  
（二）应当退还取保候审保证金不退还的；  
（三）对与案件无关的财物采取查封、扣押、冻结措施的；  
（四）应当解除查封、扣押、冻结不解除的；  
（五）贪污、挪用、私分、调换、违反规定使用查封、扣押、冻结的财物的。  
受理申诉或者控告的公安机关应当及时进行调查核实，并在收到申诉、控告之日起三十日以内作出处理决定，书面回复申诉人、控告人。发现公安机关及其侦查人员有上述行为之一的，应当立即纠正。

**Article 196** If a party, defender, litigation agent, or interested person has any of the following conduct by the public security organ or its investigators, they have the right to file a complaint or accusation with that organ:  
(1) failing to release, lift, or modify compulsory measures after the statutory time limit has expired;  
(2) failing to return the deposit for release on bail pending investigation or trial that should be returned;  
(3) taking measures to seal up, seize, or freeze property unrelated to the case;  
(4) failing to lift sealing-up, seizure, or freezing that should be lifted;  
(5) embezzling, misappropriating, privately dividing, swapping, or using in violation of regulations the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property.  
The public security organ that receives the complaint or accusation shall promptly conduct an investigation and verification, and make a handling decision within thirty days from the date of receiving the complaint or accusation, and reply to the complainant or accuser in writing. If it finds that the public security organ or its investigators have any of the above conduct, it shall immediately correct it.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　上级公安机关发现下级公安机关存在[本规定第一百九十六条](#art-196)第一款规定的违法行为或者对申诉、控告事项不按照规定处理的，应当责令下级公安机关限期纠正，下级公安机关应当立即执行。必要时，上级公安机关可以就申诉、控告事项直接作出处理决定。

**Article 197** If a public security organ at a higher level discovers that a public security organ at a lower level has the illegal conduct specified in Paragraph 1 of Article 196 of these Provisions or fails to handle complaints or accusations in accordance with the provisions, it shall order the lower-level public security organ to correct within a time limit, and the lower-level public security organ shall immediately implement the order. If necessary, the higher-level public security organ may directly make a handling decision on the complaint or accusation.

#### 第二节　讯问犯罪嫌疑人

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　讯问犯罪嫌疑人，除下列情形以外，应当在公安机关执法办案场所的讯问室进行：  
（一）紧急情况下在现场进行讯问的；  
（二）对有严重伤病或者残疾、行动不便的，以及正在怀孕的犯罪嫌疑人，在其住处或者就诊的医疗机构进行讯问的。  
对于已送交看守所羁押的犯罪嫌疑人，应当在看守所讯问室进行讯问。  
对于正在被执行行政拘留、强制隔离戒毒的人员以及正在监狱服刑的罪犯，可以在其执行场所进行讯问。  
对于不需要拘留、逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，经办案部门负责人批准，可以传唤到犯罪嫌疑人所在市、县公安机关执法办案场所或者到他的住处进行讯问。

**Article 198** When interrogating a criminal suspect, the interrogation shall be conducted in the interrogation room of the public security organ’s case handling premises, except under the following circumstances:  
(1) Interrogation conducted at the scene under emergency circumstances;  
(2) Interrogation of a criminal suspect who is seriously ill or disabled, has difficulty moving, or is pregnant, conducted at his or her residence or the medical institution where he or she is receiving treatment.  
For a criminal suspect who has been delivered to a detention house for custody, interrogation shall be conducted in the interrogation room of the detention house.  
For a person who is undergoing administrative detention, compulsory isolation for drug rehabilitation, or serving a sentence in prison, interrogation may be conducted at the place of enforcement.  
For a criminal suspect who does not need to be detained or arrested, upon approval by the head of the case handling department, the suspect may be summoned to the public security organ’s case handling premises in the city or county where the suspect is located, or to his or her residence for interrogation.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　传唤犯罪嫌疑人时，应当出示传唤证和侦查人员的人民警察证，并责令其在传唤证上签名、捺指印。  
犯罪嫌疑人到案后，应当由其在传唤证上填写到案时间。传唤结束时，应当由其在传唤证上填写传唤结束时间。犯罪嫌疑人拒绝填写的，侦查人员应当在传唤证上注明。  
对在现场发现的犯罪嫌疑人，侦查人员经出示人民警察证，可以口头传唤，并将传唤的原因和依据告知被传唤人。在讯问笔录中应当注明犯罪嫌疑人到案方式，并由犯罪嫌疑人注明到案时间和传唤结束时间。  
对自动投案或者群众扭送到公安机关的犯罪嫌疑人，可以依法传唤。

**Article 199** When summoning a criminal suspect, the summons warrant and the investigator’s People’s Police certificate shall be produced, and the suspect shall be ordered to sign and fingerprint the summons warrant.  
After the criminal suspect arrives, he or she shall fill in the time of arrival on the summons warrant. Upon the conclusion of the summons, the suspect shall fill in the time of conclusion of the summons on the summons warrant. If the criminal suspect refuses to fill in, the investigator shall note it on the summons warrant.  
For a criminal suspect found at the scene, the investigator may summon him or her orally upon producing the People’s Police certificate, and shall inform the summoned person of the reason and basis for the summons. The manner of arrival of the criminal suspect shall be noted in the interrogation record, and the criminal suspect shall note the time of arrival and the time of conclusion of the summons.  
For a criminal suspect who voluntarily surrenders or is brought by the masses to the public security organ, the suspect may be summoned according to law.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　传唤持续的时间不得超过十二小时。案情特别重大、复杂，需要采取拘留、逮捕措施的，经办案部门负责人批准，传唤持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。不得以连续传唤的形式变相拘禁犯罪嫌疑人。  
传唤期限届满，未作出采取其他强制措施决定的，应当立即结束传唤。

**Article 200** The duration of a summons shall not exceed twelve hours. Where the case is particularly serious and complex and detention or arrest measures are required, upon approval by the head of the case handling department, the duration of the summons shall not exceed twenty-four hours. A criminal suspect shall not be detained in disguised form by consecutive summons.  
If no decision to take other compulsory measures is made upon expiration of the summon period, the summons shall be terminated immediately.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　传唤、拘传、讯问犯罪嫌疑人，应当保证犯罪嫌疑人的饮食和必要的休息时间，并记录在案。

**Article 201** When summoning, summoning by warrant, or interrogating a criminal suspect, the suspect’s food and necessary rest time shall be ensured, and such shall be recorded.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　讯问犯罪嫌疑人，必须由侦查人员进行。讯问的时候，侦查人员不得少于二人。  
讯问同案的犯罪嫌疑人，应当个别进行。

**Article 202** Interrogation of a criminal suspect must be conducted by investigators. During interrogation, there shall be no fewer than two investigators.  
Interrogation of criminal suspects in the same case shall be conducted individually.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　侦查人员讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，应当首先讯问犯罪嫌疑人是否有犯罪行为，并告知犯罪嫌疑人享有的诉讼权利，如实供述自己罪行可以从宽处理以及认罪认罚的法律规定，让他陈述有罪的情节或者无罪的辩解，然后向他提出问题。  
犯罪嫌疑人对侦查人员的提问，应当如实回答。但是对与本案无关的问题，有拒绝回答的权利。  
第一次讯问，应当问明犯罪嫌疑人的姓名、别名、曾用名、出生年月日、户籍所在地、现住地、籍贯、出生地、民族、职业、文化程度、政治面貌、工作单位、家庭情况、社会经历，是否属于人大代表、政协委员，是否受过刑事处罚或者行政处理等情况。

**Article 203** When interrogating a criminal suspect, the investigator shall first ask whether the criminal suspect has committed any crime, and shall inform the suspect of the litigation rights he or she enjoys, that truthful confession may lead to lenient treatment, and the legal provisions on guilty plea and acceptance of punishment. The suspect shall be allowed to state the circumstances of guilt or make a defense of innocence, and then the investigator shall pose questions.  
The criminal suspect shall answer the investigator’s questions truthfully. However, the suspect has the right to refuse to answer questions irrelevant to the case.  
For the first interrogation, the criminal suspect’s name, alias, former name, date of birth, place of household registration, current residence, place of origin, place of birth, ethnicity, occupation, education level, political affiliation, work unit, family situation, social experience, whether he or she is a deputy to the People’s Congress or a member of the Chinese People’s Political Consultative Conference, and whether he or she has been subjected to criminal punishment or administrative handling shall be ascertained.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　讯问聋、哑的犯罪嫌疑人，应当有通晓聋、哑手势的人参加，并在讯问笔录上注明犯罪嫌疑人的聋、哑情况，以及翻译人员的姓名、工作单位和职业。  
讯问不通晓当地语言文字的犯罪嫌疑人，应当配备翻译人员。

**Article 204** When interrogating a criminal suspect who is deaf or mute, a person who understands sign language shall participate, and the interrogation record shall note the suspect’s deafness or muteness and the name, work unit, and occupation of the interpreter.  
When interrogating a criminal suspect who does not understand the local language, an interpreter shall be provided.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　侦查人员应当将问话和犯罪嫌疑人的供述或者辩解如实地记录清楚。制作讯问笔录应当使用能够长期保持字迹的材料。

**Article 205** Investigators shall accurately record the questions and the criminal suspect’s confessions or defenses. The interrogation record shall be made using materials that can maintain handwriting for a long time.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　讯问笔录应当交犯罪嫌疑人核对；对于没有阅读能力的，应当向他宣读。如果记录有遗漏或者差错，应当允许犯罪嫌疑人补充或者更正，并捺指印。笔录经犯罪嫌疑人核对无误后，应当由其在笔录上逐页签名、捺指印，并在末页写明“以上笔录我看过（或向我宣读过），和我说的相符”。拒绝签名、捺指印的，侦查人员应当在笔录上注明。  
讯问笔录上所列项目，应当按照规定填写齐全。侦查人员、翻译人员应当在讯问笔录上签名。

**Article 206** The interrogation record shall be handed to the criminal suspect for verification; if the suspect is unable to read, the record shall be read out to him or her. If there are omissions or errors, the suspect shall be allowed to supplement or correct them, and shall fingerprint. After the criminal suspect has verified the record as correct, he or she shall sign and fingerprint each page of the record, and shall write at the end: ‘I have read (or have had read to me) the above record, and it is consistent with what I have stated.’ If the suspect refuses to sign or fingerprint, the investigator shall note it on the record.  
The items listed on the interrogation record shall be filled in completely according to the provisions. The investigator and the interpreter shall sign the interrogation record.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　犯罪嫌疑人请求自行书写供述的，应当准许；必要时，侦查人员也可以要求犯罪嫌疑人亲笔书写供词。犯罪嫌疑人应当在亲笔供词上逐页签名、捺指印。侦查人员收到后，应当在首页右上方写明“于某年某月某日收到”，并签名。

**Article 207** If a criminal suspect requests to write a confession personally, the request shall be permitted; when necessary, investigators may also require the suspect to write a confession in his or her own hand. The criminal suspect shall sign and fingerprint each page of the personally written confession. After receiving it, the investigator shall write at the top right of the first page: ‘Received on ___ year ___ month ___ day,’ and sign.

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　讯问犯罪嫌疑人，在文字记录的同时，可以对讯问过程进行录音录像。对于可能判处无期徒刑、死刑的案件或者其他重大犯罪案件，应当对讯问过程进行录音录像。  
前款规定的“可能判处无期徒刑、死刑的案件”，是指应当适用的法定刑或者量刑档次包含无期徒刑、死刑的案件。“其他重大犯罪案件”，是指致人重伤、死亡的严重危害公共安全犯罪、严重侵犯公民人身权利犯罪，以及黑社会性质组织犯罪、严重毒品犯罪等重大故意犯罪案件。  
对讯问过程录音录像的，应当对每一次讯问全程不间断进行，保持完整性。不得选择性地录制，不得剪接、删改。

**Article 208** When interrogating a criminal suspect, the interrogation process may be audio and video recorded simultaneously with written recording. For cases where life imprisonment or the death penalty may be imposed, or other major criminal cases, the interrogation process shall be audio and video recorded.  
The ‘cases where life imprisonment or the death penalty may be imposed’ as mentioned in the preceding paragraph refers to cases where the applicable statutory punishment or sentencing bracket includes life imprisonment or the death penalty. ‘Other major criminal cases’ refers to major intentional crimes such as crimes seriously endangering public security involving serious injury or death, crimes seriously infringing upon citizens’ personal rights, organized crimes of a mafia nature, and serious drug crimes.  
Where audio and video recording of the interrogation process is conducted, the entire process of each interrogation shall be recorded without interruption, maintaining completeness. It shall not be selectively recorded, edited, or altered.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　对犯罪嫌疑人供述的犯罪事实、无罪或者罪轻的事实、申辩和反证，以及犯罪嫌疑人提供的证明自己无罪、罪轻的证据，公安机关应当认真核查；对有关证据，无论是否采信，都应当如实记录、妥善保管，并连同核查情况附卷。

**Article 209** The public security organ shall carefully verify the criminal suspect’s confession of criminal facts, facts of innocence or lesser guilt, defenses and counter-evidence, as well as evidence provided by the criminal suspect to prove his or her innocence or lesser guilt; regarding the relevant evidence, whether or not it is admitted, it shall be truthfully recorded and properly preserved, and shall be attached to the file along with the verification.

#### 第三节　询问证人、被害人

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　询问证人、被害人，可以在现场进行，也可以到证人、被害人所在单位、住处或者证人、被害人提出的地点进行。在必要的时候，可以书面、电话或者当场通知证人、被害人到公安机关提供证言。  
询问证人、被害人应当个别进行。  
在现场询问证人、被害人，侦查人员应当出示人民警察证。到证人、被害人所在单位、住处或者证人、被害人提出的地点询问证人、被害人，应当经办案部门负责人批准，制作询问通知书。询问前，侦查人员应当出示询问通知书和人民警察证。

**Article 210** Questioning witnesses and victims may be conducted at the scene, or at the witness’s or victim’s work unit, residence, or a location proposed by the witness or victim. When necessary, the witness or victim may be notified in writing, by telephone, or on the spot to go to the public security organ to provide testimony.  
Witnesses and victims shall be questioned individually.  
When questioning a witness or victim at the scene, the investigator shall produce the People’s Police certificate. When questioning a witness or victim at the witness’s or victim’s work unit, residence, or a location proposed by the witness or victim, approval of the head of the case handling department shall be obtained, and a questioning notice shall be prepared. Before questioning, the investigator shall produce the questioning notice and the People’s Police certificate.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　询问前，应当了解证人、被害人的身份，证人、被害人、犯罪嫌疑人之间的关系。询问时，应当告知证人、被害人必须如实地提供证据、证言和有意作伪证或者隐匿罪证应负的法律责任。  
侦查人员不得向证人、被害人泄露案情或者表示对案件的看法，严禁采用暴力、威胁等非法方法询问证人、被害人。

**Article 211** Before questioning, the identity of the witness or victim, and the relationship between the witness, victim, and criminal suspect shall be ascertained. During questioning, the witness or victim shall be informed that they must provide evidence and testimony truthfully, and the legal liability for intentionally giving false testimony or concealing criminal evidence.  
Investigators shall not disclose case details or express opinions on the case to witnesses or victims, and it is strictly prohibited to question witnesses or victims by illegal methods such as violence or threats.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　[本规定第二百零六条](#art-206)、第二百零七条的规定，也适用于询问证人、被害人。

**Article 212** The provisions of Articles 206 and 207 of these Provisions also apply to questioning witnesses and victims.

#### 第四节　勘验、检查

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　侦查人员对于与犯罪有关的场所、物品、人身、尸体应当进行勘验或者检查，及时提取、采集与案件有关的痕迹、物证、生物样本等。在必要的时候，可以指派或者聘请具有专门知识的人，在侦查人员的主持下进行勘验、检查。

**Article 213** Investigators shall conduct inspection or examination of places, articles, persons, and corpses related to the crime, and promptly extract and collect traces, physical evidence, biological samples, etc. related to the case. When necessary, persons with specialized knowledge may be appointed or commissioned to conduct inspection or examination under the direction of an investigator.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　发案地派出所、巡警等部门应当妥善保护犯罪现场和证据，控制犯罪嫌疑人，并立即报告公安机关主管部门。  
执行勘查的侦查人员接到通知后，应当立即赶赴现场；勘查现场，应当持有刑事犯罪现场勘查证。

**Article 214** The police station of the location where the crime occurred and the patrol police department shall properly protect the crime scene and evidence, control the criminal suspect, and immediately report to the competent department of the public security organ.  
An investigator assigned to conduct investigation shall immediately proceed to the scene upon receiving the notice; for investigation of the scene, a crime scene investigation certificate shall be held.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　公安机关对案件现场进行勘查，侦查人员不得少于二人。

**Article 215** When the public security organ conducts investigation of a crime scene, there shall be no fewer than two investigators.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　勘查现场，应当拍摄现场照片、绘制现场图，制作笔录，由参加勘查的人和见证人签名。对重大案件的现场勘查，应当录音录像。

**Article 216** When investigating a crime scene, scene photographs shall be taken, a scene diagram shall be drawn, and a record shall be made, signed by the participants in the investigation and witnesses. For investigation of scenes of major cases, audio and video recording shall be conducted.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　为了确定被害人、犯罪嫌疑人的某些特征、伤害情况或者生理状态，可以对人身进行检查，依法提取、采集肖像、指纹等人体生物识别信息，采集血液、尿液等生物样本。被害人死亡的，应当通过被害人近亲属辨认、提取生物样本鉴定等方式确定被害人身份。  
犯罪嫌疑人拒绝检查、提取、采集的，侦查人员认为必要的时候，经办案部门负责人批准，可以强制检查、提取、采集。  
检查妇女的身体，应当由女工作人员或者医师进行。  
检查的情况应当制作笔录，由参加检查的侦查人员、检查人员、被检查人员和见证人签名。被检查人员拒绝签名的，侦查人员应当在笔录中注明。

**Article 217** For the purpose of determining certain characteristics, injury conditions, or physiological conditions of a victim or criminal suspect, a body examination may be conducted, and human biological identification information such as portraits and fingerprints may be extracted and collected according to law, and biological samples such as blood and urine may be collected. If the victim is deceased, the victim’s identity shall be determined through identification by close relatives, extraction of biological samples for evaluation, etc.  
If a criminal suspect refuses examination, extraction, or collection, where an investigator deems it necessary, upon approval by the head of the case handling department, compulsory examination, extraction, or collection may be conducted.  
Examination of a woman’s body shall be conducted by a female staff member or a physician.  
The circumstances of the examination shall be recorded in a record, signed by the investigators, examiners, the person examined, and witnesses who participated in the examination. If the person examined refuses to sign, the investigator shall note it in the record.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　为了确定死因，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以解剖尸体，并且通知死者家属到场，让其在解剖尸体通知书上签名。  
死者家属无正当理由拒不到场或者拒绝签名的，侦查人员应当在解剖尸体通知书上注明。对身份不明的尸体，无法通知死者家属的，应当在笔录中注明。

**Article 218** : To determine the cause of death, upon approval by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, a corpse may be dissected, and the family members of the deceased shall be notified to be present and to sign the notice of autopsy.  
If the family members of the deceased refuse to be present or refuse to sign without justifiable reasons, the investigators shall indicate such circumstances on the notice of autopsy. For a corpse of unidentified identity, if the family members of the deceased cannot be notified, such circumstances shall be indicated in the record.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　对已查明死因，没有继续保存必要的尸体，应当通知家属领回处理，对于无法通知或者通知后家属拒绝领回的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以及时处理。

**Article 219** : For a corpse for which the cause of death has been ascertained and there is no need for continued preservation, the family members shall be notified to take back and dispose of it; if notification is impossible or the family members refuse to take it back after notification, upon approval by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, the corpse may be disposed of in a timely manner.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　公安机关进行勘验、检查后，人民检察院要求复验、复查的，公安机关应当进行复验、复查，并可以通知人民检察院派员参加。

**Article 220** : After a public security organ conducts an inspection and examination, if the People's Procuratorate requests a re-inspection or re-examination, the public security organ shall conduct a re-inspection or re-examination, and may notify the People's Procuratorate to send personnel to participate.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　为了查明案情，在必要的时候，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以进行侦查实验。  
进行侦查实验，应当全程录音录像，并制作侦查实验笔录，由参加实验的人签名。  
进行侦查实验，禁止一切足以造成危险、侮辱人格或者有伤风化的行为。

**Article 221** : To ascertain the facts of a case, when necessary, upon approval by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, an investigative experiment may be conducted.  
When conducting an investigative experiment, audio and video recording shall be made throughout the entire process, and a record of the investigative experiment shall be prepared, signed by the persons participating in the experiment.  
When conducting an investigative experiment, all acts that may cause danger, humiliate human dignity, or offend public decency are prohibited.

#### 第五节　搜 查

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　为了收集犯罪证据、查获犯罪人，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，侦查人员可以对犯罪嫌疑人以及可能隐藏罪犯或者犯罪证据的人的身体、物品、住处和其他有关的地方进行搜查。

**Article 222** : To collect criminal evidence and apprehend the offender, upon approval by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, investigators may conduct a search of the body, belongings, residence, and other relevant places of a criminal suspect and of any person who may be concealing the offender or criminal evidence.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　进行搜查，必须向被搜查人出示搜查证，执行搜查的侦查人员不得少于二人。

**Article 223** : When conducting a search, a search warrant must be presented to the person subject to the search, and the number of investigators executing the search shall not be less than two.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　执行拘留、逮捕的时候，遇有下列紧急情况之一的，不用搜查证也可以进行搜查：  
（一）可能随身携带凶器的；  
（二）可能隐藏爆炸、剧毒等危险物品的；  
（三）可能隐匿、毁弃、转移犯罪证据的；  
（四）可能隐匿其他犯罪嫌疑人的；  
（五）其他突然发生的紧急情况。

**Article 224** : When executing a detention or arrest, in the event of any of the following emergency circumstances, a search may be conducted without a search warrant:  
(1) The person may be carrying lethal weapons;  
(2) The person may be concealing dangerous articles such as explosives or highly toxic substances;  
(3) The person may be concealing, destroying, or transferring criminal evidence;  
(4) The person may be harboring other criminal suspects;  
(5) Other sudden emergency circumstances.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　进行搜查时，应当有被搜查人或者他的家属、邻居或者其他见证人在场。  
公安机关可以要求有关单位和个人交出可以证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪的物证、书证、视听资料等证据。遇到阻碍搜查的，侦查人员可以强制搜查。  
搜查妇女的身体，应当由女工作人员进行。

**Article 225** : When conducting a search, the person subject to the search or his/her family members, neighbors, or other witnesses shall be present.  
A public security organ may require relevant entities and individuals to hand over physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, and other evidence that may prove the criminal suspect guilty or innocent. If the search is obstructed, investigators may conduct a compulsory search.  
A search of a woman's body shall be conducted by female staff members.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　搜查的情况应当制作笔录，由侦查人员和被搜查人或者他的家属，邻居或者其他见证人签名。  
如果被搜查人拒绝签名，或者被搜查人在逃，他的家属拒绝签名或者不在场的，侦查人员应当在笔录中注明。

**Article 226** : A record of the search shall be prepared, signed by the investigators and the person subject to the search or his/her family members, neighbors, or other witnesses.  
If the person subject to the search refuses to sign, or if the person subject to the search is at large and his/her family members refuse to sign or are not present, the investigators shall indicate such circumstances in the record.

#### 第六节　查封、扣押

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　在侦查活动中发现的可用以证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪的各种财物、文件，应当查封、扣押；但与案件无关的财物、文件，不得查封、扣押。  
持有人拒绝交出应当查封、扣押的财物、文件的，公安机关可以强制查封、扣押。

**Article 227** : Any property or documents discovered in investigative activities that may be used to prove a criminal suspect guilty or innocent shall be sealed up or seized; however, property or documents unrelated to the case shall not be sealed up or seized.  
If the holder refuses to hand over property or documents that shall be sealed up or seized, the public security organ may compulsorily seal them up or seize them.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　在侦查过程中需要扣押财物、文件的，应当经办案部门负责人批准，制作扣押决定书；在现场勘查或者搜查中需要扣押财物、文件的，由现场指挥人员决定；但扣押财物、文件价值较高或者可能严重影响正常生产经营的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作扣押决定书。  
在侦查过程中需要查封土地、房屋等不动产，或者船舶、航空器以及其他不宜移动的大型机器、设备等特定动产的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准并制作查封决定书。

**Article 228** : If it is necessary to seize property or documents during the investigation, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the case-handling department, and a decision on seizure shall be issued; if it is necessary to seize property or documents during an on-site inspection or search, the on-site commander shall decide; however, if the value of the property or documents to be seized is relatively high or may seriously affect normal production and operations, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, and a decision on seizure shall be issued.  
If it is necessary to seal up immovable property such as land or houses, or specific movable property such as ships, aircraft, or large machinery and equipment that are not suitable for removal, during the investigation, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, and a decision on sealing up shall be issued.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　执行查封、扣押的侦查人员不得少于二人，并出示[本规定第二百二十八条](#art-228)规定的有关法律文书。  
查封、扣押的情况应当制作笔录，由侦查人员、持有人和见证人签名。对于无法确定持有人或者持有人拒绝签名的，侦查人员应当在笔录中注明。

**Article 229** : The number of investigators executing a sealing-up or seizure shall not be less than two, and they shall present the relevant legal documents specified in Article 228 of these Provisions.  
A record of the sealing-up or seizure shall be prepared, signed by the investigators, the holder, and the witness. If the holder cannot be determined or refuses to sign, the investigators shall indicate such circumstances in the record.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　对查封、扣押的财物和文件，应当会同在场见证人和被查封、扣押财物、文件的持有人查点清楚，当场开列查封、扣押清单一式三份，写明财物或者文件的名称、编号、数量、特征及其来源等，由侦查人员、持有人和见证人签名，一份交给持有人，一份交给公安机关保管人员，一份附卷备查。  
对于财物、文件的持有人无法确定，以及持有人不在现场或者拒绝签名的，侦查人员应当在清单中注明。  
依法扣押文物、贵金属、珠宝、字画等贵重财物的，应当拍照或者录音录像，并及时鉴定、估价。  
执行查封、扣押时，应当为犯罪嫌疑人及其所扶养的亲属保留必需的生活费用和物品。能够保证侦查活动正常进行的，可以允许有关当事人继续合理使用有关涉案财物，但应当采取必要的保值、保管措施。

**Article 230** : The property and documents sealed up or seized shall be checked and counted in the presence of the on-site witnesses and the holder of the sealed up or seized property and documents, and a list of sealed up or seized items shall be made on the spot in triplicate, specifying the name, serial number, quantity, characteristics, and source of the property or documents, etc., signed by the investigators, the holder, and the witness. One copy shall be given to the holder, one copy to the custodian of the public security organ, and one copy shall be placed on file for reference.  
If the holder of the property or documents cannot be determined, or if the holder is not present or refuses to sign, the investigators shall indicate such circumstances in the list.  
If cultural relics, precious metals, jewelry, calligraphy and paintings, or other valuable property are seized according to law, photographs or audio/video recordings shall be taken, and an appraisal and valuation shall be conducted in a timely manner.  
When executing a sealing-up or seizure, necessary living expenses and articles shall be reserved for the criminal suspect and his/her dependents. If normal investigative activities can be ensured, the relevant party may be allowed to continue to use the relevant property involved in the case reasonably, but necessary measures for value preservation and custody shall be taken.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　对作为犯罪证据但不便提取或者没有必要提取的财物、文件，经登记、拍照或者录音录像、估价后，可以交财物、文件持有人保管或者封存，并且开具登记保存清单一式两份，由侦查人员、持有人和见证人签名，一份交给财物、文件持有人，另一份连同照片或者录音录像资料附卷备查。财物、文件持有人应当妥善保管，不得转移、变卖、毁损。

**Article 231** : For property or documents that serve as criminal evidence but are inconvenient or unnecessary to be taken, after registration, photographing or audio/video recording, and valuation, they may be handed over to the holder for custody or sealed up, and a list of registration and preservation shall be issued in duplicate, signed by the investigators, the holder, and the witness. One copy shall be given to the holder of the property or documents, and the other copy, together with the photographs or audio/video recording materials, shall be placed on file for reference. The holder of the property or documents shall properly safeguard them and shall not transfer, sell, or damage them.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　扣押犯罪嫌疑人的邮件、电子邮件、电报，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作扣押邮件、电报通知书，通知邮电部门或者网络服务单位检交扣押。  
不需要继续扣押的时候，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作解除扣押邮件、电报通知书，立即通知邮电部门或者网络服务单位。

**Article 232** : To seize the mail, email, or telegrams of a criminal suspect, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, and a notice of seizure of mail and telegrams shall be issued, notifying the postal and telecommunications department or the network service provider to check and hand over for seizure.  
When continued seizure is no longer necessary, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, and a notice of release of seizure of mail and telegrams shall be issued, immediately notifying the postal and telecommunications department or the network service provider.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　对查封、扣押的财物、文件、邮件、电子邮件、电报，经查明确实与案件无关的，应当在三日以内解除查封、扣押，退还原主或者原邮电部门、网络服务单位；原主不明确的，应当采取公告方式告知原主认领。在通知原主或者公告后六个月以内，无人认领的，按照无主财物处理，登记后上缴国库。

**Article 233** : If, after investigation, the sealed up or seized property, documents, mail, email, or telegrams are confirmed to be unrelated to the case, the sealing-up or seizure shall be released within three days, and the items shall be returned to the original owner or the original postal and telecommunications department or network service provider. If the original owner is unclear, a public notice shall be issued to inform the original owner to claim the items. If no one claims them within six months after the notice to the original owner or the public notice, the items shall be treated as ownerless property, registered, and turned over to the State Treasury.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　有关犯罪事实查证属实后，对于有证据证明权属明确且无争议的被害人合法财产及其孳息，且返还不损害其他被害人或者利害关系人的利益，不影响案件正常办理的，应当在登记、拍照或者录音录像和估价后，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，开具发还清单返还，并在案卷材料中注明返还的理由，将原物照片、发还清单和被害人的领取手续存卷备查。  
领取人应当是涉案财物的合法权利人或者其委托的人；委托他人领取的，应当出具委托书。侦查人员或者公安机关其他工作人员不得代为领取。  
查找不到被害人，或者通知被害人后，无人领取的，应当将有关财产及其孳息随案移送。

**Article 234** : After the relevant criminal facts have been verified, if there is evidence to prove that the lawful property and its fruits of the victim are clearly owned and undisputed, and the return thereof does not harm the interests of other victims or interested persons, and does not affect the normal handling of the case, after registration, photographing or audio/video recording, and valuation, the matter shall be reported to the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level for approval, a return list shall be issued for the return, and the reason for the return shall be stated in the case file materials, with the original photographs, the return list, and the victim's receipt documents kept on file for reference.  
The recipient shall be the lawful right holder of the property involved in the case or a person authorized by him/her; if a person is authorized to receive the property, a power of attorney shall be issued. Investigators or other staff members of the public security organ shall not receive the property on behalf of the victim.  
If the victim cannot be located, or if after notification no one comes to claim the property, the relevant property and its fruits shall be transferred along with the case.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　对查封、扣押的财物及其孳息、文件，公安机关应当妥善保管，以供核查。任何单位和个人不得违规使用、调换、损毁或者自行处理。  
县级以上公安机关应当指定一个内设部门作为涉案财物管理部门，负责对涉案财物实行统一管理，并设立或者指定专门保管场所，对涉案财物进行集中保管。  
对价值较低、易于保管，或者需要作为证据继续使用，以及需要先行返还被害人的涉案财物，可以由办案部门设置专门的场所进行保管。办案部门应当指定不承担办案工作的民警负责本部门涉案财物的接收、保管、移交等管理工作；严禁由侦查人员自行保管涉案财物。

**Article 235** : A public security organ shall properly safeguard the sealed up or seized property and its fruits and documents for verification. No entity or individual may use, exchange, damage, or dispose of them in violation of regulations.  
A public security organ at or above the county level shall designate an internal department as the management department for property involved in the case, responsible for the unified management of the property involved in the case, and shall establish or designate a special storage place for centralized safekeeping of the property involved in the case.  
For property involved in the case that is of low value, easy to safeguard, or needs to be continuously used as evidence, or needs to be returned to the victim in advance, the case-handling department may set up a special place for safekeeping. The case-handling department shall designate a police officer who does not undertake case-handling work to be responsible for the receipt, safekeeping, and transfer of the property involved in the case within the department; investigators are strictly prohibited from personally safeguarding the property involved in the case.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　在侦查期间，对于易损毁、灭失、腐烂、变质而不宜长期保存，或者难以保管的物品，经县级以上公安机关主要负责人批准，可以在拍照或者录音录像后委托有关部门变卖、拍卖，变卖、拍卖的价款暂予保存，待诉讼终结后一并处理。  
对于违禁品，应当依照国家有关规定处理；需要作为证据使用的，应当在诉讼终结后处理。

**Article 236** : During the investigation period, for items that are prone to damage, loss, decay, or deterioration and are not suitable for long-term preservation, or are difficult to safeguard, upon approval by the principal responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, after photographing or audio/video recording, the items may be entrusted to relevant departments for sale or auction, and the proceeds from the sale or auction shall be temporarily preserved and dealt with together after the conclusion of the proceedings.  
For contraband, it shall be dealt with in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State; if it needs to be used as evidence, it shall be dealt with after the conclusion of the proceedings.

#### 第七节　查询、冻结

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　公安机关根据侦查犯罪的需要，可以依照规定查询、冻结犯罪嫌疑人的存款、汇款、证券交易结算资金、期货保证金等资金，债券、股票、基金份额和其他证券，以及股权、保单权益和其他投资权益等财产，并可以要求有关单位和个人配合。  
对于前款规定的财产，不得划转、转账或者以其他方式变相扣押。

**Article 237** : A public security organ, based on the needs of investigating a crime, may, in accordance with the provisions, inquire into and freeze a criminal suspect's funds such as deposits, remittances, securities transaction settlement funds, and futures deposits; bonds, stocks, fund shares, and other securities; and property such as equity, insurance policy interests, and other investment interests, and may require relevant entities and individuals to cooperate.  
For the property specified in the preceding paragraph, no transfer, remittance, or other form of disguised seizure shall be carried out.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　向金融机构等单位查询犯罪嫌疑人的存款、汇款、证券交易结算资金、期货保证金等资金，债券、股票、基金份额和其他证券，以及股权、保单权益和其他投资权益等财产，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作协助查询财产通知书，通知金融机构等单位协助办理。

**Article 238** Where inquiring into funds such as deposits, remittances, securities trading settlement funds, and futures margins of a criminal suspect, and properties such as bonds, stocks, fund shares and other securities, as well as equities, insurance policy interests and other investment interests, from financial institutions and other entities, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, and a Notice of Assistance in Property Inquiry shall be prepared to notify the financial institutions and other entities to assist in handling.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　需要冻结犯罪嫌疑人财产的，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作协助冻结财产通知书，明确冻结财产的账户名称、账户号码、冻结数额、冻结期限、冻结范围以及是否及于孳息等事项，通知金融机构等单位协助办理。  
冻结股权、保单权益的，应当经设区的市一级以上公安机关负责人批准。  
冻结上市公司股权的，应当经省级以上公安机关负责人批准。

**Article 239** Where it is necessary to freeze the property of a criminal suspect, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, and a Notice of Assistance in Freezing Property shall be prepared, specifying the account name, account number, frozen amount, freezing period, scope of freezing, and whether it extends to proceeds and other matters, and notify financial institutions and other entities to assist in handling.  
For freezing equities or insurance policy interests, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts.  
For freezing equities of a listed company, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the provincial level.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　需要延长冻结期限的，应当按照原批准权限和程序，在冻结期限届满前办理继续冻结手续。逾期不办理继续冻结手续的，视为自动解除冻结。

**Article 240** Where it is necessary to extend the freezing period, the procedures for continuing the freeze shall be completed before the expiration of the freezing period in accordance with the original approval authority and procedures. If the procedures for continuing the freeze are not completed before the expiration, the freeze shall be deemed automatically lifted.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　不需要继续冻结犯罪嫌疑人财产时，应当经原批准冻结的公安机关负责人批准，制作协助解除冻结财产通知书，通知金融机构等单位协助办理。

**Article 241** Where it is no longer necessary to freeze the property of a criminal suspect, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ that originally approved the freezing, and a Notice of Assistance in Lifting the Freeze on Property shall be prepared to notify financial institutions and other entities to assist in handling.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　犯罪嫌疑人的财产已被冻结的，不得重复冻结，但可以轮候冻结。

**Article 242** Where the property of a criminal suspect has already been frozen, it shall not be frozen again, but may be frozen by rotation (sequential freeze).

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　冻结存款、汇款、证券交易结算资金、期货保证金等财产的期限为六个月。每次续冻期限最长不得超过六个月。  
对于重大、复杂案件，经设区的市一级以上公安机关负责人批准，冻结存款、汇款、证券交易结算资金、期货保证金等财产的期限可以为一年。每次续冻期限最长不得超过一年。

**Article 243** The period for freezing property such as deposits, remittances, securities trading settlement funds, and futures margins shall be six months. The maximum period for each renewal of the freeze shall not exceed six months.  
For major or complex cases, with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts, the period for freezing property such as deposits, remittances, securities trading settlement funds, and futures margins may be one year. The maximum period for each renewal of the freeze shall not exceed one year.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　冻结债券、股票、基金份额等证券的期限为二年。每次续冻期限最长不得超过二年。

**Article 244** The period for freezing securities such as bonds, stocks, and fund shares shall be two years. The maximum period for each renewal of the freeze shall not exceed two years.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　冻结股权、保单权益或者投资权益的期限为六个月。每次续冻期限最长不得超过六个月。

**Article 245** The period for freezing equities, insurance policy interests, or investment interests shall be six months. The maximum period for each renewal of the freeze shall not exceed six months.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　对冻结的债券、股票、基金份额等财产，应当告知当事人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人有权申请出售。  
权利人书面申请出售被冻结的债券、股票、基金份额等财产，不损害国家利益、被害人、其他权利人利益，不影响诉讼正常进行的，以及冻结的汇票、本票、支票的有效期即将届满的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以依法出售或者变现，所得价款应当继续冻结在其对应的银行账户中；没有对应的银行账户的，所得价款由公安机关在银行指定专门账户保管，并及时告知当事人或者其近亲属。

**Article 246** For frozen property such as bonds, stocks, and fund shares, the party or his or her statutory agent or authorized agent shall be informed that they have the right to apply for the sale.  
Where the right holder applies in writing to sell the frozen bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other property, if such sale does not harm the national interest, the interests of the victim or other right holders, and does not affect the normal conduct of the proceedings, or if the validity period of the frozen draft, promissory note, or check is about to expire, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, the property may be sold or realized according to law, and the proceeds shall continue to be frozen in their corresponding bank accounts; if there is no corresponding bank account, the proceeds shall be kept in a special account designated by the public security organ at a bank, and the party or his or her close relatives shall be informed in a timely manner.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　对冻结的财产，经查明确实与案件无关的，应当在三日以内通知金融机构等单位解除冻结，并通知被冻结财产的所有人。

**Article 247** Where frozen property is verified to be indeed irrelevant to the case, the financial institution or other entity shall be notified within three days to lift the freeze, and the owner of the frozen property shall be notified.

#### 第八节　鉴 定

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　为了查明案情，解决案件中某些专门性问题，应当指派、聘请有专门知识的人进行鉴定。  
需要聘请有专门知识的人进行鉴定，应当经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，制作鉴定聘请书。

**Article 248** To ascertain the facts of a case and resolve certain specialized issues in the case, persons with specialized knowledge shall be designated or appointed to conduct expert evaluation.  
Where it is necessary to appoint a person with specialized knowledge to conduct expert evaluation, approval shall be obtained from the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, and a Notice of Appointment for Expert Evaluation shall be prepared.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　公安机关应当为鉴定人进行鉴定提供必要的条件，及时向鉴定人送交有关检材和对比样本等原始材料，介绍与鉴定有关的情况，并且明确提出要求鉴定解决的问题。  
禁止暗示或者强迫鉴定人作出某种鉴定意见。

**Article 249** The public security organ shall provide the necessary conditions for the expert evaluator to conduct the evaluation, promptly deliver relevant inspection materials and comparison samples and other original materials to the expert evaluator, introduce the circumstances related to the evaluation, and clearly specify the issues to be solved by the evaluation.  
It is prohibited to suggest or compel the expert evaluator to produce a particular expert opinion.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　侦查人员应当做好检材的保管和送检工作，并注明检材送检环节的责任人，确保检材在流转环节中的同一性和不被污染。

**Article 250** Investigators shall properly handle the custody and submission of inspection materials, and specify the persons responsible for each link in the submission process, to ensure the identity of the inspection materials during transfer and that they are not contaminated.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　鉴定人应当按照鉴定规则，运用科学方法独立进行鉴定。鉴定后，应当出具鉴定意见，并在鉴定意见书上签名，同时附上鉴定机构和鉴定人的资质证明或者其他证明文件。  
多人参加鉴定，鉴定人有不同意见的，应当注明。

**Article 251** The expert evaluator shall independently conduct the evaluation in accordance with the evaluation rules and using scientific methods. After the evaluation, the expert evaluator shall issue an expert opinion and sign the expert opinion document, and shall attach the qualification certificates of the evaluation institution and the expert evaluator or other supporting documents.  
Where multiple persons participate in the evaluation and the expert evaluators have different opinions, such differences shall be noted.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　对鉴定意见，侦查人员应当进行审查。  
对经审查作为证据使用的鉴定意见，公安机关应当及时告知犯罪嫌疑人、被害人或者其法定代理人。

**Article 252** Investigators shall review the expert opinion.  
For an expert opinion that is used as evidence after review, the public security organ shall promptly inform the criminal suspect, the victim, or their statutory agents.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　犯罪嫌疑人、被害人对鉴定意见有异议提出申请，以及办案部门或者侦查人员对鉴定意见有疑义的，可以将鉴定意见送交其他有专门知识的人员提出意见。必要时，询问鉴定人并制作笔录附卷。

**Article 253** Where the criminal suspect or the victim has an objection to the expert opinion and submits an application, or where the case-handling department or the investigator has doubts about the expert opinion, the expert opinion may be submitted to other persons with specialized knowledge for their opinions. If necessary, the expert evaluator shall be questioned and a record shall be made and attached to the case file.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　经审查，发现有下列情形之一的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，应当补充鉴定：  
（一）鉴定内容有明显遗漏的；  
（二）发现新的有鉴定意义的证物的；  
（三）对鉴定证物有新的鉴定要求的；  
（四）鉴定意见不完整，委托事项无法确定的；  
（五）其他需要补充鉴定的情形。  
经审查，不符合上述情形的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，作出不准予补充鉴定的决定，并在作出决定后三日以内书面通知申请人。

**Article 254** Upon review, if any of the following circumstances is found, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a supplementary expert evaluation shall be conducted:  
(1) There is an obvious omission in the evaluation content;  
(2) New evidentiary material with evaluation significance is discovered;  
(3) There is a new evaluation requirement for the evaluated evidentiary material;  
(4) The expert opinion is incomplete and the entrusted matters cannot be determined;  
(5) Other circumstances requiring a supplementary evaluation.  
Upon review, if the circumstances do not meet the above conditions, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a decision not to permit supplementary evaluation shall be made, and the applicant shall be notified in writing within three days after the decision is made.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　经审查，发现有下列情形之一的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，应当重新鉴定：  
（一）鉴定程序违法或者违反相关专业技术要求的；  
（二）鉴定机构、鉴定人不具备鉴定资质和条件的；  
（三）鉴定人故意作虚假鉴定或者违反回避规定的；  
（四）鉴定意见依据明显不足的；  
（五）检材虚假或者被损坏的；  
（六）其他应当重新鉴定的情形。  
重新鉴定，应当另行指派或者聘请鉴定人。  
经审查，不符合上述情形的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，作出不准予重新鉴定的决定，并在作出决定后三日以内书面通知申请人。

**Article 255** Upon review, if any of the following circumstances is found, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a new expert evaluation shall be conducted:  
(1) The evaluation procedure is illegal or violates relevant professional technical requirements;  
(2) The evaluation institution or expert evaluator does not have the qualifications and conditions for evaluation;  
(3) The expert evaluator intentionally makes a false evaluation or violates the recusal provisions;  
(4) The basis for the expert opinion is obviously insufficient;  
(5) The inspection material is false or damaged;  
(6) Other circumstances requiring a new evaluation.  
For a new evaluation, a different expert evaluator shall be designated or appointed.  
Upon review, if the circumstances do not meet the above conditions, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, a decision not to permit a new evaluation shall be made, and the applicant shall be notified in writing within three days after the decision is made.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　公诉人、当事人或者辩护人、诉讼代理人对鉴定意见有异议，经人民法院依法通知的，公安机关鉴定人应当出庭作证。  
鉴定人故意作虚假鉴定的，应当依法追究其法律责任。

**Article 256** Where the public prosecutor, a party, or the defender or litigation agent has an objection to the expert opinion, and the People's Court has notified according to law, the expert evaluator of the public security organ shall appear in court to testify.  
If an expert evaluator intentionally makes a false evaluation, his or her legal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　对犯罪嫌疑人作精神病鉴定的时间不计入办案期限，其他鉴定时间都应当计入办案期限。

**Article 257** The time for conducting a psychiatric evaluation on a criminal suspect shall not be counted in the case-handling period; the time for other evaluations shall be counted in the case-handling period.

#### 第九节　辨 认

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　为了查明案情，在必要的时候，侦查人员可以让被害人、证人或者犯罪嫌疑人对与犯罪有关的物品、文件、尸体、场所或者犯罪嫌疑人进行辨认。

**Article 258** : To ascertain the facts of a case, when necessary, investigators may have the victim, witness, or criminal suspect identify objects, documents, bodies, places, or criminal suspects related to the crime.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　辨认应当在侦查人员的主持下进行。主持辨认的侦查人员不得少于二人。  
几名辨认人对同一辨认对象进行辨认时，应当由辨认人个别进行。

**Article 259** : Identification shall be conducted under the direction of investigators. The number of investigators directing the identification shall not be less than two.  
Where several persons are to make identifications of the same object, each person shall make the identification individually.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　辨认时，应当将辨认对象混杂在特征相类似的其他对象中，不得在辨认前向辨认人展示辨认对象及其影像资料，不得给辨认人任何暗示。  
辨认犯罪嫌疑人时，被辨认的人数不得少于七人；对犯罪嫌疑人照片进行辨认的，不得少于十人的照片。  
辨认物品时，混杂的同类物品不得少于五件；对物品的照片进行辨认的，不得少于十个物品的照片。  
对场所、尸体等特定辨认对象进行辨认，或者辨认人能够准确描述物品独有特征的，陪衬物不受数量的限制。

**Article 260** : When making identifications, the object to be identified shall be mixed with other objects of similar characteristics; the object to be identified and its video or photographic materials shall not be shown to the person making the identification before the identification, and no suggestion shall be given to the person making the identification.  
When identifying a criminal suspect, the number of persons to be identified shall not be less than seven; when identifying a photograph of a criminal suspect, the number of photographs shall not be less than ten.  
When identifying an object, the number of similar objects mixed shall not be less than five; when identifying a photograph of an object, the number of photographs of objects shall not be less than ten.  
When identifying specific objects such as places or bodies, or where the person making the identification is able to accurately describe the unique characteristics of the object, there shall be no limit on the number of foils.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　对犯罪嫌疑人的辨认，辨认人不愿意公开进行时，可以在不暴露辨认人的情况下进行，并应当为其保守秘密。

**Article 261** : If the person making the identification of a criminal suspect does not wish to do so publicly, the identification may be conducted without exposing the person making the identification, and confidentiality shall be maintained for that person.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　对辨认经过和结果，应当制作辨认笔录，由侦查人员、辨认人、见证人签名。必要时，应当对辨认过程进行录音录像。

**Article 262** : A record of the identification process and results shall be made, which shall be signed by the investigators, the person making the identification, and the witness. When necessary, the identification process shall be recorded by audio or video.

#### 第十节　技术侦查

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　公安机关在立案后，根据侦查犯罪的需要，可以对下列严重危害社会的犯罪案件采取技术侦查措施：  
（一）危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪、黑社会性质的组织犯罪、重大毒品犯罪案件；  
（二）故意杀人、故意伤害致人重伤或者死亡、强奸、抢劫、绑架、放火、爆炸、投放危险物质等严重暴力犯罪案件；  
（三）集团性、系列性、跨区域性重大犯罪案件；  
（四）利用电信、计算机网络、寄递渠道等实施的重大犯罪案件，以及针对计算机网络实施的重大犯罪案件；  
（五）其他严重危害社会的犯罪案件，依法可能判处七年以上有期徒刑的。  
公安机关追捕被通缉或者批准、决定逮捕的在逃的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人，可以采取追捕所必需的技术侦查措施。

**Article 263** : After placing a case on file, a public security organ may, based on the needs of criminal investigation, adopt technical investigation measures for the following criminal cases that seriously endanger society:  
(1) Crimes endangering national security, terrorist activities, organized crimes of a gangland nature, and major drug crimes;  
(2) Serious violent crimes such as intentional homicide, intentional injury causing serious injury or death, rape, robbery, kidnapping, arson, explosion, and release of dangerous substances;  
(3) Major crimes of a group, serial, or cross-regional nature;  
(4) Major crimes committed through telecommunications, computer networks, postal or courier channels, and major crimes committed against computer networks;  
(5) Other crimes that seriously endanger society and are punishable by law with a fixed-term imprisonment of seven years or more.  
When pursuing and capturing a criminal suspect or defendant who is wanted or has been approved or decided for arrest and is at large, a public security organ may adopt technical investigation measures necessary for the pursuit.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　技术侦查措施是指由设区的市一级以上公安机关负责技术侦查的部门实施的记录监控、行踪监控、通信监控、场所监控等措施。  
技术侦查措施的适用对象是犯罪嫌疑人、被告人以及与犯罪活动直接关联的人员。

**Article 264** : Technical investigation measures refer to measures such as recording surveillance, tracking surveillance, communications surveillance, and premises surveillance implemented by the department responsible for technical investigation of a public security organ at or above the level of a city divided into districts.  
The persons subject to technical investigation measures are criminal suspects, defendants, and persons directly associated with criminal activities.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　需要采取技术侦查措施的，应当制作呈请采取技术侦查措施报告书，报设区的市一级以上公安机关负责人批准，制作采取技术侦查措施决定书。  
人民检察院等部门决定采取技术侦查措施，交公安机关执行的，由设区的市一级以上公安机关按照规定办理相关手续后，交负责技术侦查的部门执行，并将执行情况通知人民检察院等部门。

**Article 265** : If technical investigation measures need to be adopted, a written request for the adoption of technical investigation measures shall be prepared and submitted to the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the level of a city divided into districts for approval, and a decision letter for adopting technical investigation measures shall be made.  
If a People's Procuratorate or other department decides to adopt technical investigation measures and has them executed by a public security organ, the public security organ at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall, after completing the relevant procedures in accordance with the provisions, hand the matter over to the department responsible for technical investigation for execution, and shall notify the People's Procuratorate or other department of the execution.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　批准采取技术侦查措施的决定自签发之日起三个月以内有效。  
在有效期限内，对不需要继续采取技术侦查措施的，办案部门应当立即书面通知负责技术侦查的部门解除技术侦查措施；负责技术侦查的部门认为需要解除技术侦查措施的，报批准机关负责人批准，制作解除技术侦查措施决定书，并及时通知办案部门。  
对复杂、疑难案件，采取技术侦查措施的有效期限届满仍需要继续采取技术侦查措施的，经负责技术侦查的部门审核后，报批准机关负责人批准，制作延长技术侦查措施期限决定书。批准延长期限，每次不得超过三个月。  
有效期限届满，负责技术侦查的部门应当立即解除技术侦查措施。

**Article 266** : A decision approving the adoption of technical investigation measures shall be valid for a period not exceeding three months from the date of issuance.  
Within the validity period, if there is no need to continue the technical investigation measures, the case-handling department shall immediately notify the department responsible for technical investigation in writing to lift the technical investigation measures; if the department responsible for technical investigation considers that the technical investigation measures need to be lifted, it shall submit a report to the responsible person of the approving authority for approval, make a decision letter for lifting the technical investigation measures, and promptly notify the case-handling department.  
For complex or difficult cases, if there is still a need to continue technical investigation measures after the validity period expires, after review by the department responsible for technical investigation, a report shall be submitted to the responsible person of the approving authority for approval, and a decision letter for extending the period of technical investigation measures shall be made. Each extension shall not exceed three months.  
Upon expiration of the validity period, the department responsible for technical investigation shall immediately lift the technical investigation measures.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　采取技术侦查措施，必须严格按照批准的措施种类、适用对象和期限执行。  
在有效期限内，需要变更技术侦查措施种类或者适用对象的，应当按照[本规定第二百六十五条](#art-265)规定重新办理批准手续。

**Article 267** : Technical investigation measures must be strictly executed in accordance with the approved types of measures, applicable subjects, and duration.  
Within the validity period, if the type or applicable subject of technical investigation measures needs to be changed, the approval formalities shall be re-completed in accordance with Article 265 of these Provisions.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　采取技术侦查措施收集的材料在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。使用技术侦查措施收集的材料作为证据时，可能危及有关人员的人身安全，或者可能产生其他严重后果的，应当采取不暴露有关人员身份和使用的技术设备、侦查方法等保护措施。  
采取技术侦查措施收集的材料作为证据使用的，采取技术侦查措施决定书应当附卷。

**Article 268** : Materials collected through the adoption of technical investigation measures may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings. When using materials collected through technical investigation measures as evidence, if it may endanger the personal safety of the persons concerned or may cause other serious consequences, protective measures shall be taken, such as not disclosing the identities of the persons concerned and the technical equipment and investigation methods used.  
Where materials collected through technical investigation measures are used as evidence, the decision letter for adopting technical investigation measures shall be attached to the case file.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　采取技术侦查措施收集的材料，应当严格依照有关规定存放，只能用于对犯罪的侦查、起诉和审判，不得用于其他用途。  
采取技术侦查措施收集的与案件无关的材料，必须及时销毁，并制作销毁记录。

**Article 269** : Materials collected through the adoption of technical investigation measures shall be stored strictly in accordance with relevant provisions, and may only be used for the investigation, prosecution, and adjudication of crimes, and shall not be used for other purposes.  
Materials collected through technical investigation measures that are unrelated to the case must be promptly destroyed, and a record of destruction shall be made.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　侦查人员对采取技术侦查措施过程中知悉的国家秘密、商业秘密和个人隐私，应当保密。  
公安机关依法采取技术侦查措施，有关单位和个人应当配合，并对有关情况予以保密。

**Article 270** : Investigators shall keep confidential any State secrets, trade secrets, and personal privacy learned during the adoption of technical investigation measures.  
Where a public security organ adopts technical investigation measures according to law, the relevant entities and individuals shall cooperate and keep the relevant information confidential.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　为了查明案情，在必要的时候，经县级以上公安机关负责人决定，可以由侦查人员或者公安机关指定的其他人员隐匿身份实施侦查。  
隐匿身份实施侦查时，不得使用促使他人产生犯罪意图的方法诱使他人犯罪，不得采用可能危害公共安全或者发生重大人身危险的方法。

**Article 271** : To ascertain the facts of a case, when necessary, with a decision by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, investigators or other persons designated by the public security organ may conceal their identities to conduct investigations.  
When conducting investigations with concealed identities, methods that induce others to commit crimes by causing them to form criminal intent shall not be used, and methods that may endanger public safety or cause serious personal danger shall not be adopted.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　对涉及给付毒品等违禁品或者财物的犯罪活动，为查明参与该项犯罪的人员和犯罪事实，根据侦查需要，经县级以上公安机关负责人决定，可以实施控制下交付。

**Article 272** : For criminal activities involving the delivery of drugs or other contraband or property, in order to identify the persons participating in such criminal activities and the facts of the crime, based on investigation needs and with a decision by the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, controlled delivery may be carried out.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　公安机关依照本节规定实施隐匿身份侦查和控制下交付收集的材料在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。  
使用隐匿身份侦查和控制下交付收集的材料作为证据时，可能危及隐匿身份人员的人身安全，或者可能产生其他严重后果的，应当采取不暴露有关人员身份等保护措施。

**Article 273** : Materials collected by a public security organ through investigations with concealed identities and controlled delivery conducted in accordance with the provisions of this Section may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings.  
When using materials collected through investigations with concealed identities and controlled delivery as evidence, if it may endanger the personal safety of the persons with concealed identities or may cause other serious consequences, protective measures shall be taken, such as not disclosing the identities of the persons concerned.

#### 第十一节　通 缉

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　应当逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人在逃的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以发布通缉令，采取有效措施，追捕归案。  
县级以上公安机关在自己管辖的地区内，可以直接发布通缉令；超出自己管辖的地区，应当报请有权决定的上级公安机关发布。  
通缉令的发送范围，由签发通缉令的公安机关负责人决定。

**Article 274** : If a criminal suspect who should be arrested is at large, with the approval of the responsible person of a public security organ at or above the county level, a wanted notice may be issued, and effective measures shall be taken to pursue and capture the suspect and bring him or her to justice.  
A public security organ at or above the county level may directly issue a wanted notice within the area under its jurisdiction; if the wanted notice extends beyond its jurisdiction, it shall report to the superior public security organ with authority to decide to issue the wanted notice.  
The distribution scope of the wanted notice shall be decided by the responsible person of the public security organ that issues the wanted notice.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　通缉令中应当尽可能写明被通缉人的姓名、别名、曾用名、绰号、性别、年龄、民族、籍贯、出生地、户籍所在地、居住地、职业、身份证号码、衣着和体貌特征、口音、行为习惯，并附被通缉人近期照片，可以附指纹及其他物证的照片。除了必须保密的事项以外，应当写明发案的时间、地点和简要案情。

**Article 275** : A wanted notice shall, to the extent possible, include the wanted person's name, alias, former name, nickname, gender, age, ethnicity, place of origin, place of birth, place of household registration, place of residence, occupation, identity card number, clothing and physical features, accent, and behavioral habits, and shall be accompanied by a recent photograph of the wanted person, and may be accompanied by fingerprints and photographs of other physical evidence. Except for matters that must be kept confidential, the time, place, and brief facts of the case shall be stated.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　通缉令发出后，如果发现新的重要情况可以补发通报。通报必须注明原通缉令的编号和日期。

**Article 276** : After a wanted notice is issued, if new important information is discovered, a supplementary notice may be issued. The supplementary notice must indicate the number and date of the original wanted notice.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　公安机关接到通缉令后，应当及时布置查缉。抓获犯罪嫌疑人后，报经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，凭通缉令或者相关法律文书羁押，并通知通缉令发布机关进行核实，办理交接手续。

**Article 277** : After receiving a wanted notice, a public security organ shall promptly arrange for the search and apprehension. After the criminal suspect is captured, the public security organ shall, with the approval of the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, detain the suspect on the basis of the wanted notice or relevant legal documents, and notify the issuing authority of the wanted notice to verify and complete the handover procedures.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　需要对犯罪嫌疑人在口岸采取边控措施的，应当按照有关规定制作边控对象通知书，并附有关法律文书，经县级以上公安机关负责人审核后，层报省级公安机关批准，办理全国范围内的边控措施。需要限制犯罪嫌疑人人身自由的，应当附有关限制人身自由的法律文书。  
紧急情况下，需要采取边控措施的，县级以上公安机关可以出具公函，先向有关口岸所在地出入境边防检查机关交控，但应当在七日以内按照规定程序办理全国范围内的边控措施。

**Article 278** : Where it is necessary to take border control measures against a criminal suspect at a port, a notice of the person subject to border control shall be prepared in accordance with relevant provisions, together with the relevant legal instruments, and after review by the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, shall be reported level by level to the public security organ at the provincial level for approval, and border control measures within the nationwide scope shall be processed. Where it is necessary to restrict the personal freedom of the criminal suspect, the relevant legal instruments restricting personal freedom shall be attached.  
In an emergency, where it is necessary to take border control measures, a public security organ at or above the county level may issue an official letter to first submit the control to the exit-entry border inspection authority at the location of the relevant port, but shall, within seven days, process border control measures within the nationwide scope in accordance with the prescribed procedures.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　为发现重大犯罪线索，追缴涉案财物、证据，查获犯罪嫌疑人，必要时，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以发布悬赏通告。  
悬赏通告应当写明悬赏对象的基本情况和赏金的具体数额。

**Article 279** : For the purpose of discovering major criminal leads, recovering property or evidence involved in the case, or apprehending a criminal suspect, when necessary, with the approval of the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, a reward notice may be published.  
A reward notice shall state the basic information of the person sought and the specific amount of the reward.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　通缉令、悬赏通告应当广泛张贴，并可以通过广播、电视、报刊、计算机网络等方式发布。

**Article 280** : Wanted circulars and reward notices shall be widely posted and may be published by means of radio, television, newspapers, computer networks, and other means.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　经核实，犯罪嫌疑人已经自动投案、被击毙或者被抓获，以及发现有其他不需要采取通缉、边控、悬赏通告的情形的，发布机关应当在原通缉、通知、通告范围内，撤销通缉令、边控通知、悬赏通告。

**Article 281** : Upon verification that the criminal suspect has voluntarily surrendered, has been shot dead, or has been apprehended, or that other circumstances exist where it is unnecessary to take wanted, border control, or reward notice measures, the issuing organ shall, within the original scope of the wanted circular, notice, or notice, revoke the wanted circular, border control notice, or reward notice.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　通缉越狱逃跑的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者罪犯，适用本节的有关规定。

**Article 282** : The relevant provisions of this Section shall apply to the wanted circular for a criminal suspect, defendant, or criminal offender who has escaped from prison.

#### 第十二节　侦查终结

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　侦查终结的案件，应当同时符合以下条件：  
（一）案件事实清楚；  
（二）证据确实、充分；  
（三）犯罪性质和罪名认定正确；  
（四）法律手续完备；  
（五）依法应当追究刑事责任。

**Article 283** : A case concluded after investigation shall meet all of the following conditions:  
(1) the facts of the case are clear;  
(2) the evidence is reliable and sufficient;  
(3) the nature of the crime and the charge are correctly determined;  
(4) the legal formalities are complete; and  
(5) criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　对侦查终结的案件，公安机关应当全面审查证明证据收集合法性的证据材料，依法排除非法证据。排除非法证据后证据不足的，不得移送审查起诉。  
公安机关发现侦查人员非法取证的，应当依法作出处理，并可另行指派侦查人员重新调查取证。

**Article 284** : For a case concluded after investigation, the public security organ shall comprehensively examine the evidentiary materials proving the legality of evidence collection and shall, according to law, exclude illegally obtained evidence. Where the evidence is insufficient after the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence, the case shall not be transferred for examination and prosecution.  
Where a public security organ discovers that an investigator has obtained evidence illegally, it shall handle the matter according to law and may assign another investigator to conduct investigation and collect evidence anew.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　侦查终结的案件，侦查人员应当制作结案报告。  
结案报告应当包括以下内容：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人的基本情况；  
（二）是否采取了强制措施及其理由；  
（三）案件的事实和证据；  
（四）法律依据和处理意见。

**Article 285** : For a case concluded after investigation, the investigator shall prepare a case closure report.  
The case closure report shall include the following:  
(1) the basic information of the criminal suspect;  
(2) whether compulsory measures have been taken and the reasons therefor;  
(3) the facts and evidence of the case; and  
(4) the legal basis and proposed handling.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　侦查终结案件的处理，由县级以上公安机关负责人批准；重大、复杂、疑难的案件应当经过集体讨论。

**Article 286** : The handling of a case concluded after investigation shall be approved by the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level; major, complex, or difficult cases shall be subject to collective discussion.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　侦查终结后，应当将全部案卷材料按照要求装订立卷。  
向人民检察院移送案件时，只移送诉讼卷，侦查卷由公安机关存档备查。

**Article 287** : After the conclusion of investigation, all case file materials shall be bound and filed in accordance with requirements.  
When a case is transferred to a People's Procuratorate, only the litigation volume shall be transferred, and the investigation volume shall be kept on file by the public security organ for future reference.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　对查封、扣押的犯罪嫌疑人的财物及其孳息、文件或者冻结的财产，作为证据使用的，应当随案移送，并制作随案移送清单一式两份，一份留存，一份交人民检察院。制作清单时，应当根据已经查明的案情，写明对涉案财物的处理建议。  
对于实物不宜移送的，应当将其清单、照片或者其他证明文件随案移送。待人民法院作出生效判决后，按照人民法院送达的生效判决书、裁定书依法作出处理，并向人民法院送交回执。人民法院在判决、裁定中未对涉案财物作出处理的，公安机关应当征求人民法院意见，并根据人民法院的决定依法作出处理。

**Article 288** : With respect to the sealed up or seized property, its fruits, or documents of a criminal suspect, or frozen property, where they are used as evidence, they shall be transferred together with the case, and a checklist of the items transferred together with the case shall be prepared in duplicate, with one copy retained and one copy submitted to the People's Procuratorate. When preparing the checklist, the handling suggestion for the property involved in the case shall be stated based on the facts that have been ascertained.  
Where physical evidence is not suitable for transfer, its checklist, photographs, or other certification documents shall be transferred together with the case. After the People's Court has rendered an effective judgment, it shall be handled according to law in accordance with the effective written judgment or ruling served by the People's Court, and a receipt shall be submitted to the People's Court. Where the People's Court does not handle the property involved in the case in the judgment or ruling, the public security organ shall seek the opinion of the People's Court and shall handle it according to law based on the decision of the People's Court.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　对侦查终结的案件，应当制作起诉意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，连同全部案卷材料、证据，以及辩护律师提出的意见，一并移送同级人民检察院审查决定；同时将案件移送情况告知犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护律师。  
犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪的，应当记录在案，随案移送，并在起诉意见书中写明有关情况；认为案件符合速裁程序适用条件的，可以向人民检察院提出适用速裁程序的建议。

**Article 289** : For a case concluded after investigation, a written opinion on prosecution shall be prepared, and after approval by the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, it shall be transferred together with all case file materials and evidence, as well as the opinions raised by the defense lawyer, to the People's Procuratorate at the same level for examination and decision; at the same time, the fact of the case transfer shall be notified to the criminal suspect and his or her defense lawyer.  
Where the criminal suspect voluntarily admits guilt, this shall be recorded in the case file, transferred together with the case, and the relevant circumstances shall be stated in the written opinion on prosecution; if it is considered that the case meets the conditions for application of the fast-track sentencing procedure, a proposal for applying the fast-track sentencing procedure may be submitted to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　对于犯罪嫌疑人在境外，需要及时进行审判的严重危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪案件，应当在侦查终结后层报公安部批准，移送同级人民检察院审查起诉。  
在审查起诉或者缺席审理过程中，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人向公安机关自动投案或者被公安机关抓获的，公安机关应当立即通知人民检察院、人民法院。

**Article 290** : For a crime case seriously endangering national security or a terrorist activity crime case where the criminal suspect is outside the territory and a timely trial is necessary, after the conclusion of investigation, it shall be reported level by level to the Ministry of Public Security for approval and transferred to the People's Procuratorate at the same level for examination and prosecution.  
Where, during the process of examination and prosecution or trial in absentia, the criminal suspect or defendant voluntarily surrenders to or is apprehended by the public security organ, the public security organ shall immediately notify the People's Procuratorate and the People's Court.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　共同犯罪案件的起诉意见书，应当写明每个犯罪嫌疑人在共同犯罪中的地位、作用、具体罪责和认罪态度，并分别提出处理意见。

**Article 291** : In a written opinion on prosecution for a joint crime case, the status, role, specific criminal responsibility, and attitude toward admission of guilt of each criminal suspect in the joint crime shall be stated, and handling opinions shall be proposed separately.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　被害人提出附带民事诉讼的，应当记录在案；移送审查起诉时，应当在起诉意见书末页注明。

**Article 292** : Where a victim brings an incidental civil action, it shall be recorded in the case file; when transferring the case for examination and prosecution, it shall be indicated on the last page of the written opinion on prosecution.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　人民检察院作出不起诉决定的，如果被不起诉人在押，公安机关应当立即办理释放手续。除依法转为行政案件办理外，应当根据人民检察院解除查封、扣押、冻结财物的书面通知，及时解除查封、扣押、冻结。  
人民检察院提出对被不起诉人给予行政处罚、处分或者没收其违法所得的检察意见，移送公安机关处理的，公安机关应当将处理结果及时通知人民检察院。

**Article 293** : Where the People's Procuratorate makes a decision not to prosecute, if the person who is not prosecuted is in custody, the public security organ shall immediately go through the release formalities. Except where the case is transferred to be handled as an administrative case according to law, the sealing up, seizure, or freezing shall be promptly lifted in accordance with the written notice of the People's Procuratorate to lift the sealing up, seizure, or freezing of property.  
Where the People's Procuratorate puts forward a prosecutorial opinion to impose an administrative penalty, sanction, or confiscate the illegal gains of the person who is not prosecuted, and transfers it to the public security organ for handling, the public security organ shall promptly notify the People's Procuratorate of the result of the handling.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　认为人民检察院作出的不起诉决定有错误的，应当在收到不起诉决定书后七日以内制作要求复议意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，移送人民检察院复议。  
要求复议的意见不被接受的，可以在收到人民检察院的复议决定书后七日以内制作提请复核意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，连同人民检察院的复议决定书，一并提请上一级人民检察院复核。

**Article 294** : Where a public security organ considers that the decision not to prosecute made by a People's Procuratorate is erroneous, it shall, within seven days after receiving the written decision not to prosecute, prepare a written opinion requesting reconsideration, and after approval by the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, transfer it to the People's Procuratorate for reconsideration.  
Where the opinion requesting reconsideration is not accepted, within seven days after receiving the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate, a written opinion requesting review may be prepared, and after approval by the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, it shall be submitted together with the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level for review.

#### 第十三节　补充侦查

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　侦查终结，移送人民检察院审查起诉的案件，人民检察院退回公安机关补充侦查的，公安机关接到人民检察院退回补充侦查的法律文书后，应当按照补充侦查提纲在一个月以内补充侦查完毕。  
补充侦查以二次为限。

**Article 295** : Where, for a case concluded after investigation and transferred to a People's Procuratorate for examination and prosecution, the People's Procuratorate returns the case to the public security organ for supplementary investigation, the public security organ shall, after receiving the legal instruments for the return for supplementary investigation from the People's Procuratorate, complete the supplementary investigation within one month in accordance with the supplementary investigation outline.  
Supplementary investigation shall be limited to two times.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　对人民检察院退回补充侦查的案件，根据不同情况，报县级以上公安机关负责人批准，分别作如下处理：  
（一）原认定犯罪事实不清或者证据不够充分的，应当在查清事实、补充证据后，制作补充侦查报告书，移送人民检察院审查；对确实无法查明的事项或者无法补充的证据，应当书面向人民检察院说明情况；  
（二）在补充侦查过程中，发现新的同案犯或者新的罪行，需要追究刑事责任的，应当重新制作起诉意见书，移送人民检察院审查；  
（三）发现原认定的犯罪事实有重大变化，不应当追究刑事责任的，应当撤销案件或者对犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查，并将有关情况通知退查的人民检察院；  
（四）原认定犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分，人民检察院退回补充侦查不当的，应当说明理由，移送人民检察院审查。
