**Article 296** : For a case returned by the People's Procuratorate for supplementary investigation, depending on the different circumstances, after reporting to and obtaining approval from the person responsible for a public security organ at or above the county level, the following handling shall be made respectively:  
(1) where the originally determined criminal facts are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, after clarifying the facts and supplementing the evidence, a supplementary investigation report shall be prepared and transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination; for matters that truly cannot be ascertained or evidence that cannot be supplemented, the circumstances shall be explained in writing to the People's Procuratorate;  
(2) where, during the supplementary investigation, a new accomplice or a new crime is discovered, and criminal liability needs to be investigated, a new written opinion on prosecution shall be prepared and transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination;  
(3) where it is discovered that the originally determined criminal facts have undergone major changes and criminal liability should not be investigated, the case shall be dismissed or the investigation of the criminal suspect shall be terminated, and the relevant circumstances shall be notified to the People's Procuratorate that returned the case for supplementary investigation;  
(4) where the originally determined criminal facts are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the People's Procuratorate's return for supplementary investigation is inappropriate, the reasons shall be explained and the case shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　对于人民检察院在审查起诉过程中以及在人民法院作出生效判决前，要求公安机关提供法庭审判所必需的证据材料的，应当及时收集和提供。

**Article 297** : Where a People's Procuratorate, during the process of examination and prosecution or before the People's Court renders an effective judgment, requests a public security organ to provide evidentiary materials necessary for court trial, such materials shall be promptly collected and provided.

### 第九章　执行刑罚

#### 第一节　罪犯的交付

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　对被依法判处刑罚的罪犯，如果罪犯已被采取强制措施的，公安机关应当依据人民法院生效的判决书、裁定书以及执行通知书，将罪犯交付执行。  
对人民法院作出无罪或者免除刑事处罚的判决，如果被告人在押，公安机关在收到相应的法律文书后应当立即办理释放手续；对人民法院建议给予行政处理的，应当依照有关规定处理或者移送有关部门。

**Article 298** For a criminal sentenced to a penalty according to law, if compulsory measures have been taken against the criminal, the public security organ shall, based on the effective judgment, ruling, and enforcement notice of the People's Court, deliver the criminal for enforcement.  
If the People's Court renders a judgment of acquittal or exemption from criminal punishment, and the defendant is in custody, the public security organ shall, upon receipt of the relevant legal documents, immediately handle the release procedures; if the People's Court recommends administrative disposition, the public security organ shall handle the matter in accordance with relevant provisions or transfer it to the relevant department.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　对被判处死刑的罪犯，公安机关应当依据人民法院执行死刑的命令，将罪犯交由人民法院执行。

**Article 299** For a criminal sentenced to death, the public security organ shall, based on the order of the People's Court for execution of the death penalty, deliver the criminal to the People's Court for enforcement.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　公安机关接到人民法院生效的判处死刑缓期二年执行、无期徒刑、有期徒刑的判决书、裁定书以及执行通知书后，应当在一个月以内将罪犯送交监狱执行。  
对未成年犯应当送交未成年犯管教所执行刑罚。

**Article 300** After receiving the effective judgment, ruling, and enforcement notice of the People's Court imposing a death sentence with a two-year reprieve, life imprisonment, or fixed-term imprisonment, the public security organ shall, within one month, deliver the criminal to a prison for enforcement.  
For a juvenile offender, the criminal shall be delivered to a juvenile correctional facility for enforcement of the penalty.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　对被判处有期徒刑的罪犯，在被交付执行刑罚前，剩余刑期在三个月以下的，由看守所根据人民法院的判决代为执行。  
对被判处拘役的罪犯，由看守所执行。

**Article 301** For a criminal sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment, if the remaining term of the sentence is less than three months before being delivered for enforcement, the detention house shall execute the sentence on behalf of the People's Court based on the judgment.  
For a criminal sentenced to criminal detention, the detention house shall execute the sentence.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　对被判处管制、宣告缓刑、假释或者暂予监外执行的罪犯，已被羁押的，由看守所将其交付社区矫正机构执行。  
对被判处剥夺政治权利的罪犯，由罪犯居住地的派出所负责执行。

**Article 302** For a criminal sentenced to public surveillance, placed on probation, granted parole, or temporarily serving a sentence outside prison, if the criminal has been held in custody, the detention house shall deliver the criminal to a community correctional institution for enforcement.  
For a criminal sentenced to deprivation of political rights, the police station at the place of the criminal's residence shall be responsible for enforcement.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　对被判处有期徒刑由看守所代为执行和被判处拘役的罪犯，执行期间如果没有再犯新罪，执行期满，看守所应当发给刑满释放证明书。

**Article 303** For a criminal sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment executed by the detention house on behalf and a criminal sentenced to criminal detention, if no new crime is committed during the enforcement period, upon expiration of the enforcement, the detention house shall issue a certificate of release after serving the full sentence.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　公安机关在执行刑罚中，如果认为判决有错误或者罪犯提出申诉，应当转请人民检察院或者原判人民法院处理。

**Article 304** If, during the enforcement of a penalty, the public security organ considers that the judgment is erroneous or the criminal files a petition, the public security organ shall refer the matter to the People's Procuratorate or the People's Court that rendered the original judgment for handling.

#### 第二节　减刑、假释、暂予监外执行

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　对依法留看守所执行刑罚的罪犯，符合减刑条件的，由看守所制作减刑建议书，经设区的市一级以上公安机关审查同意后，报请所在地中级以上人民法院审核裁定。

**Article 305** For a criminal who is lawfully detained in the detention house for execution of a penalty and meets the conditions for commutation of punishment, the detention house shall prepare a proposal for commutation of punishment, which, after examination and approval by the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts, shall be submitted to the intermediate People's Court or above at the place where the prison is located for examination and ruling.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　对依法留看守所执行刑罚的罪犯，符合假释条件的，由看守所制作假释建议书，经设区的市一级以上公安机关审查同意后，报请所在地中级以上人民法院审核裁定。

**Article 306** For a criminal who is lawfully detained in the detention house for execution of a penalty and meets the conditions for parole, the detention house shall prepare a proposal for parole, which, after examination and approval by the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts, shall be submitted to the intermediate People's Court or above at the place where the prison is located for examination and ruling.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　对依法留所执行刑罚的罪犯，有下列情形之一的，可以暂予监外执行：  
（一）有严重疾病需要保外就医的；  
（二）怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女；  
（三）生活不能自理，适用暂予监外执行不致危害社会的。  
对罪犯暂予监外执行的，看守所应当提出书面意见，报设区的市一级以上公安机关批准，同时将书面意见抄送同级人民检察院。  
对适用保外就医可能有社会危险性的罪犯，或者自伤自残的罪犯，不得保外就医。  
对罪犯确有严重疾病，必须保外就医的，由省级人民政府指定的医院诊断并开具证明文件。

**Article 307** For a criminal who is lawfully detained in the detention house for execution of a penalty, if any of the following circumstances exists, temporary service of sentence outside prison may be granted:  
(1) suffering from a serious illness requiring medical treatment outside prison;  
(2) being a pregnant woman or a woman nursing her own infant;  
(3) being unable to take care of oneself, and application of temporary service of sentence outside prison would not endanger society.  
If a criminal is to be temporarily served sentence outside prison, the detention house shall submit a written opinion to the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts for approval, and simultaneously copy the written opinion to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.  
For a criminal for whom medical treatment outside prison may pose a social danger, or for a criminal who self-inflicts injury or disability, medical treatment outside prison shall not be granted.  
If a criminal truly suffers from a serious illness and must receive medical treatment outside prison, the diagnosis and certification documents shall be issued by a hospital designated by the provincial people's government.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　公安机关决定对罪犯暂予监外执行的，应当将暂予监外执行决定书交被暂予监外执行的罪犯和负责监外执行的社区矫正机构，同时抄送同级人民检察院。

**Article 308** If the public security organ decides to grant temporary service of sentence outside prison to a criminal, it shall deliver the decision on temporary service of sentence outside prison to the criminal granted such service and the community correctional institution responsible for the service outside prison, and simultaneously copy it to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　批准暂予监外执行的公安机关接到人民检察院认为暂予监外执行不当的意见后，应当立即对暂予监外执行的决定进行重新核查。

**Article 309** After receiving the opinion of the People's Procuratorate that the temporary service of sentence outside prison is improper, the public security organ that approved the temporary service of sentence outside prison shall immediately re-examine the decision on temporary service of sentence outside prison.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　对暂予监外执行的罪犯，有下列情形之一的，批准暂予监外执行的公安机关应当作出收监执行决定：  
（一）发现不符合暂予监外执行条件的；  
（二）严重违反有关暂予监外执行监督管理规定的；  
（三）暂予监外执行的情形消失后，罪犯刑期未满的。  
对暂予监外执行的罪犯决定收监执行的，由暂予监外执行地看守所将罪犯收监执行。  
不符合暂予监外执行条件的罪犯通过贿赂等非法手段被暂予监外执行的，或者罪犯在暂予监外执行期间脱逃的，罪犯被收监执行后，所在看守所应当提出不计入执行刑期的建议，经设区的市一级以上公安机关审查同意后，报请所在地中级以上人民法院审核裁定。

**Article 310** For a criminal under temporary service of sentence outside prison, if any of the following circumstances exists, the public security organ that approved the temporary service of sentence outside prison shall make a decision to have the criminal taken back into prison for enforcement:  
(1) it is discovered that the conditions for temporary service of sentence outside prison are not met;  
(2) seriously violating the regulatory provisions concerning supervision and management of temporary service of sentence outside prison;  
(3) after the circumstances for temporary service of sentence outside prison have ceased to exist, the criminal's sentence has not yet expired.  
If a decision is made to have a criminal under temporary service of sentence outside prison taken back into prison for enforcement, the detention house at the place of temporary service of sentence outside prison shall carry out the enforcement.  
If a criminal who does not meet the conditions for temporary service of sentence outside prison is granted such service through illegal means such as bribery, or if the criminal escapes during the period of temporary service of sentence outside prison, after the criminal is taken back into prison for enforcement, the detention house where the criminal is held shall propose that the period not be counted toward the execution of the sentence, and after examination and approval by the public security organ at or above the level of a city with districts, the matter shall be submitted to the intermediate People's Court or above at the place where the prison is located for examination and ruling.

#### 第三节　剥夺政治权利

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　负责执行剥夺政治权利的派出所应当按照人民法院的判决，向罪犯及其所在单位、居住地基层组织宣布其犯罪事实、被剥夺政治权利的期限，以及罪犯在执行期间应当遵守的规定。

**Article 311** The police station responsible for executing the deprivation of political rights shall, in accordance with the judgment of the People's Court, announce to the criminal, the criminal's work unit, and the grassroots organization at the criminal's place of residence the facts of the crime, the term of deprivation of political rights, and the rules that the criminal shall observe during the enforcement period.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　被剥夺政治权利的罪犯在执行期间应当遵守下列规定：  
（一）遵守国家法律、行政法规和公安部制定的有关规定，服从监督管理；  
（二）不得享有选举权和被选举权；  
（三）不得组织或者参加集会、游行、示威、结社活动；  
（四）不得出版、制作、发行书籍、音像制品；  
（五）不得接受采访，发表演说；  
（六）不得在境内外发表有损国家荣誉、利益或者其他具有社会危害性的言论；  
（七）不得担任国家机关职务；  
（八）不得担任国有公司、企业、事业单位和人民团体的领导职务。

**Article 312** A criminal deprived of political rights shall observe the following provisions during the enforcement period:  
(1) abide by national laws, administrative regulations, and relevant provisions formulated by the Ministry of Public Security, and submit to supervision and management;  
(2) shall not enjoy the right to vote and the right to be elected;  
(3) shall not organize or participate in assemblies, processions, demonstrations, or association activities;  
(4) shall not publish, produce, or distribute books or audio-visual products;  
(5) shall not accept interviews or deliver speeches;  
(6) shall not make statements, either domestically or abroad, that damage national honor or interests, or that are otherwise socially harmful;  
(7) shall not hold any position in state organs;  
(8) shall not hold any leading position in state-owned companies, enterprises, institutions, or people's organizations.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　被剥夺政治权利的罪犯违反[本规定第三百一十二条](#art-312)的规定，尚未构成新的犯罪的，公安机关依法可以给予治安管理处罚。

**Article 313** If a criminal deprived of political rights violates the provisions of Article 312 of these Provisions but has not yet constituted a new crime, the public security organ may, according to law, impose a public security administration penalty.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　被剥夺政治权利的罪犯，执行期满，公安机关应当书面通知本人及其所在单位、居住地基层组织。

**Article 314** For a criminal deprived of political rights, upon expiration of the enforcement period, the public security organ shall notify in writing the criminal, the criminal's work unit, and the grassroots organization at the criminal's place of residence.

#### 第四节　对又犯新罪罪犯的处理

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　对留看守所执行刑罚的罪犯，在暂予监外执行期间又犯新罪的，由犯罪地公安机关立案侦查，并通知批准机关。批准机关作出收监执行决定后，应当根据侦查、审判需要，由犯罪地看守所或者暂予监外执行地看守所收监执行。

**Article 315** If a criminal detained in the detention house for execution of a penalty commits a new crime during the period of temporary service of sentence outside prison, the public security organ at the place of the crime shall file the case for investigation and notify the approving authority. After the approving authority makes a decision to take the criminal back into prison for enforcement, based on the needs of investigation and adjudication, the detention house at the place of the crime or the detention house at the place of temporary service of sentence outside prison shall take the criminal back into prison for enforcement.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　被剥夺政治权利、管制、宣告缓刑和假释的罪犯在执行期间又犯新罪的，由犯罪地公安机关立案侦查。  
对留看守所执行刑罚的罪犯，因犯新罪被撤销假释的，应当根据侦查、审判需要，由犯罪地看守所或者原执行看守所收监执行。

**Article 316** If a criminal under deprivation of political rights, public surveillance, probation, or parole commits a new crime during the enforcement period, the public security organ at the place of the crime shall file the case for investigation.  
For a criminal detained in the detention house for execution of a penalty whose parole is revoked due to committing a new crime, based on the needs of investigation and adjudication, the detention house at the place of the crime or the original enforcement detention house shall take the criminal back into prison for enforcement.

### 第十章　特别程序

#### 第一节　未成年人刑事案件诉讼程序

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　公安机关办理未成年人刑事案件，实行教育、感化、挽救的方针，坚持教育为主、惩罚为辅的原则。

**Article 317** In handling criminal cases involving minors, the public security organ shall implement the policy of education, persuasion, and redemption, and adhere to the principle of education as the main component and punishment as a supplement.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　公安机关办理未成年人刑事案件，应当保障未成年人行使其诉讼权利并得到法律帮助，依法保护未成年人的名誉和隐私，尊重其人格尊严。

**Article 318** When handling criminal cases involving minors, public security organs shall ensure that minors exercise their procedural rights and obtain legal assistance, protect their reputation and privacy according to law, and respect their personal dignity.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　公安机关应当设置专门机构或者配备专职人员办理未成年人刑事案件。  
未成年人刑事案件应当由熟悉未成年人身心特点，善于做未成年人思想教育工作，具有一定办案经验的人员办理。

**Article 319** Public security organs shall set up specialized agencies or assign full-time personnel to handle criminal cases involving minors.  
Criminal cases involving minors shall be handled by personnel who are familiar with the physical and psychological characteristics of minors, skilled in ideological education of minors, and possess certain case-handling experience.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　未成年犯罪嫌疑人没有委托辩护人的，公安机关应当通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 320** Where a juvenile criminal suspect has not retained a defender, the public security organ shall notify a legal aid agency to appoint a lawyer to provide defense for him or her.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　公安机关办理未成年人刑事案件时，应当重点查清未成年犯罪嫌疑人实施犯罪行为时是否已满十四周岁、十六周岁、十八周岁的临界年龄。

**Article 321** When handling criminal cases involving minors, public security organs shall focus on ascertaining whether the juvenile criminal suspect has reached the critical ages of 14, 16, or 18 years old at the time of committing the criminal act.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　公安机关办理未成年人刑事案件，根据情况可以对未成年犯罪嫌疑人的成长经历、犯罪原因、监护教育等情况进行调查并制作调查报告。  
作出调查报告的，在提请批准逮捕、移送审查起诉时，应当结合案情综合考虑，并将调查报告与案卷材料一并移送人民检察院。

**Article 322** When handling criminal cases involving minors, public security organs may, depending on the circumstances, investigate the juvenile criminal suspect's growth experience, cause of crime, guardianship education, and other circumstances and produce an investigation report.  
Where an investigation report is made, when submitting a request for approval of arrest or transferring the case for examination and prosecution, the public security organ shall comprehensively consider the case circumstances and transfer the investigation report together with the case file to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当通知未成年犯罪嫌疑人的法定代理人到场。无法通知、法定代理人不能到场或者法定代理人是共犯的，也可以通知未成年犯罪嫌疑人的其他成年亲属，所在学校、单位、居住地或者办案单位所在地基层组织或者未成年人保护组织的代表到场，并将有关情况记录在案。到场的法定代理人可以代为行使未成年犯罪嫌疑人的诉讼权利。  
到场的法定代理人或者其他人员提出侦查人员在讯问中侵犯未成年人合法权益的，公安机关应当认真核查，依法处理。

**Article 323** When interrogating a juvenile criminal suspect, the public security organ shall notify the legal representative of the juvenile criminal suspect to be present. If the legal representative cannot be notified, cannot be present, or is an accomplice, other adult relatives of the juvenile criminal suspect, representatives of the school, work unit, grassroots organization of the residence or the place where the case-handling organ is located, or a minor protection organization may also be notified to be present, and the relevant circumstances shall be recorded. The legal representative present may exercise the procedural rights of the juvenile criminal suspect on his or her behalf.  
Where the legal representative present or other persons raise that the investigators have infringed upon the minor's lawful rights and interests during interrogation, the public security organ shall carefully verify and handle the matter according to law.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人应当采取适合未成年人的方式，耐心细致地听取其供述或者辩解，认真审核、查证与案件有关的证据和线索，并针对其思想顾虑、恐惧心理、抵触情绪进行疏导和教育。  
讯问女性未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当有女工作人员在场。

**Article 324** Interrogation of a juvenile criminal suspect shall be conducted in a manner suitable for minors, with patient and careful listening to his or her confession or defense, careful examination and verification of evidence and leads related to the case, and counseling and education addressing his or her concerns, fears, and resistance.  
When interrogating a female juvenile criminal suspect, female staff members shall be present.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　讯问笔录应当交未成年犯罪嫌疑人、到场的法定代理人或者其他人员阅读或者向其宣读；对笔录内容有异议的，应当核实清楚，准予更正或者补充。

**Article 325** The interrogation record shall be handed to the juvenile criminal suspect, the legal representative present, or other persons for reading or shall be read to them; if there is any objection to the content of the record, the matter shall be verified and corrections or supplements shall be permitted.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　询问未成年被害人、证人，适用[本规定第三百二十三条](#art-323)、第三百二十四条、第三百二十五条的规定。  
询问未成年被害人、证人，应当以适当的方式进行，注意保护其隐私和名誉，尽可能减少询问频次，避免造成二次伤害。必要时，可以聘请熟悉未成年人身心特点的专业人员协助。

**Article 326** The provisions of Articles 323, 324, and 325 of these Provisions shall apply to the questioning of juvenile victims and witnesses.  
Questioning of juvenile victims and witnesses shall be conducted in an appropriate manner, with attention to protecting their privacy and reputation, minimizing the frequency of questioning as much as possible, and avoiding secondary harm. When necessary, professionals familiar with the physical and psychological characteristics of minors may be engaged to assist.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　对未成年犯罪嫌疑人应当严格限制和尽量减少使用逮捕措施。  
未成年犯罪嫌疑人被拘留、逮捕后服从管理、依法变更强制措施不致发生社会危险性，能够保证诉讼正常进行的，公安机关应当依法及时变更强制措施；人民检察院批准逮捕的案件，公安机关应当将变更强制措施情况及时通知人民检察院。

**Article 327** The use of arrest measures against juvenile criminal suspects shall be strictly restricted and minimized.  
Where a juvenile criminal suspect, after being detained or arrested, complies with management and the change of compulsory measures according to law will not cause social danger and can ensure the normal progress of the proceedings, the public security organ shall promptly change the compulsory measures according to law; for cases where the People's Procuratorate has approved arrest, the public security organ shall promptly notify the People's Procuratorate of the change of compulsory measures.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　对被羁押的未成年人应当与成年人分别关押、分别管理、分别教育，并根据其生理和心理特点在生活和学习方面给予照顾。

**Article 328** Juveniles in custody shall be detained, managed, and educated separately from adults, and shall be given care in terms of living and learning according to their physiological and psychological characteristics.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　人民检察院在对未成年人作出附条件不起诉的决定前，听取公安机关意见时，公安机关应当提出书面意见，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，移送同级人民检察院。

**Article 329** Where the People's Procuratorate, before making a decision on conditional non-prosecution of a juvenile, solicits the opinion of the public security organ, the public security organ shall submit a written opinion, which after approval by the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level, shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　认为人民检察院作出的附条件不起诉决定有错误的，应当在收到不起诉决定书后七日以内制作要求复议意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，移送同级人民检察院复议。  
要求复议的意见不被接受的，可以在收到人民检察院的复议决定书后七日以内制作提请复核意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准后，连同人民检察院的复议决定书，一并提请上一级人民检察院复核。

**Article 330** If the public security organ considers that the conditional non-prosecution decision made by the People's Procuratorate is erroneous, it shall, within seven days after receiving the decision of non-prosecution, produce a written opinion requesting reconsideration, and after approval by the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level, transfer it to the People's Procuratorate at the same level for reconsideration.  
If the opinion requesting reconsideration is not accepted, the public security organ may, within seven days after receiving the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate, produce a written opinion requesting review, and after approval by the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level, submit it together with the reconsideration decision of the People's Procuratorate to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level for review.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　未成年人犯罪的时候不满十八周岁，被判处五年有期徒刑以下刑罚的，公安机关应当依据人民法院已经生效的判决书，将该未成年人的犯罪记录予以封存。  
犯罪记录被封存的，除司法机关为办案需要或者有关单位根据国家规定进行查询外，公安机关不得向其他任何单位和个人提供。  
被封存犯罪记录的未成年人，如果发现漏罪，合并被判处五年有期徒刑以上刑罚的，应当对其犯罪记录解除封存。

**Article 331** Where a minor is under 18 years old at the time of committing the crime and is sentenced to a punishment of fixed-term imprisonment of five years or less, the public security organ shall, based on the effective judgment of the People's Court, seal the criminal record of the minor.  
If the criminal record is sealed, the public security organ shall not provide it to any other unit or individual, except where a judicial organ requires it for case handling or a relevant unit inquires pursuant to state provisions.  
Where a minor whose criminal record has been sealed is found to have omitted crimes and is sentenced to a combined punishment of fixed-term imprisonment of five years or more, the seal on the criminal record shall be lifted.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　办理未成年人刑事案件，除本节已有规定的以外，按照本规定的其他规定进行。

**Article 332** Handling criminal cases involving minors, unless otherwise provided in this Section, shall be conducted in accordance with other provisions of these Provisions.

#### 第二节　当事人和解的公诉案件诉讼程序

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　下列公诉案件，犯罪嫌疑人真诚悔罪，通过向被害人赔偿损失、赔礼道歉等方式获得被害人谅解，被害人自愿和解的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，可以依法作为当事人和解的公诉案件办理：  
（一）因民间纠纷引起，涉嫌刑法分则第四章、第五章规定的犯罪案件，可能判处三年有期徒刑以下刑罚的；  
（二）除渎职犯罪以外的可能判处七年有期徒刑以下刑罚的过失犯罪案件。  
犯罪嫌疑人在五年以内曾经故意犯罪的，不得作为当事人和解的公诉案件办理。

**Article 333** For the following public prosecution cases, where the criminal suspect sincerely repents and obtains the victim's understanding through compensation for losses, apology, or other means, and the victim voluntarily reconciles, upon approval by the person in charge of the public security organ at or above the county level, the case may be handled as a public prosecution case settled by party reconciliation according to law:  
(1) cases arising from civil disputes, suspected of crimes as stipulated in Chapter IV or V of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, for which the possible punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of three years or less;  
(2) cases of negligent crimes other than malfeasance crimes for which the possible punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of seven years or less.  
If the criminal suspect has committed an intentional crime within the previous five years, the case shall not be handled as a public prosecution case settled by party reconciliation.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　有下列情形之一的，不属于因民间纠纷引起的犯罪案件：  
（一）雇凶伤害他人的；  
（二）涉及黑社会性质组织犯罪的；  
（三）涉及寻衅滋事的；  
（四）涉及聚众斗殴的；  
（五）多次故意伤害他人身体的；  
（六）其他不宜和解的。

**Article 334** Cases with any of the following circumstances shall not be considered as criminal cases arising from civil disputes:  
(1) hiring someone to harm another person;  
(2) involving organized crime of a mafia-like nature;  
(3) involving picking quarrels and causing trouble;  
(4) involving gathering people to engage in affrays;  
(5) intentionally harming another person's body multiple times;  
(6) other circumstances not suitable for reconciliation.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　双方当事人和解的，公安机关应当审查案件事实是否清楚，被害人是否自愿和解，是否符合规定的条件。  
公安机关审查时，应当听取双方当事人的意见，并记录在案；必要时，可以听取双方当事人亲属、当地居民委员会或者村民委员会人员以及其他了解案件情况的相关人员的意见。

**Article 335** Where the parties reconcile, the public security organ shall examine whether the case facts are clear, whether the victim voluntarily reconciles, and whether the prescribed conditions are met.  
During the examination, the public security organ shall hear the opinions of both parties and record them; when necessary, it may hear the opinions of the relatives of both parties, personnel of the local residents' committee or villagers' committee, and other relevant persons who know the circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　达成和解的，公安机关应当主持制作和解协议书，并由双方当事人及其他参加人员签名。  
当事人中有未成年人的，未成年当事人的法定代理人或者其他成年亲属应当在场。

**Article 336** Where reconciliation is reached, the public security organ shall preside over the production of a reconciliation agreement, which shall be signed by both parties and other participants.  
If one of the parties is a minor, the legal representative or other adult relative of the minor party shall be present.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　和解协议书应当包括以下内容：  
（一）案件的基本事实和主要证据；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人承认自己所犯罪行，对指控的犯罪事实没有异议，真诚悔罪；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人通过向被害人赔礼道歉、赔偿损失等方式获得被害人谅解；涉及赔偿损失的，应当写明赔偿的数额、方式等；提起附带民事诉讼的，由附带民事诉讼原告人撤回附带民事诉讼；  
（四）被害人自愿和解，请求或者同意对犯罪嫌疑人依法从宽处罚。  
和解协议应当及时履行。

**Article 337** The reconciliation agreement shall include the following contents:  
(1) the basic facts of the case and the main evidence;  
(2) the criminal suspect admits to the crime committed, has no objection to the alleged criminal facts, and sincerely repents;  
(3) the criminal suspect obtains the victim's understanding by apologizing, compensating for losses, or other means; if compensation for losses is involved, the amount and method of compensation shall be specified; if a collateral civil action is initiated, the plaintiff of the collateral civil action shall withdraw the collateral civil action;  
(4) the victim voluntarily reconciles and requests or agrees to a lenient punishment for the criminal suspect according to law.  
The reconciliation agreement shall be performed promptly.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　对达成和解协议的案件，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，公安机关将案件移送人民检察院审查起诉时，可以提出从宽处理的建议。

**Article 338** For a case where a settlement agreement has been reached, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, when the public security organ transfers the case to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution, it may make a recommendation for lenient treatment.

#### 第三节　犯罪嫌疑人逃匿、死亡案件违法所得的没收程序

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　有下列情形之一，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，公安机关应当写出没收违法所得意见书，连同相关证据材料一并移送同级人民检察院：  
（一）恐怖活动犯罪等重大犯罪案件，犯罪嫌疑人逃匿，在通缉一年后不能到案的；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人死亡的。  
犯罪嫌疑人死亡，现有证据证明其存在违法所得及其他涉案财产应当予以没收的，公安机关可以进行调查。公安机关进行调查，可以依法进行查封、扣押、查询、冻结。

**Article 339** Where any of the following circumstances exists, and it is necessary to confiscate the illegal gains and other property involved in the case in accordance with the provisions of the Criminal Law, the public security organ shall, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, prepare a written opinion on confiscation of illegal gains and transfer it together with the relevant evidence materials to the People's Procuratorate at the same level:  
(a) In cases of major crimes such as terrorist activities, the criminal suspect has absconded and cannot be apprehended within one year after being wanted;  
(b) The criminal suspect is deceased.  
Where the criminal suspect is deceased and existing evidence proves that there are illegal gains and other property involved that shall be confiscated, the public security organ may conduct an investigation. When conducting an investigation, the public security organ may, according to law, seal up, seize, inquire into, and freeze such property.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　没收违法所得意见书应当包括以下内容：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人的基本情况；  
（二）犯罪事实和相关的证据材料；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人逃匿、被通缉或者死亡的情况；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人的违法所得及其他涉案财产的种类、数量、所在地；  
（五）查封、扣押、冻结的情况等。

**Article 340** The written opinion on confiscation of illegal gains shall include the following contents:  
(a) Basic information of the criminal suspect;  
(b) Facts of the crime and relevant evidence materials;  
(c) The circumstances of the criminal suspect's absconding, being wanted, or death;  
(d) The types, quantities, and locations of the illegal gains and other property involved of the criminal suspect;  
(e) The circumstances of sealing up, seizure, inquiry, and freezing, etc.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　公安机关将没收违法所得意见书移送人民检察院后，在逃的犯罪嫌疑人自动投案或者被抓获的，公安机关应当及时通知同级人民检察院。

**Article 341** After the public security organ has transferred the written opinion on confiscation of illegal gains to the People's Procuratorate, if the absconding criminal suspect voluntarily surrenders or is apprehended, the public security organ shall promptly notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

#### 第四节　依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的强制医疗程序

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　公安机关发现实施暴力行为，危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全的犯罪嫌疑人，可能属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的，应当对其进行精神病鉴定。

**Article 342** Where a public security organ discovers that a criminal suspect who commits violent acts, endangering public security or seriously endangering the personal safety of citizens, may be a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law, it shall arrange for a psychiatric assessment of such suspect.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　对经法定程序鉴定依法不负刑事责任的精神病人，有继续危害社会可能，符合强制医疗条件的，公安机关应当在七日以内写出强制医疗意见书，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，连同相关证据材料和鉴定意见一并移送同级人民检察院。

**Article 343** For a mental patient who, after assessment through statutory procedures, is not criminally liable according to law, who may continue to endanger society, and who meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, the public security organ shall, within seven days, prepare a written opinion on compulsory medical treatment, and upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, transfer it together with the relevant evidence materials and the assessment opinion to the People's Procuratorate at the same level.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　对实施暴力行为的精神病人，在人民法院决定强制医疗前，经县级以上公安机关负责人批准，公安机关可以采取临时的保护性约束措施。必要时，可以将其送精神病医院接受治疗。

**Article 344** For a mental patient who has committed violent acts, before the People's Court decides on compulsory medical treatment, the public security organ may, upon approval by the responsible person of the public security organ at or above the county level, take temporary protective restraint measures. When necessary, such patient may be sent to a psychiatric hospital for treatment.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　采取临时的保护性约束措施时，应当对精神病人严加看管，并注意约束的方式、方法和力度，以避免和防止危害他人和精神病人的自身安全为限度。  
对于精神病人已没有继续危害社会可能，解除约束后不致发生社会危险性的，公安机关应当及时解除保护性约束措施。

**Article 345** When taking temporary protective restraint measures, the mental patient shall be strictly supervised, and attention shall be paid to the manner, method, and intensity of restraint, with the limit of avoiding and preventing harm to others and to the mental patient's own safety.  
Where the mental patient no longer has the possibility of continuing to endanger society, and no social danger would arise after release from restraint, the public security organ shall promptly release the protective restraint measures.

### 第十一章　办案协作

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　公安机关在异地执行传唤、拘传、拘留、逮捕，开展勘验、检查、搜查、查封、扣押、冻结、讯问等侦查活动，应当向当地公安机关提出办案协作请求，并在当地公安机关协助下进行，或者委托当地公安机关代为执行。  
开展查询、询问、辨认等侦查活动或者送达法律文书的，也可以向当地公安机关提出办案协作请求，并按照有关规定进行通报。

**Article 346** When a public security organ conducts investigation activities such as summons, summons by force, criminal detention, arrest, or conducts investigative activities such as inspection, examination, search, sealing, seizure, freezing, or interrogation in another jurisdiction, it shall request assistance from the local public security organ and proceed with the assistance of the local public security organ, or entrust the local public security organ to carry out the activities on its behalf.  
When conducting investigative activities such as inquiries, questioning, or identification, or serving legal documents, a request for assistance may also be made to the local public security organ, and notification shall be made in accordance with relevant provisions.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　需要异地公安机关协助的，办案地公安机关应当制作办案协作函件，连同有关法律文书和人民警察证复印件一并提供给协作地公安机关。必要时，可以将前述法律手续传真或者通过公安机关有关信息系统传输至协作地公安机关。  
请求协助执行传唤、拘传、拘留、逮捕的，应当提供传唤证、拘传证、拘留证、逮捕证；请求协助开展搜查、查封、扣押、查询、冻结等侦查活动的，应当提供搜查证、查封决定书、扣押决定书、协助查询财产通知书、协助冻结财产通知书；请求协助开展勘验、检查、讯问、询问等侦查活动的，应当提供立案决定书。

**Article 347** When assistance from a public security organ in another jurisdiction is needed, the public security organ handling the case shall prepare a letter of assistance, and provide it together with relevant legal documents and copies of the people's police certificate to the assisting public security organ. Where necessary, the aforementioned legal formalities may be transmitted by fax or through the public security organ's relevant information system to the assisting public security organ.  
If requesting assistance in executing summons, summons by force, criminal detention, or arrest, the summons certificate, summons by force certificate, criminal detention certificate, or arrest certificate shall be provided; if requesting assistance in conducting investigative activities such as search, sealing, seizure, inquiry, or freezing, a search warrant, decision on sealing, decision on seizure, notice on assisting in property inquiry, or notice on assisting in property freezing shall be provided; if requesting assistance in conducting investigative activities such as inspection, examination, interrogation, or questioning, a decision to place a case on file shall be provided.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　公安机关应当指定一个部门归口接收协作请求，并进行审核。对符合[本规定第三百四十七条](#art-347)规定的协作请求，应当及时交主管业务部门办理。  
异地公安机关提出协作请求的，只要法律手续完备，协作地公安机关就应当及时无条件予以配合，不得收取任何形式的费用或者设置其他条件。

**Article 348** A public security organ shall designate a department to centrally receive assistance requests and conduct review. If the assistance request complies with the provisions of Article 347 of these Provisions, it shall be promptly referred to the competent business department for handling.  
When a public security organ in another jurisdiction makes an assistance request, as long as the legal formalities are complete, the assisting public security organ shall provide unconditional cooperation in a timely manner, and shall not charge any fees or impose other conditions.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　对协作过程中获取的犯罪线索，不属于自己管辖的，应当及时移交有管辖权的公安机关或者其他有关部门。

**Article 349** Criminal leads obtained during the cooperation process that are not under one's own jurisdiction shall be promptly transferred to the public security organ or other relevant department with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　异地执行传唤、拘传的，协作地公安机关应当协助将犯罪嫌疑人传唤、拘传到本市、县公安机关执法办案场所或者到他的住处进行讯问。  
异地执行拘留、逮捕的，协作地公安机关应当派员协助执行。

**Article 350** For summons or compulsory summons executed in a different locality, the cooperating public security organ shall assist in summoning or compelling the criminal suspect to the law enforcement case-handling site of the public security organ of the city or county or to his residence for interrogation.  
For detention or arrest executed in a different locality, the cooperating public security organ shall dispatch personnel to assist in the execution.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　已被决定拘留、逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人在逃的，可以通过网上工作平台发布犯罪嫌疑人相关信息、拘留证或者逮捕证。各地公安机关发现网上逃犯的，应当立即组织抓捕。  
协作地公安机关抓获犯罪嫌疑人后，应当立即通知办案地公安机关。办案地公安机关应当立即携带法律文书及时提解，提解的侦查人员不得少于二人。  
办案地公安机关不能及时到达协作地的，应当委托协作地公安机关在拘留、逮捕后二十四小时以内进行讯问。

**Article 351** If a criminal suspect who has been decided to be detained or arrested is at large, the relevant information of the criminal suspect, the detention warrant or arrest warrant may be published through an online work platform. Where a public security organ at any locality discovers an online fugitive, it shall immediately organize his capture.  
After the cooperating public security organ captures the criminal suspect, it shall immediately notify the case-handling public security organ. The case-handling public security organ shall immediately carry the legal documents and take over the suspect in a timely manner, and the number of investigators taking over shall not be less than two.  
If the case-handling public security organ cannot arrive at the cooperating locality in a timely manner, it shall entrust the cooperating public security organ to conduct interrogation within 24 hours after the detention or arrest.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　办案地公安机关请求代为讯问、询问、辨认的，协作地公安机关应当制作讯问、询问、辨认笔录，交被讯问、询问人和辨认人签名、捺指印后，提供给办案地公安机关。  
办案地公安机关可以委托协作地公安机关协助进行远程视频讯问、询问，讯问、询问过程应当全程录音录像。

**Article 352** Where the case-handling public security organ requests the cooperating public security organ to conduct interrogation, inquiry, or identification on its behalf, the cooperating public security organ shall make records of the interrogation, inquiry, and identification, and after having the interrogated, inquired persons and the identifier sign and affix their fingerprints, provide them to the case-handling public security organ.  
The case-handling public security organ may entrust the cooperating public security organ to assist in conducting remote video interrogation or inquiry, and the interrogation or inquiry process shall be fully recorded in audio and video.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　办案地公安机关请求协查犯罪嫌疑人的身份、年龄、违法犯罪经历等情况的，协作地公安机关应当在接到请求后七日以内将协查结果通知办案地公安机关；交通十分不便的边远地区，应当在十五日以内将协查结果通知办案地公安机关。  
办案地公安机关请求协助调查取证或者查询犯罪信息、资料的，协作地公安机关应当及时协查并反馈。

**Article 353** Where the case-handling public security organ requests assistance in verifying the identity, age, criminal and illegal record, etc., of a criminal suspect, the cooperating public security organ shall notify the case-handling public security organ of the results of the verification within seven days of receiving the request; in remote areas with extremely inconvenient transportation, the cooperating public security organ shall notify the case-handling public security organ of the results within fifteen days.  
Where the case-handling public security organ requests assistance in investigating and collecting evidence or inquiring about criminal information or materials, the cooperating public security organ shall promptly assist in the investigation and provide feedback.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　对不履行办案协作程序或者协作职责造成严重后果的，对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，应当给予处分；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 354** Where the failure to comply with the case-handling cooperation procedures or cooperation duties causes serious consequences, the directly responsible supervisors and other directly liable persons shall be given sanctions; if a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　协作地公安机关依照办案地公安机关的协作请求履行办案协作职责所产生的法律责任，由办案地公安机关承担。但是，协作行为超出协作请求范围，造成执法过错的，由协作地公安机关承担相应法律责任。

**Article 355** The legal liability arising from the performance of case-handling cooperation duties by the cooperating public security organ in accordance with the cooperation request of the case-handling public security organ shall be borne by the case-handling public security organ. However, if the cooperation act exceeds the scope of the cooperation request and causes a law enforcement fault, the cooperating public security organ shall bear the corresponding legal liability.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　办案地和协作地公安机关对于案件管辖、定性处理等发生争议的，可以进行协商。协商不成的，提请共同的上级公安机关决定。

**Article 356** Where the case-handling public security organ and the cooperating public security organ have a dispute over jurisdiction, nature determination, or disposition of a case, they may negotiate. If negotiation fails, the matter shall be submitted to their common superior public security organ for decision.

### 第十二章　外国人犯罪案件的办理

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　办理外国人犯罪案件，应当严格依照我国法律、法规、规章，维护国家主权和利益，并在对等互惠原则的基础上，履行我国所承担的国际条约义务。

**Article 357** Handling of criminal cases involving foreigners shall strictly follow the laws, regulations, and rules of China, safeguard national sovereignty and interests, and on the basis of the principle of reciprocity, fulfill the international treaty obligations assumed by China.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　外国籍犯罪嫌疑人在刑事诉讼中，享有我国法律规定的诉讼权利，并承担相应的义务。

**Article 358** A criminal suspect of foreign nationality, in criminal proceedings, shall enjoy the litigation rights prescribed by Chinese law and shall bear the corresponding obligations.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　外国籍犯罪嫌疑人的国籍，以其在入境时持用的有效证件予以确认；国籍不明的，由出入境管理部门协助予以查明。国籍确实无法查明的，以无国籍人对待。

**Article 359** The nationality of a criminal suspect of foreign nationality shall be confirmed based on the valid documents he holds at the time of entry; if the nationality is unclear, the exit and entry administration department shall assist in ascertaining it. If the nationality is truly unable to be ascertained, he shall be treated as a stateless person.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　确认外国籍犯罪嫌疑人身份，可以依照有关国际条约或者通过国际刑事警察组织、警务合作渠道办理。确实无法查明的，可以按其自报的姓名移送人民检察院审查起诉。

**Article 360** The identification of a criminal suspect of foreign nationality may be handled in accordance with relevant international treaties or through the International Criminal Police Organization or police cooperation channels. If the identification truly cannot be ascertained, the case may be transferred to the People's Procuratorate for review and prosecution according to the name he self-reports.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　犯罪嫌疑人为享有外交或者领事特权和豁免权的外国人的，应当层报公安部，同时通报同级人民政府外事办公室，由公安部商请外交部通过外交途径办理。

**Article 361** Where a criminal suspect is a foreigner entitled to diplomatic or consular privileges and immunities, the matter shall be reported level by level to the Ministry of Public Security, and simultaneously notified to the foreign affairs office of the people's government at the same level, and the Ministry of Public Security shall consult the Ministry of Foreign Affairs to handle it through diplomatic channels.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　公安机关办理外国人犯罪案件，使用中华人民共和国通用的语言文字。犯罪嫌疑人不通晓我国语言文字的，公安机关应当为他翻译；犯罪嫌疑人通晓我国语言文字，不需要他人翻译的，应当出具书面声明。

**Article 362** When handling criminal cases involving foreigners, the public security organ shall use the commonly used language and writing of the People's Republic of China. If the criminal suspect is not familiar with the Chinese language and writing, the public security organ shall provide translation for him; if the criminal suspect is familiar with the Chinese language and writing and does not require translation, he shall issue a written statement.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　外国人犯罪案件，由犯罪地的县级以上公安机关立案侦查。

**Article 363** Criminal cases involving foreigners shall be placed on file and investigated by the public security organ at or above the county level of the place where the crime was committed.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　外国人犯中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约规定的罪行后进入我国领域内的，由该外国人被抓获地的设区的市一级以上公安机关立案侦查。

**Article 364** Where a foreigner commits a crime stipulated in an international treaty concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China and then enters Chinese territory, the case shall be placed on file and investigated by the public security organ at or above the city-with-districts level of the place where the foreigner was captured.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　外国人在中华人民共和国领域外对中华人民共和国国家或者公民犯罪，应当受刑罚处罚的，由该外国人入境地或者入境后居住地的县级以上公安机关立案侦查；该外国人未入境的，由被害人居住地的县级以上公安机关立案侦查；没有被害人或者是对中华人民共和国国家犯罪的，由公安部指定管辖。

**Article 365** Where a foreigner commits a crime outside the territory of the People's Republic of China against the State or a citizen of the People's Republic of China and shall be subject to criminal punishment, the case shall be placed on file and investigated by the public security organ at or above the county level of the place where the foreigner enters China or lives after entry; if the foreigner has not entered China, the case shall be placed on file and investigated by the public security organ at or above the county level of the place of residence of the victim; if there is no victim or the crime is against the State of the People's Republic of China, the Ministry of Public Security shall designate the jurisdiction.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　发生重大或者可能引起外交交涉的外国人犯罪案件的，有关省级公安机关应当及时将案件办理情况报告公安部，同时通报同级人民政府外事办公室。必要时，由公安部商外交部将案件情况通知我国驻外使馆、领事馆。

**Article 366** Where a major criminal case involving a foreigner occurs or a case that may cause diplomatic representations, the relevant provincial public security organ shall promptly report the case handling status to the Ministry of Public Security, and simultaneously notify the foreign affairs office of the people's government at the same level. When necessary, the Ministry of Public Security shall consult the Ministry of Foreign Affairs to notify the Chinese embassies and consulates abroad of the case status.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　对外国籍犯罪嫌疑人依法作出取保候审、监视居住决定或者执行拘留、逮捕后，应当在四十八小时以内层报省级公安机关，同时通报同级人民政府外事办公室。  
重大涉外案件应当在四十八小时以内层报公安部，同时通报同级人民政府外事办公室。

**Article 367** After lawfully making a decision to release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance or executing detention or arrest on a criminal suspect of foreign nationality, the matter shall be reported level by level to the provincial public security organ within 48 hours, and simultaneously notified to the foreign affairs office of the people's government at the same level.  
Major foreign-related cases shall be reported level by level to the Ministry of Public Security within 48 hours, and simultaneously notified to the foreign affairs office of the people's government at the same level.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　对外国籍犯罪嫌疑人依法作出取保候审、监视居住决定或者执行拘留、逮捕后，由省级公安机关根据有关规定，将其姓名、性别、入境时间、护照或者证件号码、案件发生的时间、地点，涉嫌犯罪的主要事实，已采取的强制措施及其法律依据等，通知该外国人所属国家的驻华使馆、领事馆，同时报告公安部。经省级公安机关批准，领事通报任务较重的副省级城市公安局可以直接行使领事通报职能。  
外国人在公安机关侦查或者执行刑罚期间死亡的，有关省级公安机关应当通知该外国人国籍国的驻华使馆、领事馆，同时报告公安部。  
未在华设立使馆、领事馆的国家，可以通知其代管国家的驻华使馆、领事馆；无代管国家或者代管国家不明的，可以不予通知。

**Article 368** After lawfully making a decision to release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance or executing detention or arrest on a criminal suspect of foreign nationality, the provincial public security organ shall, according to relevant provisions, notify the embassy or consulate of the foreigner's country in China of his name, gender, time of entry, passport or document number, time and place of the incident, main facts of suspected crimes, compulsory measures taken and their legal basis, etc., and simultaneously report to the Ministry of Public Security. With approval of the provincial public security organ, a sub-provincial city public security bureau with a heavy consular notification task may directly exercise the consular notification function.  
If a foreigner dies during the period of investigation by the public security organ or execution of punishment, the relevant provincial public security organ shall notify the embassy or consulate of the foreigner's country of nationality in China, and simultaneously report to the Ministry of Public Security.  
For a country without an embassy or consulate in China, notification may be given to the embassy or consulate of the country acting on its behalf; if there is no acting country or the acting country is unclear, notification may be withheld.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　外国籍犯罪嫌疑人委托辩护人的，应当委托在中华人民共和国的律师事务所执业的律师。

**Article 369** Where a criminal suspect of foreign nationality retains a defender, he or she shall retain a lawyer who is practicing in a law firm within the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　公安机关侦查终结前，外国驻华外交、领事官员要求探视被监视居住、拘留、逮捕或者正在看守所服刑的本国公民的，应当及时安排有关探视事宜。犯罪嫌疑人拒绝其国籍国驻华外交、领事官员探视的，公安机关可以不予安排，但应当由其本人提出书面声明。  
在公安机关侦查羁押期间，经公安机关批准，外国籍犯罪嫌疑人可以与其近亲属、监护人会见、与外界通信。

**Article 370** Before the public security organ concludes the investigation, if foreign diplomatic or consular officials in China request to visit a national of their country who is under residential surveillance, detained, arrested, or serving a sentence in a detention house, the public security organ shall promptly arrange the relevant visit matters. Where the criminal suspect refuses to be visited by the diplomatic or consular officials of his or her country of nationality in China, the public security organ may not arrange such a visit, but the suspect shall personally submit a written statement.  
During the period of custody for investigation by the public security organ, with the approval of the public security organ, a criminal suspect of foreign nationality may meet with his or her close relatives or guardians, and communicate with the outside world.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　对判处独立适用驱逐出境刑罚的外国人，省级公安机关在收到人民法院的刑事判决书、执行通知书的副本后，应当指定该外国人所在地的设区的市一级公安机关执行。  
被判处徒刑的外国人，主刑执行期满后应当执行驱逐出境附加刑的，省级公安机关在收到执行监狱的上级主管部门转交的刑事判决书、执行通知书副本或者复印件后，应当通知该外国人所在地的设区的市一级公安机关或者指定有关公安机关执行。  
我国政府已按照国际条约或者《中华人民共和国外交特权与豁免条例》的规定，对实施犯罪，但享有外交或者领事特权和豁免权的外国人宣布为不受欢迎的人，或者不可接受并拒绝承认其外交或者领事人员身份，责令限期出境的人，无正当理由逾期不自动出境的，由公安部凭外交部公文指定该外国人所在地的省级公安机关负责执行或者监督执行。

**Article 371** With respect to a foreigner who is sentenced to the penalty of deportation imposed independently, the provincial public security organ shall, after receiving the copies of the criminal judgment and enforcement notice from the People's Court, designate the public security organ at the city divided into districts level in the locality where the foreigner is located to enforce the penalty.  
Where a foreigner who is sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment is also required to have the supplementary punishment of deportation enforced after the completion of the principal punishment, the provincial public security organ shall, after receiving the copies or photocopies of the criminal judgment and enforcement notice forwarded by the competent authority at the higher level of the prison where the sentence is served, notify the public security organ at the city divided into districts level in the locality where the foreigner is located or designate the relevant public security organ to enforce the punishment.  
Where the Chinese Government has, in accordance with the provisions of an international treaty or the Regulations of the People's Republic of China on Diplomatic Privileges and Immunities, declared as persona non grata or unacceptable and refused to recognize the diplomatic or consular status of a foreigner who has committed a crime but enjoys diplomatic or consular privileges and immunities, and has ordered the foreigner to leave the country within a specified time limit, and the foreigner fails to leave the country voluntarily within the specified time limit without justifiable reasons, the Ministry of Public Security shall, on the basis of a document from the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, designate the provincial public security organ in the locality where the foreigner is located to be responsible for the enforcement or supervision of enforcement.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　办理外国人犯罪案件，本章未规定的，适用本规定其他各章的有关规定。

**Article 372** For matters not provided for in this Chapter in handling criminal cases involving foreigners, the relevant provisions in other Chapters of these Provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　办理无国籍人犯罪案件，适用本章的规定。

**Article 373** The provisions of this Chapter shall apply to the handling of criminal cases involving stateless persons.

### 第十三章　刑事司法协助和警务合作

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　公安部是公安机关进行刑事司法协助和警务合作的中央主管机关，通过有关法律、国际条约、协议规定的联系途径、外交途径或者国际刑事警察组织渠道，接收或者向外国提出刑事司法协助或者警务合作请求。  
地方各级公安机关依照职责权限办理刑事司法协助事务和警务合作事务。  
其他司法机关在办理刑事案件中，需要外国警方协助的，由其中央主管机关与公安部联系办理。

**Article 374** The Ministry of Public Security shall be the central competent authority for public security organs in undertaking criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation. It shall receive or submit requests for criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation to or from foreign countries through the contact channels provided for by relevant laws, international treaties, or agreements, through diplomatic channels, or through the channels of the International Criminal Police Organization.  
Local public security organs at all levels shall handle matters of criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation within their functions, powers, and jurisdiction.  
Where other judicial organs need assistance from foreign police in handling criminal cases, the central competent authority of such organs shall contact the Ministry of Public Security to handle the matter.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　公安机关进行刑事司法协助和警务合作的范围，主要包括犯罪情报信息的交流与合作，调查取证，安排证人作证或者协助调查，查封、扣押、冻结涉案财物，没收、返还违法所得及其他涉案财物，送达刑事诉讼文书，引渡、缉捕和递解犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者罪犯，以及国际条约、协议规定的其他刑事司法协助和警务合作事宜。

**Article 375** The scope of criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation undertaken by public security organs mainly includes: exchange and cooperation in criminal intelligence information; investigation and collection of evidence; arranging for witnesses to testify or assist in investigations; sealing, seizing, and freezing of property involved in a case; confiscation and return of illegal gains and other property involved in a case; service of criminal procedure documents; extradition, arrest, and transfer of criminal suspects, defendants, or offenders; and other matters of criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation as provided for by international treaties or agreements.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　在不违背我国法律和有关国际条约、协议的前提下，我国边境地区设区的市一级公安机关和县级公安机关与相邻国家的警察机关，可以按照惯例相互开展执法会晤、人员往来、边境管控、情报信息交流等警务合作，但应当报省级公安机关批准，并报公安部备案；开展其他警务合作的，应当报公安部批准。

**Article 376** Provided that it does not contravene Chinese laws and relevant international treaties or agreements, public security organs at the city divided into districts level and at the county level in China's border areas may, in accordance with customary practice, conduct police cooperation with police authorities of neighboring countries in areas such as law enforcement meetings, personnel exchanges, border control, and exchange of intelligence information. However, such cooperation shall be subject to approval by the provincial public security organ and shall be reported to the Ministry of Public Security for record. For other types of police cooperation, approval shall be obtained from the Ministry of Public Security.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　公安部收到外国的刑事司法协助或者警务合作请求后，应当依据我国法律和国际条约、协议的规定进行审查。对于符合规定的，交有关省级公安机关办理，或者移交其他有关中央主管机关；对于不符合条约或者协议规定的，通过接收请求的途径退回请求方。  
对于请求书的签署机关、请求书及所附材料的语言文字、有关办理期限和具体程序等事项，在不违反我国法律基本原则的情况下，可以按照刑事司法协助条约、警务合作协议规定或者双方协商办理。

**Article 377** After receiving a request from a foreign country for criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation, the Ministry of Public Security shall review it in accordance with Chinese laws and the provisions of international treaties or agreements. If the request complies with the relevant provisions, it shall be referred to the relevant provincial public security organ for handling, or to another relevant central competent authority; if it does not comply with the provisions of the treaty or agreement, it shall be returned to the requesting party through the channel through which the request was received.  
With regard to matters such as the authority signing the request, the language of the request and attached materials, the relevant time limits for handling, and specific procedures, these may, provided they do not violate the fundamental principles of Chinese laws, be handled in accordance with the provisions of the criminal judicial assistance treaty or police cooperation agreement, or through consultation between the parties.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　负责执行刑事司法协助或者警务合作的公安机关收到请求书和所附材料后，应当按照我国法律和有关国际条约、协议的规定安排执行，并将执行结果及其有关材料报经省级公安机关审核后报送公安部。  
在执行过程中，需要采取查询、查封、扣押、冻结等措施或者返还涉案财物，且符合法律规定的条件的，可以根据我国有关法律和公安部的执行通知办理有关法律手续。  
请求书提供的信息不准确或者材料不齐全难以执行的，应当立即通过省级公安机关报请公安部要求请求方补充材料；因其他原因无法执行或者具有应当拒绝协助、合作的情形等不能执行的，应当将请求书和所附材料，连同不能执行的理由通过省级公安机关报送公安部。

**Article 378** After receiving the request and attached materials, the public security organ responsible for executing the criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation shall arrange for execution in accordance with Chinese laws and the provisions of relevant international treaties or agreements, and shall submit the execution results and relevant materials to the provincial public security organ for review, which shall then be reported to the Ministry of Public Security.  
Where, during the course of execution, it is necessary to take measures such as inquiry, sealing, seizure, or freezing, or to return property involved in a case, and the conditions provided for by law are met, the relevant legal formalities may be handled in accordance with relevant Chinese laws and the execution notice of the Ministry of Public Security.  
If the information provided in the request is inaccurate or the materials are incomplete, making execution difficult, the public security organ shall immediately, through the provincial public security organ, request the Ministry of Public Security to require the requesting party to supplement the materials. If execution is impossible for other reasons or if there are circumstances where assistance or cooperation should be refused, making execution impossible, the request and attached materials shall be submitted through the provincial public security organ to the Ministry of Public Security along with the reasons for the inability to execute.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　执行刑事司法协助和警务合作，请求书中附有办理期限的，应当按期完成。未附办理期限的，调查取证应当在三个月以内完成；送达刑事诉讼文书，应当在十日以内完成。不能按期完成的，应当说明情况和理由，层报公安部。

**Article 379** When executing criminal judicial assistance and police cooperation, if a time limit for handling is attached to the request, it shall be completed within the prescribed time limit. If no time limit is attached, investigation and collection of evidence shall be completed within three months; service of criminal procedure documents shall be completed within ten days. If the time limit cannot be met, the reasons and circumstances shall be explained and reported level by level to the Ministry of Public Security.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　需要请求外国警方提供刑事司法协助或者警务合作的，应当按照我国有关法律、国际条约、协议的规定提出刑事司法协助或者警务合作请求书，所附文件及相应译文，经省级公安机关审核后报送公安部。

**Article 380** Where it is necessary to request foreign police to provide criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation, a request for criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation shall be made in accordance with the provisions of relevant Chinese laws, international treaties, or agreements, together with the attached documents and corresponding translations, and submitted to the Ministry of Public Security after review by the provincial public security organ.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　需要通过国际刑事警察组织查找或者缉捕犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者罪犯，查询资料、调查取证的，应当提出申请层报国际刑事警察组织中国国家中心局。

**Article 381** Where it is necessary to locate or arrest a criminal suspect, defendant, or offender through the International Criminal Police Organization, or to query information or investigate and collect evidence, an application shall be submitted level by level to the National Central Bureau of the International Criminal Police Organization in China.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　公安机关需要外国协助安排证人、鉴定人来中华人民共和国作证或者通过视频、音频作证，或者协助调查的，应当制作刑事司法协助请求书并附相关材料，经公安部审核同意后，由对外联系机关及时向外国提出请求。  
来中华人民共和国作证或者协助调查的证人、鉴定人离境前，公安机关不得就其入境前实施的犯罪进行追究；除因入境后实施违法犯罪而被采取强制措施的以外，其人身自由不受限制。  
证人、鉴定人在条约规定的期限内或者被通知无需继续停留后十五日内没有离境的，前款规定不再适用，但是由于不可抗力或者其他特殊原因未能离境的除外。

**Article 382** Where a public security organ needs a foreign country to assist in arranging for a witness or expert evaluator to come to the People's Republic of China to testify, or to testify via video or audio, or to assist in an investigation, it shall prepare a request for criminal judicial assistance with relevant materials attached, and after the request is examined and approved by the Ministry of Public Security, the authority handling foreign affairs shall promptly submit the request to the foreign country.  
Before a witness or expert evaluator who comes to the People's Republic of China to testify or assist in an investigation departs from China, the public security organ shall not investigate any crime committed before his or her entry into China; his or her personal freedom shall not be restricted, except where compulsory measures are taken due to illegal or criminal acts committed after entry.  
If a witness or expert evaluator fails to depart from China within the time limit specified in the treaty or within fifteen days after being notified that his or her continued stay is not required, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall no longer apply, unless his or her failure to depart is due to force majeure or other special reasons.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　公安机关提供或者请求外国提供刑事司法协助或者警务合作，应当收取或者支付费用的，根据有关国际条约、协议的规定，或者按照对等互惠的原则协商办理。

**Article 383** Where fees are to be collected or paid by public security organs for providing or requesting criminal judicial assistance or police cooperation from a foreign country, such fees shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of relevant international treaties or agreements, or through consultation on the principle of reciprocity.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　办理引渡案件，依照《中华人民共和国引渡法》等法律规定和有关条约执行。

**Article 384** Extradition cases shall be handled in accordance with the Extradition Law of the People's Republic of China and other laws, as well as relevant treaties.

### 第十四章　附 则

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　本规定所称“危害国家安全犯罪”，包括刑法分则第一章规定的危害国家安全罪以及危害国家安全的其他犯罪；“恐怖活动犯罪”，包括以制造社会恐慌、危害公共安全或者胁迫国家机关、国际组织为目的，采取暴力、破坏、恐吓等手段，造成或者意图造成人员伤亡、重大财产损失、公共设施损坏、社会秩序混乱等严重社会危害的犯罪，以及煽动、资助或者以其他方式协助实施上述活动的犯罪。

**Article 385** For the purposes of these Provisions, "crimes of endangering national security" include the crimes of endangering national security as provided for in Chapter One of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, as well as other crimes that endanger national security; "terrorist activities crimes" include crimes committed for the purpose of creating social panic, endangering public security, or coercing state organs or international organizations, by means of violence, sabotage, intimidation, etc., causing or intending to cause casualties, major property losses, damage to public facilities, or social disorder, and other serious social harms, as well as crimes of inciting, funding, or otherwise assisting in the commission of the above-mentioned activities.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　当事人及其法定代理人、诉讼代理人、辩护律师提出的复议复核请求，由公安机关法制部门办理。  
办理刑事复议、复核案件的具体程序，适用《公安机关办理刑事复议复核案件程序规定》。

**Article 386** Requests for reconsideration or review submitted by a party or his or her legal representative, litigation agent, or defense lawyer shall be handled by the legal affairs department of the public security organ.  
The specific procedures for handling criminal reconsideration and review cases shall be governed by the Provisions on Procedures for Public Security Organs in Handling Criminal Reconsideration and Review Cases.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　公安机关可以使用电子签名、电子指纹捺印技术制作电子笔录等材料，可以使用电子印章制作法律文书。对案件当事人进行电子签名、电子指纹捺印的过程，公安机关应当同步录音录像。

**Article 387** A public security organ may use electronic signature and electronic fingerprinting technologies to produce electronic records and other materials, and may use electronic seals to produce legal documents. The process of taking electronic signatures and electronic fingerprints from a party to a case shall be simultaneously recorded by audio and video by the public security organ.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　本规定自2013年1月1日起施行。1998年5月14日发布的《公安机关办理刑事案件程序规定》（公安部令第35号）和2007年10月25日发布的《公安机关办理刑事案件程序规定修正案》（公安部令第95号）同时废止。

**Article 388** These Provisions shall come into force as of January 1, 2013. The Provisions on Procedures for Public Security Organs in Handling Criminal Cases (Order No. 35 of the Ministry of Public Security) promulgated on May 14, 1998, and the Amendment to the Provisions on Procedures for Public Security Organs in Handling Criminal Cases (Order No. 95 of the Ministry of Public Security) promulgated on October 25, 2007, shall be abolished simultaneously.
